# Apocalypse of Baruch (Liber Apocalypseos Baruch filii Neriae)

**Author**: Anonymous  
**Series**: PO Tome PS  
**Columns**: pp. 1068–1206  

[p. 1068] {#po.0.p1068}

### THE APOCALYPSE OF BARUCH THE SON OF NERIAH
ܓ݂ܰܠܝܳܢܶܗ ܕܒܳܪܘܽܟ ܒܰܪ ܢܶܪܝܳܐ ܘܰܗܘܳܐ ܒܫܰܢܬ݂ܳܐ ܥܶܣܪܺܝܢ ܘܰܚܡܶܫ ܕܝܰܟܳܢܝܳܐ
1. And it came to pass in the twenty-
ܡܰܠܟܳܐ ܕܝܺܗܘܽܕܳܐ: ܗܘܳܐ ܦܶܬܓܶܡܗ ܕܡܳܪܝܳܐ ܥܰܠ ܒܳܪܘܽܟ ܒܰܪ ܢܺܝܪܺܝܰܝܐ ܘܐܶܡܰܪ ܠܶܗ: ܚܙܰܝܬ ܟ݁ܽܠ ܡܶܕܶܡ ܕܥܳܒܶܕ݂ ܠܝܿ ܥܰܡܐ ܗܳܢܳܐ: ܒܺܝ̈ܫܳܬ݁ܳܐ ܕܰܥܒܰܕ݂ܘ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܬ݁ܪܶܝܢ ܫܰܒ̈ܛܺܝܢ ܕܦܺܝܫܘ ܝܰܬܺܝܪ ܡܶܢ ܥܶܣܪܳܐ ܫܰܒ̈ܛܺܝܢ ܕܐܶܫܬܒܝܘ܆ ܫܰܒ̈ܛܶܐ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܩܰܕ݂ܡܳܝܶܐ ܡܶܢ ܡܰܠܟܰܝ̈ܗܘܽܢ ܐܶܬܰܐܠܶܨܘ ܕ݁ܢܶܚܛܘܽܢ: ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܕܶܝܢ ܬܪܶܝܝܢ ܗܶܢܘܽܢ ܠܳܨܝܺܢ ܗ̱ܘܰܘ ܥܳܨܶܝܢ ܠܡܰܠܟܰܝ̈ܗܘܽܢ ܕܢܶܚܛܘܿܢ. ܡܶܛܽܠ ܗܳܢܐ ܗܳܐ ܡܰܝܬܶܐ ܐ̱ܢܳܐ ܒܺܝܫܬ݁ܳܐ ܥܰܠ ܡܕܺܝܢ̱ܬ݁ܳܐ ܗܳܕ݂ܶܐ ܘܰܠ ܥܳܡܘܽܪܶܝܗ: ܘܐܶܬܪܚܺܩ ܡܶܢ ܩܕ݂ܳܡܰܝ ܥܰܕ݂ ܙܰܒ݁ܢܳܐ: ܘܰܡܒܰܕ݂ܰܪ ܐ̱ܢܳܐ ܠܶܗ ܠܗܳܢܐ ܥܰܡܳܐ
fifth year of Jeconiah, king of Judah, the word of the Lord came upon Baruch the son of Neriah and said to him: Have you seen all the evils which this people does to me, which the two tribes that remained have done, more than the ten tribes which were led away captive? For the former tribes were driven by their kings to sin, but these two themselves drove and compelled their kings to sin. Because of this, behold, I bring evil upon this city and upon its inhabitants, and it will be taken away from my sight for a time, and I will scatter this people among the nations, so that it may preach to the nations. And my people will be chastised, and the time will come, and they will seek the prosperity of their times.
ܒܰܝܢܳܬ ܥܰܡ̈ܡܶܐ ܕܰܠܥܰܡ̈ܡܶܐ ܢܰܟܪܶܙ. ܘܥܰܡܝ ܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܢܶܬܪܕ݁ܶܐ ܘܢܺܐܬ݂ܶܐ ܙܰܒ݂ܢܳܐ ܘܢܶܒܥܘܽܢ ܡܰܡܨܠܚܳܢܘܽܬܗܘܽܢ ܕܙܰܒܢܰܝ̈ܗܘܽܢ܀ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܐܶܡܪܶܬ ܠܳܟ ܕܬ݂ܺܐܡܰܪ ܠܶܐܪܰܡܝܳܐ ܘܰܠܟ݁ܽܠ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕ݁ܕ݂ܳܡܶܝܢ ܠܟܘܢ ܕܬܶܬܪܰܚܩܘܽܢ ܠܟܘܢ ܡܶܢ ܗܳܕ݁ܶܐ ܡܕܺܝܢ̱ܬܳܐ. ܡܶܛܽܠ ܕܰܥܒܳܕܰܝ̈ܟܘܽܢ ܐܝܺܬܰܝܗܘܽܢ ܠܗܳܕܶܐ ܡܕܺܝܢ̱ܬܳܐ ܐܰܝܟ ܥܰܡܘܽܕ݂ܳܐ ܫܰܪܺܝܪܳܐ: ܘܰܨܠܰܘܳܬܟܘܽܢ ܐܰܝܟ
II. But I have said these things to you, so that you may tell Jeremiah and all who are like you, that you should depart from this city. Because your works are to this city like a firm pillar and your prayers like a strong wall.


[p. 1069] {#po.0.p1069}
ܫܘܽܪܳܐ ܚܰܝܠܬܳܢܳܐ܀ ܘܐܶܡܪܶܬ: ܐܿܘ̄ ܡܳܪܝܳܐ ܡܳܪܝ: ܠܗܳܕ݁ܶܐ ܐܬܝܺܬ ܠܥܳܠܡܳܐ ܕܐܶܚܙܶܐ ܒܒܝܳܫ̈ܳܬܳܗ ܕܐܶܡܝ܆ ܠܳܐ ܡܳܪܝ: ܐܶܢ ܐܶܫܟܚܺܝܬ ܪ̈ܰܚܡܶܐ ܒܥܰܝ̈ܢܰܝܟ: ܣܰܒ ܠܘܽܩܕ݂ܰܡ ܪܘܽܚܝ ܕܐܺܙܰܠ ܠܝܿ ܠܘܳܬ ܐܰܒܳܗܰܝ̈ ܘܠܳܐ ܐܶܚܙܶܐ ܒܐܰܒ݂ܕ݂ܳܢܳܗ ܕܐܶܡܝ. ܡܶܛܽܠ ܕ݁ܬܰܪ̈ܬܶܝܢ ܐܶܠܰܨܢ ܠܝ ܣܰܓ݁ܝ̈: ܕܐܶܩܘܽܡ ܓܶܝܪ ܠܩܘܽܒܠܳܟ݂ ܠܳܐ ܡܶܫܟ݁ܰܚ ܐ̱ܢܳܐ: ܐܶܠܳܐ ܐܳܦ݂ ܠܳܐ ܕܐܶܚܙܶܐ ܒܒܺܝܫܬ݁ܳܗ ܕܐܶܡܝ ܡܶܫܟ݁ܚܳܐ ܢܰܦܫܝ. ܚܕ݂ܳܐ ܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܐܶܡܰܪ ܐ̱ܢܳܐ ܩܕܳܡܝܟ ܡܳܪܝܳܐ: ܡܳܢܳܐ ܗܳܟܝܶܠ ܗܳܘܶܐ ܡܶܢ ܒܳܬܰܪ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ. ܐܶܢ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܡܰܘܒܶܕ݂ ܐܰܢ̱ܬ ܡܕܺܝܢ̱ܬܳܟ݂: ܘܡܰܫܠܶܡ ܐܰܢ̱ܬ ܐܰܪܥܳܟ ܠܣܳܢ̈ܐܰܝܢ: ܐܰܝܟܰܢܳܐ ܬܘܒ ܡܶܬܕ݁ܰܟ݁ܰܪ ܡܳܐ ܕܐܺܝܣܪܳܝܶܠ ܘܰܐܝܟܰܢܳܐ ܢܡܰܠܠܽܘܢ ܥܰܠ ܬܶܫܒ̈ܚܳܬܳܟ݂ܢ ܐܰܘ ܠܡܰܢܘܽ ܡܶܬܦܰܫܰܩ ܡܳܐ ܕܰܒܢܳܡܘܽܣܳܟ܆ ܐܰܘ ܕ݁ܰܠܡܳܐ ܗܳܦܳܟ݂ ܠܳܗܬܰܨܒܝܳܬ݁ܳܐ ܠܰܟܝܳܢܶܗ܆ ܘܥܳܠܡܳܐ ܬܘّܒ ܐܿܙܰܠ ܠܗ ܠܥܶܬܩܳܐ ܕ݁ܡܶܢ ܩܕܳܡ ܘܡܶܬܢܣܶܒ ܠܶܗ ܣܘܽܓܐܗܶܝܢ ܕܢܰܦ̈ܫܳܬܳܐܢ ܘܰܟܝܳܢܳܐ ܕܰܒܢܰܝ̈ܢܳܫܳܐ ܬܘܽܒ ܠܳܐ ܡܶܫܬܰܡܰܥܗ܆ ܘܐܰܝܟܰܢܳܐ ܗ̱ܘ ܟ݁ܽܠ ܡܳܐ ܕܐܶܡܰܪܬ ܠܡܘܽܫܶܐ ܥܠܰܝܢ~
III. And I said: O Lord, ruler, did I come into the world for this, that I might see the evils of my mother? By no means, Lord. If I have found grace in your eyes, receive my spirit first, so that I may go to my fathers and not watch the destruction of my mother. Because two things press upon me vehemently: for I cannot resist you, but neither can my soul watch the evil of my mother. But I will speak one thing in your sight, Lord. What, therefore, will be after these things? For if you destroy your city and deliver the land to those who hate us, how will the name of Israel be remembered again? Or how will one speak of your praises? Or to whom will that which is in your law be explained? Will the world return to its nature and the age return to the pristine silence? And will the multitude of souls be taken away and the nature of man be named no more? And where is that which you said to Moses concerning us?


[p. 1070] {#po.0.p1070}


[p. 1071] {#po.0.p1071}
ܘܐܡܪ ܠܝ ܡܳܪܝܳܐ: ܗܳܕ݁ܶܐ ܡܕ݂ܝܺܢ̱ܬܳܐ ܠܙܰܒܢܳܐ ܡܶܫܬܰܠܡܳܝܐ: ܘܥܰܡܳܝܐ ܡܶܬܪܕܶܐ ܒܙܰܒܢܳܐ: ܘܥܳܠܡܳܐ ܠܳܐ ܡܶܬܛܥܶܐ. ܐܰܘ ܕ݁ܰܠܡܳܐ ܣܳܒܰܪ ܐܰܢ̱ܬ ܕܗܳܕܳܐ ܗ̱ܝ ܡܕ݂ܝܺܢ̱ܬ݁ܳܐ ܗܳܝ ܕܐܶܡܪܶܬ ܥܠܶܝܗ ܕܥܰܠ ܦܶܣܶܐ ܕܐܝܺܕܰܝ̈ ܪܫܰܡܬܶܟ݂ܝ&܆ ܠܳܐ ܗܘܳܐ ܗܳܢܳܐ ܒܶܢܝܳܢܳܐ ܕܗܳܫܳܐ ܒܢܶܐ ܒܰܝܢܳܬܟܘܽܢ ܗܰܘ ܕܡܶܬܓܠܶܐ ܠܘܳܬܝ: ܗܰܘ ܕܗܳܪܟܳܐ ܡܩܰܕ݂ܰܡ܂ ܡܥܰܬ݁ܰܕ݂: ܡܢ ܟܰܕ݂ ܐܶܬܚܰܫܒܶܬ ܕܐܶܥܒܶܕ݂ ܦ݁ܰܪܕ݂ܰܝܣܳܐ. ܘܚܰܘܝܺܬ݁ܶܗ ܠܳܐܕ݂ܳܡ ܡܶܢ ܩܕ݂ܳܡ ܕܠܳܐ ܢܶܚܛܶܐ: ܘܟܰܕ݂ ܐܰܥܒܰܪ ܦܘܩܕ݁ܳܢܳܐ ܐܶܬܓܰܠܙܰܬ݂ ܠܳܗ ܡܶܢܶܗ ܐܰܝܟ ܕܐܳܦ ܦܰܪܕܰܝܣܳܐ. ܘܡܶܢ ܒܳܬܰܪ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܚܰܘܝܺܬ݁ܶܗ ܠܥܰܒܕܝ ܐܰܒܪܳܗܳܡ܂ ܒܠܺܠܝܳܐ ܒܶܝܬ ܦܶܠܓ݁ܶܐ ܕܕܶܒ̈ܚܶܐ. ܘܬܘܒ ܐܳܦ ܠܡܘܽܫܶܐ ܚܰܘܝܺܬ݁ܶܗ ܒܛܘܽܪ ܣܺܝܢܰܝ ܟܰܕ݂ ܚܰܘܝܺܬܶܗ ܕܡܘܽܬܶܗ ܕܡܰܫܟܢܳܐ ܘܟܘܽܠܗܘܢ ܡܳܐܢܰܘ̈ܗ̱ܝ.
IV. And the Lord said to me: This city will be delivered for a time, and the people will be chastised for a while, and the age will not be given to oblivion. Do you think that this is the city of which I said: I have inscribed you upon the palms of my hands? It is not this building now built in your midst, but that which will be revealed with me, which was prepared here from the time I thought to make paradise. And I showed it to Adam before he sinned; but when he cast away the commandment, it was taken away from him, just as Paradise was. And afterwards I showed it to my servant Abraham by night between the divisions of the victims. And again I also showed it to Moses on Mount Sinai, when I showed him the image of the tabernacle and all its vessels. And now, behold, it is kept with me, just as Paradise is. Go, therefore, and do as I command you.
ܘܗܳܫܳܐ ܗܳܐ ܢܛܝܺܪܳܐ ܠܘܳܬܝ ܐܰܝܟ ܕܐܳܦ ܦܰܪܕܰܝܣܳܐ. ܙܶܠ ܗܳܟ݂ܶܝܠ ܘܰܥܒܶܕ݂ ܐܰܝܟ ܕ݁ܰܡܦ݁ܰܩܶܕ݂ ܐ̱ܢܳܐ ܠܳܟ݂܀ ܘܰܥܢܺܝܬ ܘܐܶܡܪܶܬ: ܥܬܝܰܕ݂ ܐ݈ܢܳܐ ܗܳܟ݂ܶܝܠ ܕܐܶܗܘܶܐ ܚܰܝܒ ܒܨܲܗܝܘܽܢ ܕܢܺܐܬܘܽܢ ܣܳܢܰܐܝܟ ܠܗܳܢܳܐ ܐܰܬܪܳܐ ܘܰܢܛܰܡܐܘܽܢ ܩܘܽܕ݂ܫܳܟ݂ ܘܢܶܫܒܘܽܢ ܚܳܪܬܘܽܬܳܟ݂ ܘܢܶܫܬܰܠܛܘܽܢ ܥܰܠ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܰܪܚܶܡܬ: ܘܢܺܐܙܠ̱ܘܽܢ ܬܘܽܒ ܠܐܰܬ݂ܪܳܐ ܕܰܦܬܰܟ݂ܪܰܝܗܘܽܢ ܘܢܶܫܬܰܒܗܪܘܽܢ ܩܕܳܡܰܝܗܘܽܢ. ܘܡܳܢܳܐ ܥܳܒܕ݂ܰܐ ܠܶܗ ܠܰܫܡܳܟ݂ ܪܰܒܳܐ& ܘܐܶܡܰܪ ܠܝ ܡܳܪܝܳܐ:
V. And I answered and said: Shall I then be guilty in Zion, because those who hate you will come to this place and will defile your sanctuary and will lead your inheritance into captivity and will rule over those whom you have loved, and they will go again into the region of their idols and will boast before them? And what will you do for your great name? And the Lord said to me: For my name and for my glory, the age is eternal: but my judge keeps his own justice in his own time. And you will see with your own eyes, not the enemies overthrowing Zion, nor


[p. 1072] {#po.0.p1072}


[p. 1073] {#po.0.p1073}
ܠܰܫܡܝ ܘܰܠܬܶܫܒܘܽܚܬܝ ܥܳܠܡܳܐ ܥܰܬܝܺܩ ܗ̱ܘ: ܕܰܝܢܳܐ ܕܶܝܢ ܒܙܰܒ݂ܢܶܗ ܢܳܛܰܪ ܙܶܕ̣ܩܶܗ. ܘܐܰܢ̱ܬ ܬ݁ܶܚܙܶܐ ܒܥܰܝܢܰܝܟ ܕܠܳܐ ܗܘܳܐ ܒܥܶܠܕ݁̈ܒܳܒܶܐ ܣܳܚܩܝܺܢ ܠܨܲܗܝܘܿܢ ܘܠܳܐ ܡܰܘܩܕܝܺܢ ܠܐܘܽܪܰܫܠܶܝܡ: ܐܶܠܳܐ ܠܕܰܝܢܳܐ ܡܫܰܡܫܝܺܢ ܠܙܰܒܢܳܐ. ܐܰܢ̱ܬ ܕܝܢ ܙܶܠ ܘܐܶܙܶܠ̱ܬ ܘܕܰܒܪܶܬ ܠܶܐܪܰܡܝܳܐ ܘܠܥܰܕܘܽ ܘܠܰܣܪܳܝܳܐ
burning Jerusalem, but serving judgment for a time. But you, go and do whatever I have told you. And I went and took Jeremiah and Addo and Saraiah and Iabi and Godoliah and all the honored ones of the people and led them into the valley of Cedron and told them whatever had been said to me. And they lifted up their voices and all wept. And we sat there and fasted until evening.


[p. 1074] {#po.0.p1074}
ܕܥܰܡܳܐ: ܘܐܰܚܬܝܺܬ ܐܶܢܘܽܢ ܠܢܰܚܠܳܐ ܕܩܶܕ݂ܪܘܽܢ ܘܐܰܫܩܺܝܬ ܠܗܘ̇ܢ ܟܠ ܕܐܶܬܐܰܡܰܪ ܠܝ ܘܐܰܪܝܶܡܘ ܩܳܠܗܘܽܢ ܘܰܒܟ݁ܰܘ ܟܠܗܘܽܢ. ܘܝܺܬܶܒܢ ܬܰܡܳܢ ܘܨܲܡܢ ܥܕ݂ܰܡܳܐ ܠܪܰܡܫܳܐ܀ ܘܰܗܘܳܐ ܠܚܰܝܘܡܳܐ ܕܰܡܚܳܪ ܘܗܳܐ ܚܰܝܠܳܐ ܕܟܰܠܺܕ݂ܳܝܶܐ ܟܰܪܟ݁ܳܗ ܠܰܡܕܝܰܢ̱ܬ݁ܳܐ: ܘܰܒܥܺܕ݂ܳܢ ܪܰܡܫܳܐ ܫܒܰܩܬܶܗ ܠܥܰܡܳܐ ܐܶܢܳܐ ܒܰܪܘܽܟ݂ ܘܢܶܦܩܶܬ ܘܩܳܡܶܬ ܠܝ ܥܰܠ ܒܰܠܘܽܛܳܐ ܘܡܶܬܥܰܩ ܗ̱ܘܝܺܬ ܥܰܠ ܨܶܗܝܘܽܢ: ܘܡܶܬܬ݁ܰܢܰܚ ܥܰܠ ܫܒܝܺܬ݁ܳܐ ܕܐܶܬܳܬ݂ ܥܰܠ ܥܰܡܳܐ. ܘܗܳܐ ܡܶܢ ܫܶܠܝܳܐ ܪܘܽܚܳܐ ܕܚܰܝܠܳܐ ܐܰܪܝܰܡܬ݁ܰܢܝ ܘܐܰܣܶܩܬܰܢܝ ܠܥܶܠ ܡܶܢ ܦܘܽܪܳܗ ܕܐܘܿܪܰܫܠܶܡ ܠܥܶܠ. ܘܰܚܙܝܺܬ ܘܗܳܐ ܐܰܪܒܥܳܐ ܡܰܠܰܐܟ݂ܶܐ ܕܩܳܝܡܺܝܢ ܗ̱ܘܰܘ ܥܰܠ ܐܰܪܒܥܳܐ ܙܳܘܝ̈ܳܬܳܗ ܕ݁ܡܕܺܝܢ̱ܬ݁ܳܐ ܟ݁ܰܕ݂ ܐܰܚܝܕ݂ܺܝܢ ܗ̱ܘܰܘ ܚܰܕ݂ ܚܰܕ݂
VI. And it came to pass on the next day, and behold, the army of the Chaldeans surrounded the city, and at the time of evening I, Baruch, left the people, and I went out and stood by the oak and was saddened over Zion and was groaning over the captivity that had come upon the people. And behold, suddenly a spirit of strength took me and lifted me up above the wall of Jerusalem on high. And I saw, and behold, four angels standing upon the four corners of the city, each of them holding a lamp


[p. 1075] {#po.0.p1075}
ܡܶܢܗܘܽܢ ܠܰܡܦܺܐܕ݂ܳܐ ܕܢܘܳܪܳܐ ܒܐܝܺܕ݂ܰܝ̈ܗܘܽܢ. ܘܡܰܠܰܐܟ݂ܳܐ ܐ̱ܚܪܺܢܐ ܢܚܶܬ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܡܶܢ ܟܰܝܡܳܝܐ ܘܐܶܡܰܪ ܠܗܘܽܢ: ܐܰܚܘܰܕܘ ܠܰܡܰܦܺܐܕܰܝ̈ܟܘܽܢ ܘܠܳܐ ܬ݇ܕܠܩܘܽܢ ܥܕ݂ܰܡܳܝܐ ܕܐܺܡܰܝܪ ܠܟܘܽܢ. ܐܶܫܬ݁ܰܕ݂ܪܶܬ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܕܠܘܽܩܕܰܡ ܐܳܡܰܪ ܠܰܐܪܥܳܐ ܡܶܠܬ݁ܳܐ ܘܐܶܣܚܺܡ ܠܘܳܬܳܗ ܡܶܕܶܡ ܕܦܰܩܕܰܢܝ ܡܳܪܝܳܐ ܡܪܰܡܳܐ.
of fire in their hands. And another angel was descending from heaven and said to them: Hold your lamps, and do not light them until I tell you. For I have been sent to speak first to the earth and to deposit with it what the Most High Lord has commanded me. And I saw him descend into the Holy of Holies and take from there the veil and the holy ephod and the mercy seat and the two tablets


[p. 1076] {#po.0.p1076}
ܘܰܚܙܝܺܬܶܗ ܕܰܢܚܶܬ ܠܒܺܝܬ ܩܕܘܽܫ ܩܘܽܕ̈݁ܫܶܐ ܘܰܢܣܰܒ ܡܶܢ ܬܰܡܳܢ ܐܰܦܰܝ̈ ܬܰܪܥܳܐ ܘܐܶܦܘܽܕܳܐ ܩܰܕ݂ܝܺܫܳܐ ܘܚܘܽܣܳܝܐ ܘܐܰܪܬ݁ܶܝܢ ܠܘܽܚܶܐ ܘܰܠܒܘܽܫܳܐ ܩܰܕ݂ܝܺܫܳܐ ܕܟ݂ܳܗܳܢܶܐ ܘܦܝܰܪܡܳܐ ܘܐܰܪܒܥܝܿܢ ܘܰܬܡܳܢܶܐ ܟܰܐܦ̈ܶܐ ܡܚܰܩܕ̈ܳܢ: ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܕܠܳܒܶܫ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܟܳܗܢܳܐ ܘܟܠܗܘܽܢ ܡܳܐܢܰܝ̈ ܩܘܽܕ݂ܫܳܐ ܕܡܰܫܟܢܳܐ. ܘܐܶܡܰܪ ܠܰܐܪܥܳܐ ܒܩܳܠܳܐ ܪܳܡܳܐ: ܐܰܪܥܳܐ ܐܰܪܥܳܐ ܐܰܪܥܳܐ: ܫܡܰܥ ܦܶܬܓܳܡܶܗ ܕܐܰܠܳܗܳܐ ܚܰܝܠܬ݁ܳܢܳܐ ܘܩܰܒ݁ܶܠ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܕܡܰܓܥܶܠ ܐ̱ܢܳܐ ܠܶܟܝ ܘܢܰܛܰܪ ܐܶܢܘܽܢ ܥܕ݂ܰܡܳܐ ܠܙܰܒ݂ܢܶܐ ܐ̱ܚܪ̈ܳܝܳܐ ܡܳܐ ܕܐܶܬܦܰܩܶܕܬܝ ܬܶܐܠܨܺܝܢ ܐܶܢܘܽܢ: ܕܠܳܐ ܢܶܫܬܰܠܛܘܽܢ ܒܗܘܽܢ ܢܘܽܟܪܳܝܶܐ. ܡܶܛܽܠ ܕܰܡܛܳܝܐ ܠܶܗ ܙܰܒ݂ܢܳܐ ܕܐܶܫܬܠܶܡ ܠܙܰܒ݁ܢܳܐ ܘܐܘܽܪܺܫܠܶܡ: ܥܕ݂ܰܡܳܐ ܕܢܶܬܐܶܡܰܪ ܕܬܘܽܒ ܬܶܗܦܘܽܟ݂ ܬܶܬܩܰܢ ܠܥܳܠܰܡ܂ ܘܦܶܬܚܰܬ ܐܰܪܥܳܐ ܦܘܽܡܳܗ ܘܒܶܠܥܰܬ ܐܶܢܘܽܢ܀
and the holy garment of the priests and the censer and the forty-eight precious stones which the priest wore, and all the holy vessels of the tabernacle. And he said to the earth with a loud voice: Earth, earth, earth, hear the word of the mighty God and receive what I commit to you, and guard them until the end of times, so that when you are commanded, you may return them; so that foreigners may not possess them. For the time has come that it should be delivered for a time, and Jerusalem also, until it is said that it should be restored again forever. And the earth opened its mouth and swallowed them.


[p. 1077] {#po.0.p1077}
ܘܡܶܢ ܒܳܬܰܪ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܫܡܰܥܬܶܗ ܠܡܰܠܰܐܟ݂ܳܐ ܗܰܘ ܕܐܳܡܰܪ ܠܡܰܠܰܐܟ݁ܶܐ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܕܐܰܚܝܺܕ݂ܝܺܢ ܗ̱ܘܰܘ ܠܰܡ̈ܦܺܐܕܶܐ: ܘܗܳܟܝܶܠ ܘܰܣܚܘܽܦܘ ܫܘܽܪܶܝܗ ܥܕ݂ܰܡܳܐ ܠܦ̈ܶܬܶܐܣܶܝܐ: ܕܠܳܐ ܢܶܫܬܰܒܗܪܘܽܢ ܒܥܶܠܕ݁̈ܒܳܒܶܐ ܘܢܺܐܡܪܘܽܢ: ܕ݁ܰܚܢܰܢ ܣܚܰܦܢܳܝܗ̱ܝ ܠܫܘܽܪܶܗ ܕܨܗܝܘܽܢ ܘܐܰܘܩܶܝܕ݂ܢ ܐܰܬ݂ܪܶܗ ܕ݁ܐܰܠܳܗܳܐ
VII. And after these things I heard the Angel saying to the Angels who were holding the lamps: Release therefore and overthrow its walls to the foundations, lest the enemies boast and say: We overthrew the wall of Zion and burned the wall of the mighty God and occupied the place in which I stood before.
ܚܰܝܠܬܳܢܳܝܐ: ܘܐܶܚܰܕ݂ܬܘܽܢܺܝ ܗ̱ܝ ܠܰܐܬ݂ܪܳܐ ܕܩܳܐܶܡ܂ ܗ̱ܘܝܺܬ ܒܶܗ ܡܶܢ ܩܕܝܺܡ܀ ܡܰܠܰܐܟ݂ܶܐ ܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܥܒܰܕ݂ܘ ܐܰܝܟ ܕܰܦܩܰܕ݂ ܠܗܘܽܢ: ܘܟܰܕ݂ ܒܰܕ݂ܰܪܘ ܙܳܘ̈ܝܳܬܶܗ ܕܫܘܽܪܳܐ: ܩܳܠܳܐ ܐܶܫܬܡܰܥ ܡܶܢ ܓܰܘܶܗ ܕܗܰܝܟܠܳܐ: ܡܶܢ ܒܳܬܰܪ ܕܰܢܦܰܠ ܫܘܽܪܳܐ: ܕܐܳܡܰܪ: ܒܘܽܠܘ ܒܥܶܠܕ̈ܒܳܒܶܐ ܘܐܰܘ ܣܳܢ̈ܐܶܐ: ܐܰܪܦܝܺ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܗܰܘ ܕܢܳܛܰܪ ܒܰܝܬܳܐ. ܘܐܶܙܰܠܬ ܠܥܰܡܳܐ ܐܶܢܳܐ ܒܰܚܪܘܽܟ. ܘܰܗܘܳܐ ܡܶܢ ܒܳܬܰܪ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ: ܥܰܠ ܚܰܝܠܬܳܐ ܕܟܰܠܺܕܳܝܶܐ ܘܐܰܚܕܘܽܗ̱ܝ ܠܒܰܝܬ݁ܳܐ ܘܰܠܟ݂ܽܠ ܕܰܚܕܳܪ̈ܰܘܗ̱ܝ ܘܰܠܥܰܡܳܐ ܟܟܰܝܘ: ܘܡܶܢܗܘܽܢ ܩܰܛܺܝܠܘ
VIII. The angels, however, did as he commanded them; and when they had dissipated the corners of the wall, a voice was heard from the inner part of the temple, after the wall fell, saying: Enter, enemies, and come, adversaries, for he who guarded the House has departed. And I, Baruch, went away. And it came to pass after these things, the army of the Chaldeans entered and took the house and everything around it and led the people captive and killed some of them and bound King Zedekiah and sent him to the king of Babylon.


[p. 1078] {#po.0.p1078}
ܘܰܠܨܶܕܩܺܝܳܐ ܡܰܠܟܳܐ ܦܟܰܪܘ ܘܫܰܕܪܘܽܗ̱ܝ ܠܘܳܬ ܡܰܠܟܳܐ ܕܒܳܒܶܠ܀ ܘܐܶܬܚܺܝܬ ܐܶܢܳܐ ܒܰܚܪܘܽܟ ܘܐܶܪܰܡܝܳܐ ܗܰܘ ܕܐܶܫܬܟ݁ܰܚ ܕܟܶܐ ܠܶܒܶܗ ܡܶܢ ܚ̈ܛܳܗܶܐ: ܗܰܘ ܕܠܳܐ ܐܶܬܬܚܺܝܕ݂ ܒܰܠܒܘܽܟܝܳܗ ܕܰܡܕ݂ܝܺܢܬܳܐ: ܘܰܨܪܰܝܢ ܡܳܐܢܰܝܺܢ ܘܰܒܟܰܝܢ ܘܐܶܬܐܰܒ݂ܰܠܢ ܘܨܳܡܢ ܫܰܒܥܳܐ
IX. And I, Baruch, came, and Jeremiah, whose heart was found pure from sins, who had not been captured in the occupation of the city; and we tore our garments and wept and mourned and fasted for seven days.
ܝܰܘܡܺܝܢ܀ ܘܰܗܘܳܐ ܡܶܢ ܒܳܬܰܪ ܫܰܒܥܳܐ ܝܰܘ̈ܡܝܺܢ: ܗܘܳܐ ܥܠܰܝ ܦܶܬܓܳܡܶܗ ܕܐܰܠܳܗܳܐ ܘܐܶܡܰܪ ܠܺܝ: ܐܶܡܰܪ ܠܶܐܪܰܡܝܳܐ ܕܢܺܐܙܰܠ ܘܰܢܩܰܝܶܡ ܫܒܝܽܬܶܗ ܕܥܰܡܳܐ
X. And it came to pass after seven days, the word of the Lord came upon me and said to me: Tell Jeremiah to go and confirm the captivity of the people as far


[p. 1079] {#po.0.p1079}
ܥܕ݂ܰܡܳܐ ܠܒܳܒܶܠ. ܐܰܢ̱ܬ ܕܶܝܢ ܟܰܬܰܪ ܗܳܪܟ݁ܳܐ ܒܚܘܽܪܒܳܗ ܕܨܶܗܝܘܽܢ: ܘܐܶܢܳܐ ܡܚܰܘܶܐ ܐ̱ܢܳܐ ܠܳܟ ܡܶܢ ܒܳܬܰܪ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܝܰܘ̈ܡܳܬ݁ܳܐ ܡܶܕܶܡ ܕܰܥܬܝܺܕ݂ ܕܢܶܓܕ݁ܰܫ ܒܰܐܚܪܳܝܰܬ ܝܰܘ̈ܡܳܬܳܐ. ܘܐܶܡܪܶܬ ܠܶܐܪܰܡܝܳܐ ܐܰܝܟ݂ ܕܦܰܩܕܳܢܝ ܡܳܪܝܳܐ. ܘܗܘّ ܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܐܶܙܰܠ ܥܰܡ ܥܰܡܳܐ: ܐܶܢܳܐ ܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܒܰܪܘܽܟ ܗܶܦܟܶܬ ܐܶܬܚܺܝܬ ܘܢܶܬܒܶܬ ܩܕ݂ܳܡ ܬܰܪ̈ܥܰܘܗ̱ܝ ܕܗܰܝܟܠܳܐ ܘܚܰܝܬ ܐܘܰܠܬܳܬ݁ܳܐ ܗܳܕ݂ܶܐ ܥܰܠ ܨܶܗܝܘܽܢ ܘܐܶܡܰܪܬ: ܛܘܽܒܰܘܗ̱ܝ ܠܗܰܘ ܕܠܳܐ ܐܶܬܬܝܺܠܶܕ݂ ܐܰܘ ܠܗܰܘ ܕܐܶܬܝܺܠܶܕ ܘܡܺܝܬ. ܠܰܢ ܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܠܰܐܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܚܰܝܺܝܢ: ܘܳܝ ܠܰܢ ܕ݁ܰܚܙܰܝܢ ܥܳܩ̈ܳܬ݁ܳܗ ܕܨܶܗܝܘܽܢ ܘܡܶܕ݁ܶܡ ܕܓ݂ܰܕ݂ܫܳܗ ܠܐܘܿܪܺܫܠܶܡ. ܐܶܩܪܶܐ ܠܣܝܺܪ̈ܶܢܳܬܳܐ ܡܶܢ ܝܰܡܳܐ ܘܐܰܢ݈ܬܶܝܢ ܠܺܠܺܝܬ̈ܳܐ ܡܶܢ ܡܰܕ݂ܒܪܳܐ ܐܶܬܰܝ̈ܢ: ܘܦܺܐ̈ܬܶܐ ܘܝܳܪܘܳܪܶܐ ܡܶܢ ܥܳܒ̈ܶܐ: ܐܶܬܬܥܝܺܪܘ ܘܐܰܣܘܽܪܘ ܚܰܨܰ̈ܝܟܘܽܢ ܠܡܰܪܩܘܽܕܬܳܐ ܘܣܰܒܘ ܥܰܡܝ ܐܘܰܠܬܳܬܳܐ ܘܐܰܪܩܶܕ݂ܘ ܥܰܡܝ. ܐܰܢ̱ܬܘܽܢ ܦܰܠܳܚ̈ܶܐ: ܠܳܐ ܬܶܙܪܥܘܽܢ ܬܘܒ: ܘܐܰܢ̱ܬܝ ܐܰܪܥܳܐ ܠܡܳܢܳܐ ܚܳܒܫܳܐ ܐܰܢ̱ܬܝ ܦܺܐܪ̈ܶܐ ܕܥܰܠܠܳܬܟ݂ܝ܆ ܐܰܚܝܘܽܕ݂ܝ ܒܓܰܘܶܟܝ ܚܰܠܝܘܽܬܳܐ ܕܡܳܙܘܰܢܰܝܟܝ. ܘܐ݇ܢ̱ܬܝ ܓܦܶܬܳܐ: ܠܡܳܢܳܐ ܡܰܡܛܝܳܐ ܐܰܢ̱ܬܝ ܕܐܶܐܠܶܦܶܝܢ ܚܰܡܪܶܟܝ܆ ܕܠܐ ܬܘܒ ܡܶܬܩܰܪܰܒ ܡܢܶܗ ܒܨܶܗܝܘܽܢ: ܘܠܳܐ ܬܘܽܒ ܪܝܺܫ ܦ݁ܺܐܪܶܐ ܡܶܬܩܰܪܒܝܳܢ. ܘܐܰܢ̱ܬܘܽܢ ܫܡܰܝܳܐ: ܐܰܚܘܽܕ݂ܘ ܛܰܠܟܘܽܢ: ܘܠܳܐ ܬܶܦܬܚܘܽܢ ܐܰܘܨܪ̈ܶܐ ܕ݁ܡܶܛܪܳܐ. ܘܐܰܢ̱ܬ ܫܶܡܫܳܐ: ܐܰܚܘܽܕ݂ ܢܘܽܗܪܗܘܽܢ ܕܙܰܠܝܺܩܰ̈ܝܟ: ܘܐܰܢ̱ܬܝ ܣܰܗܪܳܐ: ܐܰܕ݂ܥܶܟ݂ ܣܘܽܓܳܐܐ ܕܢܘܽܗܪܳܟܝ. ܠܡܳܢܳܐ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܬܘܽܒ ܢܶܕ݂ܢܰܚ ܢܘܽܗܪܳܐ ܐܰܝܟܳܐ ܕܢܘܽܗܪܳܗ ܕܨܲܗܝܘܽܢ ܚܫܰܟ݂: ܘܐܰܢ̱ܬܘܽܢ ܚܰܬܢܶܐ ܠܳܐ ܬܶܥܠܘܽܢ: ܘܰܒܬܘܽܠܳܬ݁ܳܐ ܒܰܟܠܺܝܠܶܐ ܠܳܐ ܢܶܨܛܺܒ݁ܬܳܢ. ܘܐܰܢ̱ܬܶܝܢ ܢܶܫܶܐ: ܠܳܐ ܬܨܰܠܝܳܢ ܕܬܰܐܠܕܳܢ. ܢܶܬܒ݂ܰܣܺܝܡܳܢ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܝܰܬܺܝܪܳܐܝܺܬ ܥܰܩܪܳܬܳܐ: ܘܢܶܚܕ݁̈ܝܳܢ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܠܰܝܬ ܠܗܶܝܢ ܒܢ̈ܰܝܳܐ: ܘܐܰܝܠܺܝܢ ܕܐܝܺܬ ܠܗܶܝܢ ܒܢܰܝܳܐ ܢܶܬܟܰܡܪܳܢ ܒܬܶܐܢܟܳܬܳܐܢ ܐܰܘ ܠܡܳܢܳܐ ܬܘܒ ܗܳܘܶܝܢ ܠܗܘܢ ܒܢܰܝܳܐ ܠܰܒܢܰܝ̈ܢܳܫܳܐܢ ܐܰܘ ܙܰܪܥܶܗ ܕܰܒܢܰܝ̈ܢܳܫܳܐ ܠܡܳܢܳܐ ܬܘܽܒ ܡܶܫܬ݁ܰܡܰܗ: ܐܰܝܟܳܐ ܕܗܳܕ݂ܶܐ ܐܶܡܳܐ ܚܶܪܒܰܬ ܘܰܒܢܶܝ̈ܗ ܐܶܫܬܒܰܝܘ܆ ܡܶܢ ܗܳܫܳܐ ܠܳܐ ܬܘܒ ܬܺܐܡܪܘܽܢ ܥܰܠ ܫܘܽܦܪܳܐ: ܘܠܳܐ ܬܶܬܢܘܽܢ ܥܰܠ ܝܰܐܝܘܽܬܳܐ. ܐܰܢ̱ܬܘܽܢ ܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܟܳܗ̈ܢܶܐ: ܣܰܒܘ ܩܠܺܝܕ݂ܶܐ ܕܒܶܝܬ ܡܰܩܕ݂ܫܳܐ ܘܰܕ݂ܡܰܘ ܐܳܢܘܽܢ ܠܪܰܘܡܳܐ ܕܰܫܡܰܝܳܐ ܘܗܰܒܘ ܐܶܢܘܽܢ ܠܡܳܪܝܳܐ ܘܐܶܡܰܪܘ: ܛܰܪ ܒܰܝܬ݁ܳܟ݂ ܐܰܢ̱ܬ: ܚܢܰܢ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܗܳܐ ܐܶܫܬܟܰܚܢ ܪܰܒ݁ܰܝ ܒܰܝܬܳܐ ܕܰܓܳܠ̈ܶܐ. ܘܐܰܢ̱ܬ݁ܶܝܢ ܒܬܘ̈ܺܠܳܬܳܐ ܕܥܳܙܠܳܢ ܒܘܵܨܶܐ ܘܫܺܪܳܢܶܐ ܥܰܡ ܕܰܗܒܳܐ ܕܐܰܘܦܝܿܪ: ܐܶܣܬ݁ܰܪ̈ܗܰܒ݁ܺܝܢ ܘܣܰܒܶܝ̈ܢ ܟܽܠܗܶܝܢ ܘܰܐܪܡܰܘ ܒܢܘܽܪܳܐ: ܕܢܰܘܒܶܠ ܐܶܢܘܽܢ ܠܘܳܬ ܗܰܘ ܕܰܥܒܰܕ݂ ܐܶܢܘܽܢ: ܘܫܰܠܗܶܒܬܳܐ ܬܰܦܢܶܐ ܐܶܢܘܽܢ ܠܘܳܬ ܗܰܘ ܕܰܒ݂ܪܳܐ ܐܶܢܘܽܢ: ܕܠܳܐ ܢܶܫܬܰܠܛܘܽܢ ܒܗܘܢ ܒܥܶܠܕ̈ܒܳܒܶܐ܀
as Babylon. But you remain here in the desolation of Zion, and I will show you after these days what is to happen at the end of days. And I said to Jeremiah: As the Lord has commanded me. And he indeed went with the people, but I, Baruch, returned and sat before the gates of the temple and lamented this lamentation over Zion and said: Blessed is he who was not born, or who was born and has died. But to us who live, woe to us, because we have seen the tribulations of Zion and what has happened to Jerusalem. I will call the Sirens from the sea; and you, Liliths, come from the desert, and Satyrs and reeds from the woods: awake and gird your loins for mourning and take up lamentations with me and wail with me! You farmers, do not sow again, and you, earth, why do you give the fruits of your crops? Keep within yourself the sweetness of your foods. And you, vine, why do you give your wine anymore? Because it will not be offered again in Zion, nor will the firstfruits be offered again. And you, heavens, withhold your dew, and do not open the treasures of rain. And you, sun, withhold the light of your rays; and you, moon, extinguish the multitude of your light. For why should light rise again, where the light of Zion is darkened? And you, bridegrooms, do not enter, and let not virgins be adorned with crowns; and you, women, do not pray to give birth. For let the barren rejoice rather and let those who have no children be glad, and let those who have children be saddened. For why should they give birth in pains and bury in groans? Or why should men have children again, or why should the seed of their nature be named again, where this Mother is desolate and her children have been led into captivity? From now on, do not speak again of beauty, nor discourse on splendor. But you, priests, take the keys of the Sanctuary and throw them into the height of heaven and give them to the Lord and say: Guard your house yourself, for we have been found to be deceitful stewards. And you, virgins, who spin fine linen and silk with the gold of Ophir, hasten and take everything and throw it into the fire, so that it may bring them to Him who made them, and the flame may send them back to Him who created them, lest the enemies possess them.


[p. 1080] {#po.0.p1080}


[p. 1081] {#po.0.p1081}


[p. 1082] {#po.0.p1082}


[p. 1083] {#po.0.p1083}
ܐܰܡܰܝܪ ܐ̱ܢܐ ܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܗܳܕ݂ܶܐ ܐܶܢܳܐ ܒܰܪܘܽܟ݂ ܠܩܘܰܒܠܶܟ݂ܝ ܒܳܒܶܠ: ܐܶܠܽܘ ܗܘܰܝܬܝ ܐܰܢ̱ܬܝ ܡܰܨܠܚܳܐ ܘܨܶܗܝܘܽܢ ܥܳܡܪܳܐ ܒܬܶܫܒ݁ܺܚܳܬܳܗ: ܟܺܐܒܿܳܐ ܗܘܳܐ ܠܰܢ ܪܰܒܿܳܐ: ܕܗܳܘܝܳܐ ܗ̱ܘܰܝܬ ܫܽܘܝܳܐ ܠܨܲܗܝܘܿܢ. ܗܳܫܳܐ ܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܗܳܐ ܟܺܐܒ݂ܳܐ ܕܠܳܐ ܡܶܣܬܰܝܳܐ: ܘܐܶܢܩܳܬ݁ܳܐ ܕܠܳܐ ܟܰܝܠܳܐ ܡܘܽܟܟܳܬܳܐ: ܕܐܰܢ̱ܬܝ ܗܳܐ ܡܰܨܠܚܰܬܝ ܘܨܶܗܝܘܽܢ ܚܰܪܒ݁ܳܐ ܡܰܢܘܽ ܢܶܗܘܶܐ ܕܰܝܳܢܳܐ ܥܰܠ ܗܳܠܺܝܢ܆ ܐܰܘ ܠܡܰܢܘܽ ܢܶܩܒܘܽܠ ܥܰܠ ܡܶܕܶܡ ܕܓ݂ܰܕ݂ܫܰܢ ܥܠܰܝܢ܆ ܐܳܘ̄ ܡܳܪܝܳܐ: ܐܰܝܟ݁ܰܢܳܐ ܣܰܝܒܰܪܬ܆ ܫܟܶܒܘ ܐܰܒ݂ܳܗܳܬܰܢ ݂ܠܳܐ ܟܺܐܒ̈ܶܐ: ܘܙܰܕܺܝ̈ܩܶܐ ܗܳܐ ܕܲܡܟܝܢ ܒܐܰܪܥܳܐ ܒܰܢܝܳܚܳܐ: ܠܳܐ ܓܝܪ ܝܳܕ݂ܥܺܝܢ ܠܥܺܝܩܘܽܬܳܐ ܗܳܕܶܐ: ܘܐܳܦ ܠܶܐ ܫܡܰܥܘ ܥܰܠ ܡܶܕܶܡ ܕܓܰܕ݂ܫܰܢ. ܢܶܗܘ̈ܝܺܢ ܠܺܟܝ ܐܰܪܥܳܐ ܐܶܕ݂ܢܶܐ:
XI. I, Baruch, say this against you, Babylon: If you had been prosperous and Zion dwelt in her glory, it would have been a great sorrow to us that you were equal to Zion. But now, behold, infinite sorrow and groaning without measure, because you, behold, have prospered and Zion is desolate. Who will be the judge of these things? Or to whom shall we complain about these things that have happened to us? O Lord, how have you suffered [this]? Our fathers rested without sorrows and the righteous, behold, sleep in the earth in tranquility. For they did not know this anguish, nor did they hear of these things that have happened to us. Let the earth have ears for you, and the dust a heart; and go and announce in the underworld and say to the dead: Blessed are you more than we ourselves, who are alive.


[p. 1084] {#po.0.p1084}
ܘܐܰܢ̱ܬ ܥܰܦܪܳܐ ܠܒܳܐ: ܘܙܶܠܘ ܘܚܰܘܰܘ ܒܰܫܝܘܽܠ ܘܐܶܡܰܪܘ ܠܡܝܺܬܶܐ: ܛܘܽܒܰܝܟܘܽܢ ܠܟܘܢ ܝܰܬܝܰܪ ܡܶܢܰܢ ܕܝܺܠܰܢ ܕ݂ܚܰܝܝܺܢ ܚܢܰܢ܀ ܐܶܠܳܐ ܗܳܕܶܐ ܐܰܡܰܪ ܐ̱ܢܳܐ ܐܰܝܟ݂ ܕܡܶܬܚܰܫܰܒ ܐ݈ܢܳܐ: ܘܐܰܡܰܠܠܶ ܠܩܘܽܒܠܶܟܝ ܐܰܪܥܳܐ ܕ݁ܡܰܨܠܚܳܐ. ܠܳܐ ܗܘܳܐ ܒܟܽܠ ܙܒܰܢ ܦ݁ܶܠܓ݁ܶܗ ܕܚܰܘܡܳܐ ܡܫܰܠܗܶܒ: ܘܠܳܐ ܐܰܡܚܝܳܢ ܙܰܠܝ̈ܩܰܘܗ̱ܝ ܕܫܶܡܫܳܐ ܡܰܡܚܝܳܢ. ܠܳܐ ܬ݁ܶܣܒ݂ܪܝܳܢ ܘܐܶܣܒܪܝܺܢ ܕܰܒܟ݁ܽܠܙܒ݁ܰܢ ܬ݁ܶܗܘܶܐ ܡܰܨܠܚܳܐ ܘܚܳܕ݂ܝܳܐ: ܘܠܳܐ ܣܰܓܝܺ ܬܶܬܪܝܺܡܝܺܢ ܘܰܬܫܰܥܒܕܺܝܢ. ܫܰܪܝܺܪܳܐܝܺܬ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܒܙܰܒ݂ܢܳܗ ܡܶܬܬܥܺܝܪܳܐ ܥܠܰܝܟܝ ܚܶܡܬ݁ܳܐ ܗܳܝ ܕܗܳܫܳܐ ܒܢܰܓܺܝܪܘܽܬ ܪܘܽܚܳܐ: ܐܰܝܟ
XII. But I will say this, as I think, and I will speak against you, earth, who prosper. The south does not burn forever, nor do the rays of the sun burn constantly. Do not presume to think that you will always be prosperous and rejoicing, nor be greatly exalted, so that you oppress. For at its certain time, the fury which is now held back in long-suffering as if by reins will awake against you. And when I had said these things, I fasted for seven days.


[p. 1085] {#po.0.p1085}
ܕܒܰܦ݁ܓܘܽܕ݂ܶܐ: ܐܰܚܺܝܕ݂ܳܐ. ܘܟ݁ܰܕ݂ ܐܶܡܪܶܬ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ: ܨܳܡܶܬ݂ ܫܰܒܥܳܐ ܝܰܘ̈ܡܝܳܢ܀ ܘܰܗܘܳܐ ܡܶܢ ܒܳܬܰܪ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ: ܐܶܢܳܐ ܒܰܪܘܽܟ݂ ܩܳܐܶܡ ܗܘܺܝܬ ܥܰܠ ܛܘܽܪܳܐ ܕܨܲܗܝܘܽܢ: ܘܗܳܐ ܩܳܠܳܐ ܐܶܬܳܐ ܡܶܢ ܡܪ̈ܰܘܡܶܐ ܘܐܶܡܰܪ ܠܝܺ: ܩܘܽܡ ܥܰܠ ܪ̈ܶܓܠܰܝܟ ܒܰܪܘܽܟ݂ ܘܰܫܡܰܥ ܡܶܠܬܶܗ ܕܐܰܠܳܗܳܐ ܚܰܝܠܬ݁ܳܢܳܐ. ܡܶܛܽܠ ܕܐܶܬܕܰܡܪ̱ܬ ܥܰܠ ܡܶܕ݁ܶܡ ܕܓ݂ܰܕ݂ܫܳܗ ܠܨܲܗܝܘܽܢ: ܡܶܛܽܠ ܗܳܢܳܐ ܡܶܬܢܛܺܝܪܘܽ ܐܶܬܢܛܰܪܬ ܠܫܘܽܠܳܡܗܘܢ ܕܙܰܒ̈ܢܶܐ ܕܬ݂ܶܗܘܶܐ ܠܣܳܗܕܘܽܬܳܐ. ܕܐܶܢ ܢܺܐܡܪܳܢ ܡܶܡܬܘܺܡ܂ ܡܕ݁̈ܝܺܢܳܬܳܐ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܡܰܨܠܚܳܢ: ܕ݁ܰܠܡܳܢܳܐ ܐܰܝܬܝܺ ܥܠܰܝܢ ܐܰܠܳܗܳܐ ܚܰܝܠܬ݁ܳܢܳܐ ܦܘܽܪܥܳܢܳܐ ܗܳܢܳܐ: ܐܶܡܰܪ ܠܗܶܝܢ ܐܰܢ̱ܬ ܘܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕ݁ܕܳܡܶܝܢ ܠܳܟ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܰܚܙܰܝܬ݁ܘܽܢ ܒܚܺܫܬܳܐ ܗܳܕ݂ܶܐ ܘܦܘܽܪ̈ܥܳܢܶܐ ܕܐܳܬܶܝܢ ܥܠܰܝܟ݁ܶܝܢ ܘܥܰܠ ܥܰܡܟܶܝܢ: ܒܙܰܒ݂ܢܶܗ ܕܰܓܡܺܝܪܳܐܝܺܬ ܢܶܬܪܕܘܽܢ ܥܰܡ̱ܡܶܐ. ܘܗܳܝܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܢܩܰܘܘܽܢ. ܘܐܶܢ ܢܺܐܡܪܳܢ ܒܗܰܘ ܙܰܒ݂ܢܳܐ ܕܐ݇ܡܰܬܝ܆ ܬܺܐܡܰܪ ܠܗܶܝܢ ܐܰܢ̱ܬ݁ܶܝܢ ܕܐܶܫܬܺܝܬܶܝܢ ܚܰܡܪܳܐ ܡܨܰܠܠܳܐ: ܐܶܫܬܳܝܺܝ̈ܢ ܐܳܦ ܡܶܢ ܬ݁ܶܛܪܶܗ. ܕܕܰܝܳܢܶܗ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܕܪܳܡܳܝܐ ܕܠܳܐ ܡܰܣܰܒ ܒܐܰܦ̈ܶܐ ܗܽܘ: ܡܶܛܽܠ ܗܳܢܳܐ ܥܰܠ ܒܢܰܘ̈ܗ̱ܝ ܠܘܽܩܕ݂ܰܡ ܠܳܐ ܚܳܣ: ܐܶܠܳܐ ܐܶܫܬ݁ܰܢܰܕ݂ ܒܗܘ̈ܢ ܐܰܝܟ ܕܰܒܣܳܢܰܐܘܗ̱ܝ ܡܶܛܽܠ ܕܰܚܛܰܝܘ. ܗܳܝܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܗܳܟܝܽܠ ܐܶܬܪܕܝܽܘ
XIII. And it came to pass after these things, I, Baruch, was standing upon Mount Zion; and behold, a voice came from the heights and said to me: Stand upon your feet, Baruch, and hear the word of the mighty God. Because you were amazed at the things that happened to Zion: for this reason you will be preserved until the end of times, so that you may be a testimony, so that if those cities that prosper ever say: Why did the mighty God bring this retribution upon us?, you and those like you, who have seen this calamity and the retributions that come upon you and upon your people in their time, may tell them, so that the nations may be perfectly chastised. And so they will remain. Even if they say at that time: When?, you will say to them: You, who have drunk the filtered wine, will also drink of its dregs. For the judgment of the Most High does not respect persons. For this reason he did not spare his own children before, but tormented them as his enemies, because they sinned. Then therefore they were chastised,


[p. 1086] {#po.0.p1086}


[p. 1087] {#po.0.p1087}
ܡܶܛܽܠ ܕܢܶܬܚܰܝܣܵܝܘܿܢ. ܗܳܫܳܐ ܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܐܰܢ̱ܬܘܽܢ ܥܰܡ̱ܡܶܐ ܘܐܶܡ̈ܘܳܬܳܐ ܚܰܝܳܒܺܝܢ ܐܢ̱ܬܘܿܢ ܕܗܳܢܳܐ ܟ݁ܽܠܶܗ ܙܰܒܢܳܐ ܕܳܫܬܘܳܢܳܗ ܠܰܐܪܥܳܐ ܘܐܶܬܚܰܫܰܚܬܘܽܢ ܒܰܒܪܝܺܬ݁ܳܐ ܕܠܳܐ ܒܙܶܕ݂ܩܳܐ. ܒܟܽܠܙܒܰܢ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܗܘܝܺܬ ܡܰܛܝܶܒ ܠܟܽܘܢ ܘܐܰܢܺܝܚܬܘܽܢ ܗܘܰܝܬܘܽܢ
so that they might obtain pardon. But now you, peoples and nations, are guilty, because in all this time you have trampled the earth and have used the creation unjustly. For I was always doing good to you and you were always ungrateful.
ܟܳܦܪܝܺܢ ܒܛܰܝܒܘܽܬܳܐ ܒܟܽܠܙܒܰܢ܀ ܘܰܥܢܺܝܬ ܘܐܶܡܪܶܬ: ܗܳܐ ܐܰܘܕܰܥܬܰܢܝ ܕܘܰܒܳܪܗܘܽܢ ܕܙܰܒܢ̈ܶܐ ܘܡܶܝܕܶܡ ܕܰܥܬܝܰܕ݂ ܕܢܶܗܘܶܐ ܡܶܢ ܒܳܬܰܪ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ: ܘܐܶܡܰܪܬ ܠܝܽ ܕܰܠܥܰܡ̱ܡܶܐ ܐܳܙܶܠ ܦܘܰܪܥܳܢܳܐ ܕܐܶܬܐܰܡܰܪ ܡܶܢܳܟ. ܘܗܳܫܳܐ ܝܺܕ݁ܰܥ ܐ̱ܢܳܐ ܕ݁ܣܰܓܺܝܐܺܝܢ ܐܶܢܘܿܢ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܰܚܛܰܝܘ: ܘܰܒܡܰܨܠܚܳܢܘܽܬܳܐ ܚܝܰܘ ܘܐܶܙܰܠܘ ܡܶܢ ܥܳܠܡܳܐ. ܘܩܰܠܺܝܠ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܥܰܡ̱ܡܶܐ ܡܶܫܬܰܚܪܝܺܢ ܒܗܳܢܘܽܢ ܙܰܒ̈ܢܶܐ. ܗܳܢܘܽܢ ܕܰܠܗܳܢܘܽܢ ܡܶܬܬܰܡܪܝܺܢ ܡܶܠܶܐ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܐܶܡܰܪܬ. ܘܡܳܢܳܐ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܝܽܘܬܪܳܢܳܐ ܐܺܝܬ ܒܗܳܕܶܐ ܐܰܘ ܡܳܢܳܐ ܕܒܺܝܫ ܡܶܢ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܕ݁ܰܚܙܰܝܢ ܕܓ݂ܳܕ݁̈ܫܳܢ ܡܣܰܟܶܝܝܢܰܢ ܕܢܶܚܙܶܐ܆ ܬܘܽܒ ܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܐܰܡܰܠܠܶ ܩܕܳܡܰܝܟ: ܡܳܢܳܐ ܝܺܬܰܪܘ ܗܳܢܘܽܢ ܕܝܺܕ݂ܰܥܘ ܩܕܳܡܰܝܟ ܘܠܳܐ ܐܶܙܰܠܘ ܒܰܣܪܝܺܩܘܽܬܳܐ ܐܰܝܟ ܫܰܪܟ݁ܳܐ ܕܥܰܡ݈ܡܶܐ܆ ܘܠܳܐ ܐܶܡܰܪܘ ܠܡܝܺ̈ܬܶܐ ܕܗܰܒܘ ܠܰܢ ܚܰܝ̈ܶܐ. ܐܶܠܳܐ ܒܟ݁ܽܠܙܒܰܢ ܡܶܢܳܟ݂ ܕܳܚܠܺܝܢ ܗ̱ܘܰܘ: ܘܐܘܽܪ̈ܚܳܬܳܟ݂ ܠܳܐ ܐܰܪܦܺܝܘ ܘܗܳܐ ܐܶܬܚܰܦ݁ܰܛܘ: ܘܐܳܦܠܳܐ ܡܶܛܘܽܠܳܬܗܘܽܢ ܪܚܰܡܬ ܥܰܠ ܨܶܗܝܘܽܢ. ܘܐܶܢ ܐ̱ܚܪ̈ܳܢܶܐ ܐܰܪܫܰܥܘ: ܡܶܬܒ݁ܥܶܐ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܠܳܗ ܠܨܶܗܝܘܽܢ ܕܒܰܡ̈ܒܳܕ݂ܰܝܗܘܽܢ ܕܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕ݁ܰܦ݂ܠܰܚܘ ܛܰܒ݁ܳܬܳܐ ܢܶܫܬܒܺܝܩ ܠܳܗ: ܘܠܳܐ ܒܰܡܒܳܕ݂ܰܝܗܘܽܢ ܕܥܳܒܕܰܝ̈ ܥܰܘܠܳܐ ܐܶܛܒܰܥ. ܐܶܠܳܐ ܡܰܢܘܽ: ܡܳܪܝܳܐ ܡܳܪܝ: ܡܰܕ݁ܪܶܟ ܠܶܗ ܠܕ݂ܝܳܢܳܟ ܐܰܘ ܡܰܢܘܽ ܡܥܰܩܶܒ ܥܘܽܡܩܳܗ ܕܐܘܽܪܚܳܟ܆ ܐܰܘ
XIV. And I answered and said: Behold, you have indicated to me the reason for the times and what is to be after these things, and you have told me that retribution is to come to the peoples as you have said. And now I know that there are many who have sinned and lived in prosperity and have departed from the world, but few of the people will be survivors in those times in which the words you have spoken will be said. And what utility is there in this, or what worse things than these that we have seen happen shall we expect to see? But I will speak again in your sight. What did those profit who confessed before you and did not walk in vanity like the rest of the peoples and did not say to the dead: Give us life, but always feared you and did not abandon your ways? And behold, they were diligent, and yet for their sake you did not have mercy on Zion. Even if others acted wickedly, it was owed to Zion that, because of the works of those who had done good, it should be forgiven, nor should it be submerged because of the works of those doing iniquity. But who, Lord and ruler, will attain your judgment? Or who will investigate the depth of your way? Or who will calculate the weight of your path? Or who will be able to think of your incomprehensible counsel? Or who among those born has ever found the beginning or the end of your wisdom? For we have all been made like a breath. For just as a breath ascends not from the heart and is extinguished: so is the nature of men, who go without their will and do not know what is to be for them in the end. For the righteous hope well for the end and depart from this life without fear, because they have the virtue of their works kept with you in a storehouse. For this reason they also leave


[p. 1088] {#po.0.p1088}


[p. 1089] {#po.0.p1089}


[p. 1090] {#po.0.p1090}
ܐܰܘ ܡܰܢܘܽ ܡܶܫܟ݁ܰܚ ܡܶܬܚܰܫܰܒ ܥܰܠ ܬܰܪܥܺܝܬ݁ܳܟ݂ ܕܠܳܐ ܡܶܬܕܰܪܟܳܢܳܐ܆ ܐܰܘ ܡܰܢܘܽ ܡܶܡܬܘܰܡ ܡܶܢ ܐ݈ܢܳܫܳܐ ܐܶܫܟ݁ܰܚ ܪܝܺܫܐ ܐܰܘ ܫܘܰܠܳܡܳܐ ܕܚܶܟܡܬ݁ܳܟ ܆ ܟܘܽܠܰܢ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܐܶܬܕܰܡܺܝܢ ܠܗܶܒܠܳܐ. ܐܰܝܟ݁ܰܢܳܐ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܕ݁ܗܶܒܠܳܐ ܣܳܠܶܩ ܕܠܳܐ ܡܶܢ ܠܶܒܳܐ ܘܗܳܦ݁ܶܟ݂ ܕܳܥܶ: ܗܳܟܰܢܰܐ ܗ̱ܘ ܟܝܢܗܘܽܢ ܕܰܒܢܰܝ̈ ܐ̱ܢܳܫܳܐ: ܕ݁ܠܳܐ ܒܕܰܥܬܗܘܽܢ ܐܳܙܠ̱ܝܢ: ܘܡܳܢܳܐ ܗܳܘܶܐ ܠܗܘܢ ܒܫܘܽܠܳܡܳܐ: ܠܳܐ ܠܫܘܽܠܳܡܳܐܝܳܐ ܘܰܕ݂ܠܳܐ ܕܶܚܠܬ݁ܳܐ ܡܶܢ ܗܳܢܳܐ ܥܘܽܡܪܳܐ ܐܶܙܠ̱ܝܢ: ܡܶܛܽܠ ܕܐܝܺܬ ܠܗܘܿܢ ܠܘܳܬܳܟ݂ ܚܰܚܠܳܐ ܕ݁ܰܥܦ̈ܳܕ݂ܶܐ ܕܰܢܛܰܪ ܒܙܰܘܨܪ̈ܶܐ. ܡܶܛܽܠ ܗܳܢܳܐ ܐܳܦ ܗܶܢܘܽܢ ܕܠܳܐ ܕ݁ܶܚܠܬܳܐ ܫܳܒܩܝܺܢ ܠܶܗ ܠܥܳܠܡܳܐ ܗܳܢܳܐ ܿ ܘܟ݁ܰܕ݂ ܬܟܝܠܝܢ ܒܚܰܝܕܘܽܬܳܐ ܡܣܰܟ݁ܺܝܢ ܕܰܢܩܰܒܠܘܽܢ ܥܳܠܡܳܐ ܕܰܡܠܰܒܬ ܠܗܘܿܢ. ܠܰܢ ܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܘܳܝ ܠܰܐܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܐܳܦ݂ ܗܳܢܳܐ ܡܶܨܛܰܥܪܝܺܢܰܢ ܘܰܒܗܰܘ ܙܰܒܢܳܐ ܡܣܰܟ݁ܶܝܢܰܢ ܠܒܚ̈ܫ̈ܳܬܳܐ. ܐܰܢ̱ܬ ܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܝܳܕ݂ܰܥ ܐܰܢ̱ܬ ܬܪܰܨܳܐܝܺܬ ܡܳܢܳܐ ܥܒܕܬ ܡܶܢ ܥܒܳܕܰܝܟ: ܡܶܛܽܠ ܕܰܚܢܰܢ ܠܳܐ ܡܶܫܟܚܝܺܢܰܢ ܕܢܶܣܬ݁ܰܟ݁ܰܠ ܒܢܶܡܕܶܡ
this world without fear and, trusting in joy, hope that they will receive the world which you have promised them. But woe to us, who are now afflicted with reproaches and expect evils at that time. But you rightly know what you have done concerning your servants, because we cannot understand any good, how you are our creator. I will speak again


[p. 1091] {#po.0.p1091}
ܕܛܳܒ ܐܰܝܟ݁ܰܢܳܐ ܕܐܰܢ̱ܬ ܓܳܒܘܽܠܰܢ. ܬܘܽܒ ܕ݁ܶܝܢ
in your sight, Lord and ruler. When the world was not of old with its inhabitants,
ܐܝܺܬܰܘܗ̱ܝ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܥܳܠܡܳܐ ܡܶܢ ܩܕ݂ܝܺܡ ܥܰܡ ܥܳܡܘܽܪ̈ܰܘܗ̱ܝ: ܐܶܬܚܰܫܰܒܬ ܘܐܶܡܺܝܪܬ ܒܢܶܡܠܬܳܐ:
you thought and said by a word and immediately the works of creation stood in your sight; and you said that you would make man the administrator of your works for your world, so that it might be known that he was not


[p. 1092] {#po.0.p1092}
ܩܕܳܡܰܝܟ: ܘܐܰܡܰܪܬ݁ ܕ݂ܬ݂ܶܥܒܶܕ݂ ܠܥܳܠܡܳܟ ܒ݁ܰܪܢܳܫܳܐ ܡܟܰܪܢܣܳܢܳܐ ܠܰܥܒܳܕ̈ܰܝܟ: ܕܢܶܬܝܺܕ݂ܰܥ ܕܠܳܐ ܗܘܳܐ ܗܽܘ ܐܶܬܥܒܺܝܕ݂ ܡܶܛܽܠ ܥܳܠܡܳܐ: ܐܶܠܳܐ ܥܳܠܡܳܐ ܡܶܛܽܠܳܬܶܗ. ܘܗܳܟܳܐ ܚܳܙܶܐ ܐ̱ܢܳܐ
made for the sake of the world, but the world for his sake. And now I see the world, which was made for us, behold, remaining, but we, for whom it was made, departing.
ܕ݁ܥܳܠܡܳܐ ܕܐܶܬܥܒܶܕ݂ ܡܶܛܽܠܳܬ݂ܰܢ ܗܳܐ ܡܩܰܘܶܐ: ܘܰܚܢܰܢ ܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܡܶܛܽܠܳܬ݁ܰܢ ܗܘܳܐ ܐܳܙܠ̱ܝܺܢܰܢ܀
XV. And the Lord answered and said
ܐܶܬܕܰܡܰܪܬ ܥܰܠ ܡܰܒܕܰܠܬܗܘ̇ܢ ܕܰܒܢܰܝ̈ܢܳܫܳܐ: ܐܶܠܳܐ ܠܳܐ ܛܳܒܳܐܝܺܬ ܕܳܢܬ ܥܰܠ ܒܺܝܫܳܬ݁ܳܐ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܓ݂ܰܕ̈ܫܳܢ ܠܰܐܝܠܺܝܢ ܕܚܳܛܶܝܢ: ܘܕܐܿܡܪܬ ܕܙܰܕ݁ܺܝܩܶܐ ܐܶܬܚܛܰܦܘ ܘܢܰܦܫܺܝܥܶܐ ܐܰܨܰܠܰܚܘ: ܘܕܐܶܡܰܪܬ ܕܐ̱ܢܳܫ ܠܳܐ ܝܰܕ݂ܥܶܗ ܠܕ݂ܝܰܢܳܟ: ܡܶܛܽܠ ܗܳܢܳܐ ܫܡܰܥ ܘܐܡܪ ܠܳܟ: ܘܨܰܘܬ ܘܐܰܫܡܥܳܟ݂ ܡܶܠܰܝ. ܫܰܦܺܝܪ ܠܳܐ ܝܳܕ݂ܰܥ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܒܰܪ ܐ̱ܢܳܫܳܐ ܕܝܰܢܝ ܐܶܠܘܽ ܠܳܐ ܩܰܒܶܠ ܢܳܡܘܽܣܳܐ ܘܠܶܠܘܽ ܠܳܐ ܐܰܪܬ݁ܺܝܬܶܗ ܒܣܰܟܘܽܠܬܳܢܘܽܬܳܐ. ܗܳܫܳܐ ܕܶܝܢ ܡܶܛܽܠ ܕܟ݂ܰܕ݂ ܝܳܕ݂ܰܥ ܥܳܒܰܪ: ܘܡܶܛܽܠ ܗܳܢܳܐ ܐܳܦ݂ ܟ݁ܰܕ݂ ܝܳܕ݂ܰܥ ܢܶܫܬܰܢܰܩ. ܘܥܰܠ ܙܰܕ̈ܺܝܩܶܐ ܕܐܶܡܰܪܬ ܥܠܰܝܗܘܽܢ ܕܡܶܛܽܠܳܬܗܘܽܢ ܐܶܬܳܐ ܗܳܢܳܐ ܥܳܠܡܳܐ: ܐܶܠܳܐ ܐܳܦ ܗܰܘ ܬܘܽܒ ܕܰܥܬܝܺܕ݂ ܡܶܛܽܠܳܬܗܘܽܢ. ܗܳܢܳܐ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܥܳܠܡܳܐ ܐܝܺܬܰܘܗ̱ܝ ܠܗܘܽܢ ܐܰܓܘܽܢܳܐ ܘܥܰܡܠܳܐ ܒܠܶܐܘܳܬܳܐ ܣܰܓܝܺܬ݂ܳܐ: ܘܗܰܘ ܗܳܟܝܶܠ ܕܰܥܬܝܺܕ݂ ܟܠܺܝܠܳܐ ܒܬܶܫܒܘܽܚܬܳܐ
to me: You are rightly amazed at the departure of men, but you have not rightly judged the evils that happen to those who sin; and because you said that the righteous were snatched away and the impious prospered and because you said that man does not know your judgment: for this reason hear and I will tell you, and listen and I will make you hear my words. Man would not have known my judgment well, unless he had received the law and unless I had exhorted him in understanding. But now, because he sinned knowingly, and therefore he will also be tormented knowingly. And concerning the righteous, what you said that this world came for their sake, indeed even the one that is to come will be for their sake. For this world is a struggle and trouble in much labor; and that one, therefore, which is to come, is a crown in great glory.


[p. 1093] {#po.0.p1093}


[p. 1094] {#po.0.p1094}
ܘܰܥܢܺܝܬ ܘܐܶܡܰܪܬ: ܡܳܪܝܳܐ ܡܳܪܝ: ܗܳܐ ܕܗܳܢܳܐ ܙܰܒܢܳܐ ܙܥܘܽܪ̈ܺܝܢ ܘܒܺܝ̈ܫܺܝܢ: ܘܡܰܢܘܽ ܡܶܫܟ݁ܰܚ ܕ݁ܰܒ݂ܗܳܢܶܐ ܩܰܠܺܝܠ ܢܶܐܪܰܬ ܗܰܘ ܕܠܝܬ
XVI. And I answered and said: Lord and ruler, behold, the years of this time are few and evil, and who can acquire that which has no measure with this little?
ܠܶܗ ܡܫܘܽܚܬܳܐ. ܘܰܥܢܳܐ ܡܳܪܝܳܐ ܘܐܶܡܰܪ ܠܺܝ: ܠܘܳܬ ܡܪܰܚܡܳܢܳܐ ܠܳܐ ܡܶܬܚܰܫܰܒ݂ ܙܰܒܢܳܐ ܣܰܓܝܳܐܳܐ܆ ܐܳܦ ܠܳܐ ܫܢ̈ܰܝܳܐ ܙܥܘܽܪ̈ܝܳܬܳܐ. ܡܳܢܳܐ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܐܰܗܢܝ̈ ܠܳܐܕ݂ܳܡ ܕܰܚܚܝܐ ܬܫܰܥܡܳܐܐ ܘܰܬܠܳܬܺܝܢ ܫܢ̈ܺܝܢ ܘܰܥܒܰܪ ܥܰܠ ܡܶܕܶܡ ܕܐܶܬܦܰܩܰܕ݂܆ ܠܳܐ ܗܳܝܶܕ݂ ܐܰܗܢܝܶܗ ܠܶܗ ܣܘܰܓܳܐܐ ܕܙܰܒܢܳܐ ܕܰܚܝܳܐ: ܐܶܠܳܐ ܐܰܝܬܝܶܗ ܡܰܘܬܳܐ ܘܰܩܛܰܥ ܫܢܰܝ̈ܳܐ ܕܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܐܶܬܝܺܠܶܕ݂ܘ ܡܶܢܶܗ. ܐܰܘ ܡܳܢܳܐ ܐܶܬܢܟܝܺ ܡܘܽܫܶܐ ܕܰܡܳܐܐ ܘܥܶܣܪܝܺܢ ܫܢܺܝܢ ܚܝܳܐ ܒܰܠܚܘܽܕ݂: ܘܡܶܛܶܠ ܕܐܰܫܬܰܥܒܰܕ݂ ܠܡܰܢܘܽ ܕܓ݂ܰܒܠܶܗ:
XVII. And the Lord answered and said to me: With the Most High, time is not counted as much, nor years as few. For what did it profit Adam that he lived nine hundred and thirty years? And he transgressed what he had been commanded. Therefore, the multitude of time in which he lived did not profit him, but it brought death and cut off the years of those who had been born from him. Or what detriment did Moses suffer because he lived only one hundred and twenty years and, because he submitted himself to Him who fashioned him, he brought the law to the seed of Jacob and illuminated the lamp for the race of Israel?


[p. 1095] {#po.0.p1095}
ܐܰܝܬܝܺ ܢܳܡܘܽܣܳܐ ܠܙܰܪܥܶܗ ܕܝܰܥܩܘܽܒ: ܘܐܰܢܗܰܪ ܪܳܓ݂ܳܐ ܠܓ݂ܶܢܣܶܗ ܕܐܝܺܣܪܳܝܶܠ܀ ܘܰܥܢܺܝܬ ܘܐܶܡܰܪܬ: ܗܰܘ ܗܘ ܕܐܰܢܗܰܪ ܢܣܰܒ ܡܶܢ ܢܘܽܗܪܳܐ: ܘܩܰܠܺܝܠ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܐܶܬ݁ܕ݂ܰܡܝܺܘ ܒܶܗ: ܗܳܢܘܽܢ ܕܝܢ ܣܰܓ̈ܺܝܐܶܐ ܕܐܰܢ݁ܗܰܪ ܠܗܘܢ ܢܣܰܒܘ ܡܶܢ ܚܶܫܘܽܟ݂ܶܗ ܕܐܳܕ݂ܳܡ
XVIII. And I answered and said: He who illuminated took from the light, and few are those who imitated him: But those many, whom he illuminated, took from the darkness of Adam, nor were they delighted by the light of the lamp.
ܘܠܳܐ ܐܶܬܒܰܣܰܡܘ ܒܢܘܽܗܪܳܐ ܕܰܦܪܳܓ݁ܳܐ܀ ܘܰܥܢܳܐ ܘܐܶܡܰܪ ܠܺܝ: ܡܶܛܶܠ ܗܳܢܶܐ ܒܗܰܘ ܙܰܒܢܳܐ ܐܰܩܺܝܡ ܠܗܘܿܢ ܕܰܝܰܬܺܩܺܐ ܘܐܶܡܰܪ: ܗܳܐ ܣܳܡܶܬ ܩܕ݂ܳܡܰܝܟܘܿܢ ܚܰܝ̈ܶܝܐ ܘܡܰܘܬܳܐ
XIX. And he answered and said to me: For this reason at that time he established a testament for them and said: Behold, I have set before you


[p. 1096] {#po.0.p1096}
ܘܐܰܣܗܶܕ݂ ܒܗܘܽܢ ܫܡܰܝܳܐ ܘܐܰܪܥܳܐ. ܝܳܕ݂ܰܥ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܕܙܰܒܢܶܗ ܩܰܠܺܝܠ ܗ̱ܘܵ. ܘܰܫܡܰܝܳܐ ܘܐܰܪܥܳܐ ܐܰܚܺܝܬܺܝܗܘܽܢ ܒܟ݁ܽܠ ܙܒܰܢ. ܗܶܢܘܽܢ ܕܝܢ ܡܶܢ ܒܳܬܰܪ ܡܰܘܬܶܗ ܕܗܰܘ ܚܛܰܝܘ ܘܐܰܥܒܰܪܘ: ܟܰܕ݂ ܝܕܥܝܢ ܕܐܺܝܬ ܠܗܘܢ ܢܳܡܘܽܣܳܐ ܡܰܟܣܳܢܳܐ ܘܢܘܽܗܪܳܐ ܗܰܘ ܕܠܐ ܡܶܫܟ݁ܰܚ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܕܢܰܛܥܶܐ ܒܶܗ ܡܶܕܶܡ ܐܳܦ ܟܰܘܟ݁ܒܶܐ ܕܣܳܗܕܝܺܢ: ܘܐܶܢܳܐ ܕܥܰܠ ܟܽܠ ܕܐܝܺܬ ܕܳܐܶܢ ܐ̱ܢܳܐ. ܐܰܢܬ ܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܠܳܐ ܐܶܬܚܰܫܰܒ݂ ܒܢܰܦܫܳܟ ܥܰܠ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ: ܘܠܳܐ ܬܶܬܩܰܝܰܪ ܥܰܠ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܰܗܘܰܝ̈. ܗܳܫܳܐ ܫܘܽܠܳܡܶܗ ܗ̱ܘܵ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܕܙܰܒܢܳܐ ܐܰܘ ܕܨܶܒ̈ܘܳܬܳܐ ܐܰܘ ܕܡܰܨܠܚܳܢܘܽܬܳܐ: ܐܰܘ ܕܨܰܥܪܳܐ ܡܶܬܒܥܺܐ ܘܠܳܐ ܫܘܿܪܳܝܶܗ. ܡܶܛܽܠ ܕܐܶܢ ܢܶܗܘܶܐ ܒܰܪܢܳܫܳܐ ܡܨܠܰܚ ܒܩܰܕ݂ܡܳܝܬ݂ܶܗ ܘܰܒܣܰܝܒܘܽܬܶܗ ܢܶܨܛܰܥܰܪ: ܛܳܥܶܐ ܟܘܽܠܳܗ ܡܰܨܠܚܳܢܘܽܬܳܐ ܕ݁ܰܗܘܳܐ ܠܶܗ. ܘܬܘܒ ܐܶܢ ܢܶܗܘܶܐ ܒܰܪ ܐ̱ܢܳܫܳܐ ܡܶܨܛܰܥܰܪ ܒܩܰܕ݂ܡܳܝܬܶܗ ܘܒܰܐ݈ܚܪܳܝܬ݂ܶܗ ܢܶܗܘܶܐ ܘܰܒܨܠܰܚ: ܠܳܐ ܡܶܬܕ݁ܰܟ݁ܰܪ ܬܘܽܒ ܨܰܥܪܶܗ. ܘܬܘܒ ܫܡܰܥ ܕܟܽܠܶܗ ܙܰܒ݂ܢܳܐ ܗܳܢܳܐ ܡܶܢ ܝܰܘܡܳܐ ܕܐܶܬܓܪܰܪ ܡܰܘܬܳܐ ܥܰܠ ܐܰܝܠܺܝܢ ܕܥܳܒܪܝܺܢ ܒܶܗ ܒܝܳܕ݂ܳܐ ܙܰܒܢܳܐ: ܐܶܠܘܽ ܟܘܽܠ ܐ̱ܢܳܫ ܡܰܨܠܰܚ ܙܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܘܰܒܚܰܪܬ݁ܶܗ ܡܶܬܚܰܒܰܠ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ: ܠܰܣܪܝܺܩܘܽܬ݂ܳܐ ܗܳܘܶܐ ܙܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܟܽܠܥܕ݁ܶܡ܀
life and death, and he called heaven and earth to witness against them. For he knew that his time was short, but that heaven and earth would always subsist. They, however, after his death sinned and transgressed, knowing that they had the law reproving them and the light, which nothing could deceive, the spheres also bearing witness and me, who judge all things that are. But you, do not think in your soul about these things, nor be afflicted about those things that have been. For now the end of time, whether of affairs or of prosperity or of ignominy, is required and not its beginning. For if a man were prosperous in his beginnings and in his old age were afflicted with reproaches, he forgets all the prosperity he had. And again, if a man were afflicted with reproaches in his beginnings, and in his ends were prosperous, he does not remember his ignominy again. And again, hear: in all this time, from the day that death was decreed against those who transgress in this time, if everyone had been prosperous and had been corrupted in his end, all would have been in vain.


[p. 1097] {#po.0.p1097}
ܡܶܛܺܠ ܗܳܢܳܐ ܗܳܐ ܝܰܘ̈ܡܳܬܳܐ ܐܳܬܶܝܢ ܘܢܶܣܬ݁ܰܪܗܒܘܽܢ ܙܰܒ̈ܢܶܐ ܝܰܬܝܰܪ ܡܶܢ ܩܰܕ݂̈ܡܳܝܶܐ: ܘܢܶܪܗܛܘܽܢ ܥܶܕ݁ܳܢܶܐ ܝܰܬܝܰܪ ܥܰܠ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܰܥܒܰܪܘ: ܘܢܶܥܒܪܳܢ ܫܢ̈ܶܐ ܩܰܠܺܝܠܳܐܝܺܬ: ܝܰܬܝܰܪ ܥܰܠ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܕܩܳܝܡܳܢ. ܡܶܛܽܠ ܗܳܢܳܐ ܗܳܐ ܐܰܪܝܺܡܬܳܗ ܠܨܶܗܝܘܽܢ ܕܐܶܣܬܰܪܗܰܒ ܝܰܬܝܺܪܳܐܝܺܬ ܘܐܶܣܥܘܽܪ ܥܳܠܡܳܐ ܒܙܰܒܢܶܗ. ܗܳܫܿܳܐ ܗܳܟܝܶܠ ܐܰܚܘܰܕ݂ ܒܠܳܒܺܝܟ ܟ݁ܽܠܕ݁ܶܡ ܕ݁ܰܡܦ݁ܰܩܶܕ݂ ܐ݈ܢܳܐ ܠܟ ܘܚܬܘܽܡ ܐܶܢܶܝܢ ܒܬܰܘܳܢܶܐ ܕܡܰܡܕ݂ܥܳܟ. ܘܗܳܝܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܐܶܚܰܘܶܝܟ ܕܝܺܢܶܗ ܕܚܰܝܠܝ: ܘܐܘܽܪ̈ܚܳܬܝ ܕܠܳܐ ܡܶܬܥܰܩ̈ܒܳܢ. ܙܰܠ ܗܳܟܝܶܠ ܘܐܶܬܩܰܕܰܫ ܫܰܒ݁ܥܳܐ ܝܰܘ̈ܡܳܬܳܐ: ܘܠܳܐ ܬܶܐܟܘܽܠ ܠܰܚܡܳܐ ܘܠܳܐ ܬܶܫܬ݁ܶܐ ܚܰܡܪܳܐ ܘܠܳܐ ܬ݁ܺܐܡܰܪ ܠܐ݈̱ܢܳܫ. ܘܡܶܢ ܒܳܬܰܪ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܬ݁ܳܐ ܠܰܐܬ݂ܪܳܐ ܗܳܢܳܐ ܘܡܶܬܓ݁ܠܶܐ
XX. For this reason, behold, days are coming and the times will hasten more than the former ones and the ages will run more than those that have passed, and the years will pass more quickly than the present ones. Therefore, I have now removed Zion, so that I might visit the world more quickly in its time. Now, therefore, keep in your heart all [that] I shall command you, and seal them in the inner chambers of your mind. And then I will show you the judgment of my power and my inscrutable ways. Go therefore and sanctify yourself for seven days, and do not eat bread nor drink wine nor speak to anyone, and afterwards come to this place; and I will appear to you and speak true things with you and give you precepts concerning the reason of the times. For they will come and will not delay.


[p. 1098] {#po.0.p1098}


[p. 1099] {#po.0.p1099}

### PRAYER OF BARUCH THE SON OF NERIAH
ܐ݈ܢܳܐ ܠܳܟ ܘܫܰܪܝܺܪܳܐܝܺܬ ܐܰܡܰܠܠ ܥܰܡܳܟ ܘܐܶܦܰܩܕ݂ܳܟ݂ ܥܰܠ ܕܘܰܒܳܪܳܐ ܕܙܰܒ݁̈ܢܶܐ. ܐܳܬܶܝܢ ܐܶܢܘܽܢ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܘܠܳܐ ܡܶܫܬܘܚܪܝܺܢ܀ ܨܠܘܳܬ݂ܶܗ ܕܒܰܪܘܽܟ ܒܰܪ ܢܺܪܺܝܰܐ ܘܐܶܙܰܠܬ ܡܶܢ ܬ݁ܰܡܳܢ ܘܢܶܬܒܶܬ ܒܢܰܚܠܳܐ ܕܩܶܕܪܘܽܢ: ܒܰܡܥܰܪܬ݁ܳܐ ܕܐܰܪܥܳܐ: ܘܩܰܕ݂ܫܶܬ ܢܰܦܫܝ ܬܰܡܳܢ: ܘܠܰܚܡܳܐ ܠܳܐ ܐܶܟ݂ܠܶܬ݂ ܘܠܳܐ ܟܶܦܢܶܬ: ܘܡܰܝ̈ܳܐ ܠܳܐ ܐܶܫܬܝܺܬ ܘܠܳܐ ܨܗܝܺܬ: ܘܰܗܘܝܺܬ ܬܰܡܳܢ ܥܕ݂ܰܡܳܐ ܠܚܰܘܡܳܐ ܫܒܝܥܳܝܳܐ: ܐܰܝܟ݂ ܕܦܰܩܕܰܢܝ. ܘܡܶܢ ܒܳܬܰܪܟ݁ܶܢ ܐܶܬܝܺܬ ܠܰܐܬ݂ܪܳܐ ܗܰܘ ܕܡܰܠܶܠ ܥܰܡܝ ܬܰܡܳܢ. ܘܰܗܘܳܐ ܒܡܰܥܪ̈ܒ݁ܰܘܗ̱ܝ
XXI. And I went from there and sat in the torrent of Cedron in a cave of the earth and sanctified my soul there and did not eat bread nor was I hungry and did not drink water, nor was I thirsty, and I was there until the seventh day, as he had commanded me. And afterwards I came to the place where he had spoken with me. And it was at the setting of the sun, my soul began to have much thought; and I began to speak before the Most Mighty and said: O you who made the earth, hear me, who established the firmament by your word and


[p. 1100] {#po.0.p1100}
ܕܫܶܡܫܳܐ: ܘܫܰܪܝܺܬ ܕܐܶܡܰܠܠܶ ܩܕ݂ܳܡ ܚܰܝܠܬ݁ܳܢܳܐ ܘܐܶܡܪܶܬ: ܐܰܘ̄ ܗܰܘ ܕܰܥܒܰܕ݂ ܐܰܪܥܳܐ: ܫܡܰܥܰܝܢܝ: ܗܰܘ ܕܰܩܒ݁ܰܥ ܪܩܺܝܥܳܐ ܒܶܡܠܬܶܗ: ܘܪܰܘܡܗܘܽܢ ܕܰܫܡܰܝܳܐ ܫܰܪܰܪ ܒܪܘܽܚܳܐ: ܗܰܘ ܕܰܩܪܳܐ ܡܶܢ ܪܝܺܫ ܥܳܠܡܳܐ ܠܡܕ݁ܶܡ ܕ݁ܰܥܕ݂ܰܟ݁ܝܶܠ ܠܳܐ ܗܘܳܐ ܘܡܶܫܬ݁ܰܡܥܺܝܢ ܠܳܟ. ܐܰܢ̱ܬ ܕܰܠܰܐܝܰܪ ܦܩܰܕܬ ܒܪܶܡܪܳܟ݂ ܘܠܰܐܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܰܥܬܝܺܕ݁ܶܢ ܚܙܰܝܬ ܐܰܝܟ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܰܥܒܰܪ. ܐܰܢ̱ܬ ܕ݁ܰܠܚܰܝܠܰܘܳܬܳܐ ܕܩܳܝܡܺܝܢ ܩܕܳܡܰܝܟ ܒܚܘܽܫܳܒܳܐ ܪܰܒ݁ܳܐ ܡܕܰܒܪܰܬ: ܐܳܦ ܠܚܰܫ̈ܳܬܳܐ ܩܰܕ̈ܝܫܳܬ݁ܳܐ ܕܡܶܢܗܶܝܢ ܠܰܝܬ ܠܗܶܝܢ ܡܶܢܝܳܢ ܥܳܠܰܡ ܕܫܰܠܗܶܒ݂ܝܳܬ݁ܳܐ ܘܰܕܢܘܽܪܳܐ: ܘܳܠܶܝܢ ܕܩܳܝܡܳܢ ܚܕ݂ܳܪ̈ܰܝ ܬܪܘܺܢܳܘܣ ܕܝܺܠܳܟ݂ ܒܰܙܥܝܺܦܘܽܬܳܐ ܡܕܰܒܪܰܬ. ܠܳܟ ܒܰܠܚܘܽܕܰܝܟ ܐܝܺܬ ܗܳܕܶܐ ܕܬܶܥܒܶܕ݂ ܒܰܪܒܳܥܬ݁ܶܗ ܟܘܽܠ ܕܨܳܒܶܝܬ. ܗܰܘ ܕܰܒܥܶܕ݁ܢܳܝܢܳܐ ܛܘܽܦܰܝ̈ ܡܶܛܪܳܐ ܥܰܠ ܐܰܪܥܳܐ ܡܰܡܛܰܪ ܘܫܘܽܠܳܡܗܘܽܢ ܕܙܰܒ݁ܢܶܐ: ܥܰܕ݂ ܠܳܐ ܢܺܐܬܘܽܢ: ܐ݇ܢ̱ܬ ܒܰܠܚܘܽܕܰܝܟ ܝܳܕ݂ܥܰܬ. ܚܘܽܪ ܒܒܶܥܘܽܬܝ: ܐܰܢ̱ܬ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܒܰܠܚܘܽܕܰܝܟ ܡܶܫܟ݁ܰܚ ܐܰܢ̱ܬ ܕܰܬܣܰܝܒܰܪ ܠܟܽܠܗܘܽܢ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕ݂ܩܳܝܡܝܳܢ ܘܠܰܐܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܥܳܒܪܝܺܢ ܘܠܰܐܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܰܥܬܝܰܕ݂ܝܺܢ: ܠܰܐܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܚܳܛܶܝܢ ܘܠܰܐܝܠܶܝܢ ܕ݁ܡܶܙܕ݂ܰܕܩܝܺܢ: ܐܰܝܟ ܚܰܝܳܐ ܕܠܳܐ ܡܶܬܥܰܩܰܒ. ܐܰܢ̱ܬ ܗ̱ܘܽ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܒܰܠܚܘܽܕܰܝܟ ܚܰܝܳܐ ܕܠܳܐ ܡܳܐܶܬ ܘܠܳܐ ܡܶܬܥܰܩܰܒ. ܘܡܶܢܝܳܢܳܐ ܕܰܒܢܰܝ̈ܢܳܫܳܐ ܚܳܕ݂ܥܰܬ. ܘܐܶܢ ܒ݁ܙܰܒ݂ܢܳܐ ܣܰܓܝܺܐܳܐ ܚܛܰܘ: ܘܰܐܚܪ̈ܳܢܶܐ ܠܳܐ ܗܘܳܐ ܩܰܠܺܝܠ ܐܶܙܕܰܕܰܩܘ. ܐܰܢ̱ܬ ܝܳܕ݂ܥܰܬ ܕܐܰܝܟܿܳܐ ܡܢܰܛܪܰܬ ܚܰܪܬ݁ܳܐ ܕܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕ݁ܰܚܛܰܝܘ: ܐܰܘ ܫܘܽܠܳܡܳܐ ܕܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܐܶܙܕܰܕܰܩܘ. ܐܶܠܽܘ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܗܘܰܘ ܚܰܝ̈ܶܐ ܒܰܠܚܘܽܕ݂ ܕܗܽܪܟ݁ܳܐ ܠܟܽܠܢܳܫ: ܠܰܝܬ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܡܶܕ݁ܶܡ ܕܡܰܪܝܰܪ ܡܶܢ ܗܳܕܶܐ. ܡܳܢܳܐ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܡܰܗܢܶܐ ܚܰܝܠܳܐ ܕܗܳܦܶܟ݂ ܠܰܟܪܝܺܗܘܽܬܳܐ: ܐܰܘ ܡܳܙܘܽܢܳܐ ܕܣܰܒܥܳܐ ܕܗܳܦ݁ܶܟ݂ ܠܟܰܦܢܳܐ: ܐܰܘ ܫܘܽܦ݂ܪܳܐ ܕܗܳܦ݁ܶܟ݂ ܕܰܒ݂ܢܰܝ̈ܢܳܫܳܐ ܡܶܬܚܰܠܰܦ: ܐܰܘ ܠܳܐ ܡܶܕܶܡ ܐܰܝܟ ܡܳܐ ܕܐܝܺܬܺܝܢ ܗܳܘܶܐ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܡܶܢ ܩܕ݂ܝܰܡ: ܗܳܫܳܐ ܠܳܐ ܐܝܰܬܰܝܢ. ܘܐܰܝܟ ܡܳܐ ܕܐܝܺܬܰܝܢ ܗܳܟܳܐ: ܒܳܬܰܪ ܟܶܢ ܠܳܐ ܡܩܰܘܶܝܢܰܢ܆ ܐܰܠܘܽܠܳܐ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܕ݁ܰܥܬܝܰܕ݂ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܕܢܶܗܘܶܐ ܫܘܽܠܳܡܳܐ ܠܟ݁ܽܠ: ܠܰܣܪܝܺܩܘܽܬܳܐ ܗܳܘܶܐ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܥܘܰܪܺܝܪܘܽܬܳܐ. ܐܶܠܳܐ ܟ݁ܽܠ ܕܐܳܬܶܐ ܡܶܢ ܠܘܳܬܳܟ݂ ܐܰܘܕܰܥܰܝܢܝ: ܘܥܰܠ ܡܶܕܶܡ ܕܒܳܥܶܐ ܐ݈ܢܳܐ ܡܶܢܳܟ ܢܰܗܰܪܺܝܢܝ. ܥܕ݂ܰܡܳܐ ܠܶܐܡܰܬܝ ܡܶܕܶܡ ܕܡܶܬܚܰܒܰܠ ܡܶܬܚܰܘܶܐ: ܘܰܒܕ݂ܰܡܳܝܐ ܠܶܐܡܰܬܝ ܙܰܒܢܗܘܽܢ ܕܡܳܚ̈ܘܳܬܳܐ ܡܰܡܨܠܰܚ: ܘܒܰܕ݂ ܐܰܝܢܳܐ ܙܰܒ݂ܢܳܐ ܐܰܚܠܺܝܢ ܕܥܳܒܪܝܺܢ ܒܥܳܠܡܳܐ ܡܶܬܦܰܠܦܠܝܺܢ ܒܪܘܽܫܥܳܐ ܣܰܓܺܝܐܬܳܐ܆ ܦܩܘܰܕ݂ ܗܳܟܶܢܳܐ ܒܪ̈ܰܚܡܶܐ: ܘܐܰܩܺܝܡ ܟ݁ܽܠ ܕܐܶܡܰܪܬ ܕܐܰܝܬܶܐ: ܕܢܶܬܝܺܕ݂ܰܥ ܚܰܝܠܳܟ݂ ܠܰܐܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܣܳܒܪܝܺܢ
strengthened the height of heaven in spirit, who called from the beginning of the world what was not yet, and they obey you: you who commanded the air by your nod and saw the things that are to be, just as those that have passed: you, who rule the powers standing before you in great thought and the holy living beings, whose number is not, which you made from the age of flame and fire, which stand in the circuit of your throne in the indignation of the king, to you alone this is, that you may do immediately whatever you will: who in number rain the drops of rain upon the earth and you alone know the end of times, before they come: attend to my supplication! For you alone can sustain all who are and those who pass away, and those who are to be, those who sin, and those who are justified, as a living being inscrutable; for you alone are the living immortal and inscrutable, and you know the number of men; and if at any time many have sinned, yet others not a few have been justified. You know where you keep the end of those who have sinned, or the outcome of those who have been justified. For if that life alone existed, which each one has here, nothing would be more bitter than this. For what does virtue profit, which is converted into infirmity, or the nourishment of satiety, which is converted into hunger, or beauty, which then becomes hateful? For the nature of men is always changed. Or are we not, just as we were from the beginning, now not, and just as we are now, we will not remain afterwards? For if it had not been prepared that there should be an end of all things, their beginning would be in vain. But whatever comes from you, indicate to me and concerning whatever thing I shall ask you, illuminate me: how long that which is corrupted may remain and how long the time of mortals may prosper, and until what time those who pass away in the world may be defiled by much wickedness? Command therefore in mercy and fulfill whatever you said you would bring about, so that your power may be known to those who think your long-suffering is infirmity, and show to those who do not know and have seen what has happened to us and to the city


[p. 1101] {#po.0.p1101}


[p. 1102] {#po.0.p1102}


[p. 1103] {#po.0.p1103}


[p. 1104] {#po.0.p1104}
ܕܢܰܓܺܝܪܘܽܬ ܪܘܽܚܳܟ݂ ܟܪܝܺܗܘܽܬܳܐ ܗ̱ܝ: ܘܚܰܘܳܐ ܠܰܐܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܠܳܐ ܝܺܕ݂ܥܺܝܢ ܘܰܚܙܰܘ ܡܶܕ݁ܶܡ ܕܓܰܕܼܫܰܢ ܘܠܰܡܕ݁ܰܥܰܬܳܢ ܥܰܕ݂ ܗܳܟܳܐ: ܐܰܝܟ ܢܰܓܺܝܪܘܽܬ ܪܘܽܚܳܐ ܕܐܘܰܚܕ݂ܳܢܳܟ݂: ܡܶܛܽܠ ܕܐܵܢ̱ܬ ܩܪܺܝܬ݂ܰܢ ܥܰܠ ܫܡܳܟ݂ ܥܰܡܳܐ ܚܰܒܺܝܒܳܐ. ܟܘܽܠ ܗܳܟܝܺܠ ܡܶܢ ܗܳܟܳܐ ܟܝܳܢܳܐ ܡܳܝܘܽܬܳܐ: ܘܓ݁ܥܘܽܪ ܡܶܟܺܝܠ ܒܡܰܠܰܐܟܳܟ݂ ܕܡܰܘܬܳܐ ܘܐܶܬܚܙܶܐ ܬܶܫܒܘܽܚܬ݁ܳܟ ܘܐܶܬܝܺܕ݂ܰܥ ܪܰܒܘܽܬ ܝܳܐܝܘܽܬܳܟ݂ ܘܐܶܬܚܬܰܡ ܦܘܽܡ ܕ݁ܡܶܢ ܗܳܟܳܐ ܠܳܐ ܬܶܣܰܒ ܡܺܝ̈ܬܶܐ ܘܢܰܦܢܘܽܢ ܐܰܘܨܪ̈ܶܐ ܕܢܰܦ݁ܺܫܳܬ݁ܳܐ ܕܰܚܒܺܝܫܳܢ ܒܗܘܿܢ. ܣܰܓ̈ܺܝܐܺܝܢ ܐܶܢܺܝܢ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܫܢܰܝܳܐ ܐܰܝܟ ܕܨܰܕ̈ܝܳܢ ܕܡܶܢ ܝܰܘ̈ܡܰܝ ܐܒܪܳܗܳܡ ܘܐܝܰܣܚܳܩ ܘܝܰܥܩܘܽܒ ܘܰܕܟܽܠܗܘܽܢ ܐܰܝܠܺܝܢ ܕ݁ܕܳܡܶܝܢ ܠܗܘܿܢ ܕܒܰܐܪܥܳܐ ܪܳܡܟܺܝܢ. ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܕܡܶܛܽܠܳܬܗܘܽܢ ܐܶܡܰܪܬ ܕܰܒܪܰܝܬ ܥܳܠܡܳܐ ܘܗܳܫܳܐ ܚܰܘܳܐ ܒܰܥܓ݂ܰܠ ܬܶܫܒܘܽܚܬ݁ܳܟ݂: ܘܠܳܐ ܬ݁ܫܰܘܚܰܪ ܡܶܕܶܡ ܕܡܶܢܳܟ ܐܶܬܡܠܶܟ. ܘܰܗܘܳܐ ܫܰܠܡܶܬ ܡܶܠܚܶܗ ܕܰܨܠܘܽܬܳܐ ܗܳܕ݂ܶܐ: ܐܶܬܪܰܫܠܶܬ݂ ܣܰܓܺܝ܀
up to now according to the long-suffering of your power, because you called us, for the sake of your name, a beloved people. Every mortal nature is therefore from this. And rebuke, therefore, the angel of death, and let your glory appear, and let the magnitude of your beauty be known, and let Sheol be sealed, so that it may not receive the dead from this time forth, and let the storehouses of souls return those which are enclosed in them. For many are the years similar to those which have been desolate from the days of Abraham, and Isaac, and Jacob, and all those who are like them, who sleep in the earth, for whose sake you said that you created the world. And now, show your glory quickly, and do not delay what was promised by you. And it came to pass; I finished the words of this prayer; I was greatly weakened.


[p. 1105] {#po.0.p1105}
ܘܰܗܘܳܐ ܡܶܢ ܒܳܬܰܪ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܘܗܳܐ ܐܶܬܦ݂ܬ݁ܰܚܘ ܫܡܰܝܳܐ: ܘܰܚܙܺܝܬ ܘܚܝܠܶܐ ܐܶܬܝܰܗܶܒ ܠܺܝ ܘܩܳܠܳܐ ܡܶܢ ܡܪ̈ܰܘܡܶܐ ܐܶܫܬܡܰܥ ܘܐܶܡܰܪ ܠܺܝ: ܒܰܪܘܽܟ݂ ܒܰܪܘܽܟ݂: ܠܡܳܢܳܐ ܡܶܬܬܙܝܰܥ ܐܰܢ̱ܬ ܗܰܘ ܕܐܳܙܶܠ ܒܐܘܽܪܚܳܐ ܠܳܐ ܡܫܰܠܶܡ ܠܳܗ܆ ܐܰܘ ܗܰܘ ܕܦܳܪܶܫ ܒ݁ܝܰܡܳܐ ܐܶܠܳܐ ܡܳܛܶܐ ܠܰܠܡܺܐܢܳܐ ܡܶܫܟ݁ܰܚ ܢܶܬܒܰܝܰܐ܆ ܐܰܘ ܗܰܘ ܕܡܶܫܬ݁ܰܘܕܶܐ ܠܐ݈ܢܳܫ ܕ݁ܢܶܬ݁ܶܠ ܠܶܗ ܡܰܘܗܰܒ݁ܬ݁ܳܐ ܐܶܠܳܐ ܡܰܫܠܶܡ ܠܳܗ ܠܳܐ ܗܳܐ ܓ݁ܠܝܺܙܘܳܬܳܐ ܐܝܺܬܰܘܗ̱ܝ܆ ܐܰܘ ܗܰܘ ܕܙܳܪܰܥ ܐܰܪܥܳܐ ܐܶܠܳܐ ܒܙܰܒܢܳܗ ܢܶܚܨܘܽܕ ܦܺܐܪܶܝܗ: ܠܳܐ ܗܳܐ ܡܚܰܒ݁ܶܠ ܠܳܗ ܠܟ݁ܽܠܳܗ܆ ܐܰܘ ܗܰܘ ܕܢܳܨܶܒ ܢܶܨܒܬ݁ܳܐ: ܐܶܠܳܐ ܬܪܰܒ݁ܺܝܐ ܥܕ݂ܰܡܳܐ ܠܙܰܒ݂ܢܳܐ ܕܙܳܕܶܩ ܠܳܗ: ܕܰܠܡܳܐ ܡܣܰܟܶܐ ܗܰܘ ܕܢܰܨܒ݂ܳܗ ܕܢܶܣܰܒ ܡܶܢܳܗ ܦܺܐܪܶܐ܆ ܐܰܘ ܐܰܢ̱ܬ݁ܬ݁ܳܐ ܕܒܰܛܢܳܐ ܐܶܢ ܬ݁ܺܐܠܰܕ ܠܳܐ ܒܙܰܒ݂ܢܳܗ ܠܳܐ ܡܶܩܛܰܠ ܩܳܛܠܳܐ ܠܶܗ ܠܥܽܘܠܳܢܳܗ܆ ܐܰܘ ܗܰܘ ܕܒܳܢܶܐ ܒܝܬܐ ܐܶܠܳܐ
XXII. And it came to pass after these things, and behold, the heavens were opened, and I saw, and power was given to me, and a voice was heard from on high, and it said to me: Baruch, Baruch, why are you troubled? Does he who goes on a journey not complete it? Or can he who travels on the sea be comforted unless he arrives at the harbor? Or he who promises someone that he will give him a gift, if he does not deliver it, is it not a fraud? Or he who sows the earth, if he does not reap its fruits in its time, does he not lose everything? Or he who plants a planting, if it has not grown until the convenient time, does he who planted it expect to receive fruits from it? Or a woman who has conceived, if she gives birth prematurely, does she not kill her infant by killing it? Or he who builds a house, if he does not cover it with a roof and complete it, can it be called a house? Tell me these things first!


[p. 1106] {#po.0.p1106}
ܢܛܰܠܠܺܝܘܗ̱ܝ ܘܰܢܫܰܟܠܠܺܝܘܗ̱ܝ ܕܰܠܡܳܐ ܡܶܫܟ݁ܰܚ ܕܢܶܬܩܪܶܐ ܒܰܝܬܳܐ܆ ܐܶܡܰܪ ܠܺܝ ܠܘܿܩܕ݂ܰܡ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ܀ ܘܰܒܢܺܝܬ ܘܐܶܡܰܪܬ݁: ܠܳܐ: ܡܳܪܝܳܐ ܡܳܪܝ. ܘܰܥܢܳܐ ܘܐܶܡܰܪ ܠܺܝ܆ ܘܥܰܠ ܡܳܢܳܐ ܗܳܟܫܶܠ ܡܶܬܬ݁ܙܝܰܥ ܐܰܢ̱ܬ ܥܰܠ ܡܶܕܶܡ ܕܠܳܐ ܝܳܕ݂ܰܥ ܐܰܢ̱ܬ: ܘܥܰܠ ܡܳܢܳܐ ܡܶܣܬ݁ܰܪܗܰܒ ܐܰܢ̱ܬ ܥܰܠ ܡܶܕܶܡ ܕܠܳܐ ܡܦܰܣ ܐܰܢ̱ܬ܆ ܐܰܝܟ݁ܰܢܳܐ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܕܠܳܐ ܛܠܺܝܬ ܠܰܒ̈ܢܰܝ ܐ̱ܢܳܫܳܐ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܩܳܝܡܝܺܢ ܘܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܰܥܒܰܪܘ: ܗܳܟ݁ܰܢܳܐ ܝܳܕܰܥ ܐ̱ܢܳܐ ܠܰܐܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܰܥܬܺܝܕܝܺܢ ܘܠܰܐܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܐܳܬܶܝܢ. ܡܶܛܽܠ ܕܟܰܕ݂ ܚܛܳܐ ܐܳܕܳܡ ܘܐܶܬܓܙܰܪ ܡܰܘܬܳܐ ܥܰܠ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܡܶܬܝܰܠܕ݁ܝܺܢ: ܗܳܝܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܐܶܬܡܢܺܝ ܣܘܽܓܐܗܘ̇ܢ ܕܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܡܶܬܝܰܠܕܝܺܢ. ܘܠܶܗ ܠܘܶܒܢܝܳܢܳܐ ܗܰܘ ܐܶܬܥܰܬܰܕ ܐܰܬ݁ܪܳܐ ܐܰܝܟܳܐ ܕܐܶܥܡܪܘܽܢ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܚܰܝ̈ܝܢ ܘܐܰܝܟ݁ܳܐ ܕܢܶܬܢܰܛܪܘܽܢ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܡܝܺܬܝܺܢ. ܐܶܠܳܐ ܘܳܠܶܐ
XXIII. And I answered and said: No, O Lord, my Master. And he answered and said to me: Why then are you troubled about that which you do not know, and why are you disturbed about those things of which you are not persuaded? For just as I have not forgotten the men who now are, and those who have passed away: so I remember those who are to come. For when Adam sinned and death was decreed against those who were to be born, then the multitude of those who were to be born was numbered, and for that number a place was prepared where the living would dwell and where the dead would be guarded. Unless, therefore, the aforementioned number is completed,


[p. 1107] {#po.0.p1107}
ܠܳܐ ܬܶܚܝܶܐ ܒܪܝܺܬ݁ܳܐ ܒܪܝܺܬ݁ܳܐ. ܪܘܽܚܝ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܒܳܪܝܳܐ ܚܰܝ̈ܶܐ: ܘܰܟܝܽܘܠ ܡܩܰܒܠܳܐ ܡܝܺ̈ܬܶܐ. ܘܬܽܘܒ ܐܝܺܬ ܠܳܟ݂ ܕܐܶܫܡܰܥ ܡܶܕ݁ܶܡ ܕܰܥܬܝܰܕ݂ ܕܢܺܐܬ݂ܶܐ ܡܶܢ ܒܳܬܰܪ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܙܰܒ݂̈ܢܶܐ. ܫܰܪܝܺܪܳܐܝܺܬ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܩܰܪܝܺܒ ܗ̱ܘ ܦܘܽܪܩܳܢܝ ܕܢܺܐܬܶܐ:
the creature will not live; for my spirit creates life, and Sheol receives the dead. And again, it is yours to hear what is to be, so that it may come after these times. For truly my redemption is near to come, and it is not far off, as it was of old.


[p. 1108] {#po.0.p1108}
ܘܠܳܐ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܪܰܚܝܺܩ ܐܰܟ ܕ݁ܡܶܢ ܩܕ݂ܺܝܡ ܀ ܗܳܐ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܝܰܘ̈ܡܳܬ݂ܳܐ ܐܳܬܶܝܢ ܘܢܶܬܓܠܘܽܢ ܣܶܦܪ̈ܶܐ: ܐܰܝܟܳܐ ܕܰܟܬܝܺܒܝܺܢ ܚܺܛܳܝܗܶܝܢ ܕܟܘܽܠܗܘܿܢ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܰܚܛܰܝܘ: ܘܬܽܘܒ ܐܳܦ ܐܰܘܨܪ̈ܶܐ ܐܰܝܟܳܐ ܕܙܰܕܝܺܩܘܽܬܗܘܽܢ ܕܟܠܗܘܽܢ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܐܶܙܕܰܕ݁ܰܩܝܘ ܒܰܒܪܝܺܬܳܐ ܐܶܬܟܰܢܫܰܬ܂ ܘܢܶܗܘܶܐ ܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܒܝܰܘܡܳܢܳܝܐ ܬܶܚܙܶܐ ܐܰܢ̱ܬ ܘܣܰܓܝ̈ܶܐܐ ܕܥܰܡܺܝܟ ܢܰܓ݁ܝܳܪܘܽܬ ܪܘܽܚܶܗ ܕܪܳܡܳܐ. ܗܳܝ ܕܰܒܟ݂ܽܠ ܕܳܪ ܘܕܳܪ ܕܡܰܡܓܰܪ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܪܘܽܚܶܗ ܥܰܠ ܟܽܠܝܗܘ̇ܢ ܝܰܠܝܺܕ݁ܶܐ ܕ݁ܚܳܛܶܝܢ ܘܡܶܙܕܰܕܩܺܝܢ. ܘܰܐܢܳܐ ܐܶܡܪܶܬ ܘܐܽܡܝܪܶܐ: ܐܶܠܳܐ ܗܳܐ ܡܳܪܝܳܐ ܐ̱ܢܳܫ ܠܳܐ ܝܳܕ݂ܰܥ ܡܶܢܝܳܢܳܐ ܕܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܰܥܒܰܪ̈ܝ ܘܐܳܦ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܰܥܬܝܺܕܳܢ. ܗܳܐ ܓܶܝܪ ܐܳܦ݂ ܐܶܢܳܐ ܡܶܕܶܡ ܕܓܰܕ݁ܰܢܝ
XXIV. For behold, the days are coming and the books will be opened, in which are written the sins of all those who have sinned, and again also the treasuries, in which the righteousness of those who have been justified in the creation has been collected. It will be, however, at that time, you and many who are with you will see the long-suffering of the Most High, which was in every generation and generation; who was long-suffering toward all those born who sinned and were justified. And I answered and said: But behold, O Lord, no one knows the number of the things that have passed, nor of those that are to be. For behold, I also know what has happened to us; but what is to be for our enemies, I do not know, and when you are going to visit your works.
ܝܳܕ݂ܰܥ ܐ̱ܢܳܐ: ܡܶܕܶܡ ܕܝܢ ܕܰܥܬܝܰܕ݂ ܕܢܶܗܘܶܐ ܠܣܳܢܺܐܺܝܢ ܠܳܐ ܝܳܕ݂ܰܥ ܐ̱ܢܳܐ. ܘܐܶܡܰܬܝ ܦܳܩܕ݂ܰܐ ܥܒܳܕܰܝܟ܀ ܘܰܥܢܳܐ ܘܐܶܡܰܪ ܠܺܝ: ܐܳܦ ܐܰܢ̱ܬ݀ ܐܶܬܢܛܰܪ ܥܕ݂ܰܡܳܐ ܠܗܰܘ ܙܰܒ݂ܢܳܐ ܠܳܐܬ݂ܳܐ ܗܳܝ ܕܰܥܬܝܰܕ݂ ܡܪܰܝܡܳܐ ܕܢܶܥܒܶܕ݂ ܠܥܳܡܘܽܪܶܝܗ ܕܐܰܪܥܳܐ ܠܚܰܪܬ݁ܳܐ ܕ݁ܚܰܘ̈ܡܳܬܳܐ. ܗܳܢܳܐ ܗܰܟܺܝܠ ܐܳܬ݁ܳܐ ܗܽܘ ܟܰܕ݂ ܬܶܐܚܘܽܕ݂ ܬܰܘܗܳܐ ܠܥܳܡܘܽܪܶܝܗ ܕܐܰܪܥܳܐ: ܘܢܶܦܠܘܽܢ ܒܐܘܽܠܨܳܢܶܐ ܣܰܓܝ̈ܶܐܐ ܘܬܽܘܒ ܢܶܦܠܘܽܢ ܒܩܽܘܫܝܳܬܶܐ ܪܰܘܪ̈ܒܳܬܳܐ. ܘܢܶܗܘܶܐ ܟܰܕ݂ ܢܺܐܡܪܘܽܢ ܒܡܰܚܫ̈ܒܳܬܗܘܽܢ ܡܶܢ ܐܘܽܠܨܳܢܗܘܽܢ ܣܰܓܝܐܳܐ: ܠܳܐ ܡܶܬܕܟܰܪ ܬܘܒ ܚܰܝܠܬ݁ܳܢܳܐ ܕܰܐܪܥܳܐ: ܘܢܶܗܘܶܐ ܟܰܕ݂ ܢܶܦܣܩܘܽܢ ܣܰܒܪܳܐ: ܗܳܝܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܢܶܬܬܥܺܝܪ
XXV. And he answered and said to me: You also shall be reserved until that time for this sign, which the Most High is about to do for the inhabitants of the earth at the end of the days. This, therefore, shall be the sign, when stupor takes hold of the inhabitants of the earth and they fall into great tribulations and fall into great torments. And it shall be, when they say in their thoughts because of their great tribulation: The Mighty One does not remember the earth anymore; and it shall be, when they despair, then the time will awaken.


[p. 1109] {#po.0.p1109}
ܙܰܒܢܳܐ܀ ܘܰܥܢܺܝܬ ܘܐܶܡܪܶܬ: ܙܰܒ݂ܢܐ ܟ݁ܰܝ ܣܰܓܺܝܐܬܳܐ ܡܩܰܘܝܳܐ ܐܘܽܠܨܳܢܳܐ ܗܰܘ ܕܗܳܘܶܐ܆ ܫܢܺܝܢ ܣܰܓܝ̈ܳܐܬ݁ܳܐ ܐܳܚܕ݂ܳܐ ܐܰܢܰܢܩܺܐ ܗܳܝ ܆
XXVI. And I answered and said: Will the tribulation that is to be last for a long time, and will that necessity encompass many years?


[p. 1110] {#po.0.p1110}
ܘܰܥܢܳܐ ܘܐܶܡܰܪ ܠܺܝ: ܠܬܰܪܬ݁ܰܥܰܣܪ݁ܶܐ ܡܢ̈ܰܘܳܢ ܐܶܬ݂ܦܰܠܰܓ݂ ܙܰܒܢܳܐ ܗܰܘ: ܘܟܠ ܚܕ݂ܳܐ ܚܕ݂ܳܐ
XXVII. And he answered and said to me: That time is divided into twelve parts, and each one is reserved for that which is appointed for it.
ܡܬܰܪܚܡܳܐ ܠܳܗ܀ ܒܰܡܢܳܬ݁ܳܐ ܩܰܕ݂ܡܳܝܬ݁ܳܐ: ܪܝܺܫܳܐ ܕܙܰܘ̈ܥܶܐ ܗܳܘܶܐ܀ ܘܒܰܡܢܳܬ݁ܳܐ ܕܬܰܪܬ݁ܶܝܢ: ܩܶܛܠܳܐ ܕܪܰܘܪ̈ܒܳܢܶܐ܀ ܘܒܰܡܢܳܬܳܐ ܕܰܬܠܳܬ: ܡܰܦܘܽܠܬ݁ܳܐ ܕܣܰܓܝ̈ܶܐܐ ܒ݁ܡܰܘܬܳܐ ܀ ܘܒܰܡܢ̈ܳܬܳܐ ܕܐܰܪܒܰܥ: ܦܘܽܕܳܪܳܐ ܕܚܰܪܒܳܐ܀ ܘܒܰܡܢ̈ܳܬܳܐ ܕܚܰܡܫ: ܟܰܦܢܳܐ ܘܟ݂ܶܠܝܳܢܳܐ ܕܡܶܛܪܳܐ܀ ܘܒܰܡܢ̈ܳܬ݁ܳܐ ܕܫܶܬ: ܙܰܘ̈ܥܶܐ ܘܓܘܽܢ̈ܚܶܐ܀
2 In the first part there will be the beginning of commotions. 3 In the second part, the killings of the great ones. 4 And in the third part, the fall of many into death. 5 And in the fourth part, the sending forth of the sword. 6 And in the fifth part, famine and the withholding of rain. 7 And in the sixth part, earthquakes and fissures. 9 And in the eighth part, a multitude of phantoms and the encounters of demons.


[p. 1111] {#po.0.p1111}
ܘܒܰܡܢܳܬ݁ܳܐ ܕܰܬܡܳܢܶܐ: ܣܘܽܓܳܐܐ ܕܦܰܢܛܰܣܳܝ̈ܶܐ ܘܦܰܓ݂ܥܶܐ ܕܫܺܐܕ̈ܶܐ܀ ܘܒܰܡܢܳܬ݁ܳܐ ܕܰܬܫ̈ܰܥ: ܢܦܳܠܳܐ ܕܢܘܽܪܶܐ܀ ܘܒܰܡܢܳܬܳܐ ܕ݁ܰܥܣܰܪ: ܚܛܘܽܦܝܳܐ ܘܰܛܠܘܽܡܝܳܐ
10 And in the ninth part, the fall of fire. 11 And in the tenth part, rapine and much oppression. 12 And in the eleventh part, iniquity and
ܘܒܰܡܢܳܬ݁ܳܐ ܕ݁ܰܚܕ݂ܰܥܶܣܪ̈ܶܐ: ܥܰܘܠܳܐ ܘܰܦܪܝܺܚܘܽܬܳܐ܀ ܘܒܰܡܢܳܬܳܐ ܕܐܰܬܪܰܥܶܣܪ̈ܶܐ: ܒܘܽܠܒܳܠܳܐ ܕ݁ܰܡܩܰܕ݁̈ܡܳܢ ܐܶܬ݂ܡܰܠܰܠ܀ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܡܢ̈ܰܘܳܬ݁ܶܗ ܕܗܰܘ ܙܰܒܢܳܐ ܢܛܝܰܪ̈ܳܢ
lust. 13 And in the twelfth part, the confusion of the mixing of all that has been said before. 14 These parts of that time are reserved and will be mixed one with another and will minister to one another.


[p. 1112] {#po.0.p1112}
ܘܗܳܘ̈ܝܳܢ ܡܚܰܠܺܛܳܢ ܚܕ݂ܳܐ ܒ݁ܰܚܕ݂ܳܐ ܘܰܚܕ݂ܳܐ ܠܚܕ݂ܳܐ ܡܫܰܡ̈ܫܳܢ܀ ܡܶܢܗܶܝܢ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܡܰܘܬܪܳܢ ܡܶܢ ܕܝܽܠܗܶܝܢ ܘܡܶܢ ܕܰܐܚܪ̈ܳܢܝܳܬܳܐ ܢܳܣܒ݂ܳܢ ܘܡܶܢܗܶܝܢ ܕܝܽܠܗܶܝܢ ܘܕܰܐܚܪ̈ܳܢܝܳܬ݁ܳܐ ܰܡܫܰܠܡܳܢ ܐܰܝܟ ܕܠܳܐ ܢܶܣܬܰܟܠܘܽܢ ܐܝܠܝܢ ܕ݂ܗܳܘܶܝܢ ܥܰܠ ܐ݈ܪܥܳܐ: ܒܗܳܢܘܽܢ ܝܰܘ̈ܡܳܬܳܐ ܕܫܘܽܠܳܡܳܐ ܗ̱ܘ ܕܙܰܒܢܶܐ܀
15 For some of them will leave from themselves and will take from their own and others, and some will complete their own and those of others, so that those who will be upon the earth in those days will not understand that the end of the times is at hand.


[p. 1113] {#po.0.p1113}
ܒܪܰܡ ܕܝܢ ܟ݁ܺܝܠ ܕܢܺܝܣܬ݁ܰܟ݁ܰܠ ܗܳܝܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܢܶܬܚܰܟ݁ܰܡ܂ ܡܫܘܽܚܬܶܗ ܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܘܡܶܢܝܳܢܶܗ ܕܗܰܘ ܙܰܒܢܳܐ ܗܳܘ̈ܝܳܢ ܬ݁ܰܪܬ݁ܶܝܢ ܡܢܰܘܳܬܳܐ
XXVIII. Nevertheless, everyone who pays attention will then be wise. The measure and calculation of that time will be two parts: seven weeks of weeks. And I answered and said: It is good for a man to arrive and see,
ܘܐܶܡܪܶܬ: ܛܲܝܒ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܕܐ̱ܢܳܫ ܢܶܡܛܶܐ ܘܢܶܚܙܶܐ: ܘܛܳܒ ܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܡܶܢ ܗܳܕ݂ܶܐ ܕܠܳܐ ܢܶܡܛܶܐ ܕܠܳܐ ܢܶܦ݁ܶܠ. ܐܶܠܳܐ ܐܶܡܰܪ ܐܳܦ ܗܳܕܶܐ: ܢܫܘܽܛ ܗܰܘ ܕܠܳܐ ܡܶܬܚܰܒܰܠ ܠܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܕܡܶܬܚܰܒ݁ܠܳܢ ܘܰܠܡܶܕ݁ܶܡ ܕܓܳܕܼܰܫ ܒܗܶܝܢ ܒܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܕܡܶܬܚܰܒ̈ܠܳܢ: ܕܒܰܠܚܘܽܕ݂ ܠܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܕܠܳܐ ܡܶܬܚܰܒ݁ܠܳܢ ܢܶܚܘܽܪܢ ܐܰܢ ܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܡܳܪܝ ܫܰܪܝܺܪܳܐܝܺܬ ܥܬܝܺܕ݂ܳܢ ܐܶܢܶܝܢ ܕܢܺܐܬ݂ܢܳܢ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܕܩܰܕܡܰܬ ܐܶܡܰܪܬ݁ ܠܝܺ: ܐܶܢܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܐܶܫܟܰܚܬ ܪ̈ܰܚܡܶܐ ܒܥܰܝܢܰܝܟ: ܐܰܘܕ݁ܰܥܰܝܢܝ ܐܳܦ݂ ܗܳܕܶܐ:
but it is better than this not to arrive, lest he fall. But I will also say this: Will the incorruptible despise corruptible things and that which happens to corruptible [things] so that it may consider only incorruptible things? But if, O Lord, it is truly to be that those things which you have foretold to me will come, if indeed I have found grace in your eyes, show me also this: whether these things are to be in one place or in one of the parts of the earth, or whether the whole earth is to perceive it?


[p. 1114] {#po.0.p1114}
ܐܶܢ ܗܘܽ ܕܰܒܚܰܕ݂ ܐܰܬܪܳܐ ܐܰܘ ܒܰܚܕ݂ܳܐ ܡܶܢ ܡܢܰܘܳܬܳܗ ܕܰܐܪܥܳܐ ܥܬܝܺܕ݂ܳܢ ܕܢܶܗܘ̈ܝܳܢ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܐܰܘ ܟ݁ܽܠܳܗ ܐܰܪܥܳܐ ܡܰܪܓ݁ܫܳܐ ܗ̱ܝ. ܘܰܥܢܳܐ ܘܐܶܡܰܪ ܠܝܺ: ܕܟܘܽܠܳܝܗ ܗ̱ܘܽ ܐܰܪܥܳܐ ܡܶܕܶܡ ܕܓܳܕ̇ܫ ܗܳܝܕܶܝܢ. ܡܶܛܽܠ ܗܳܢܐ ܟ݁ܽܠܗܘܽܢ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܚܰܝ̈ܝܺܢ ܢܰܪܓܫܘܽܢ. ܒܗܰܘ ܕܶܝܢ ܙܰܒܢܳܐ ܡܰܡܓ݂ܶܢ ܐ̱ܢܳܐ ܒܰܠܚܘܽܕ݂ ܥܰܠ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܡܶܫܬܰܟ݂ܚܺܝܢ ܒܗܘܢ ܒܗܳܢܘܽܢ ܝܰܘ̈ܡܳܬܳܐ ܒ݁ܗܳܕ݂ܶܐ ܐܰܪܥܳܐ. ܘܢܶܗܘܶܐ ܡܶܢ ܒܳܬܰܪ ܕܫܳܠܶܡ܂ ܡܶܕܶܡ ܕܰܥܬܝܺܕ݂ ܕ݁ܢܶܗܘܶܐ ܒܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܡܢܰܘܳܬܳܐ: ܗܳܝܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܢܫܰܪܶܐ ܕܢܶܬܓܠܶܐ ܡܫܝܺܚܳܐ. ܘܢܶܬܓ݁ܠܶܐ ܒܶܗܡܘܽܬ ܡܶܢ ܐܰܬܪܶܗ ܘܠܶܘܝܳܬ݂ܳܢ ܢܶܣܰܩ ܡܶܢ ܝܰܡܳܐ: ܬܪ̈ܰܝܗܘܽܢ ܐܰܢܝ̈ܺܢܶܐ ܪܰܘܪ̈ܒ݁ܶܐ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܕܰܒܪܝܺܬ ܒ݁ܝܰܘܡܳܐ ܚܡܝܺܫܳܝܳܐ ܕܰܒܪܝܺܬܳܐ: ܘܢܶܛܪܶܬ ܐܶܢܘܽܢ ܥܕ݂ܰܡܳܐ ܠܗܰܘ ܙܰܒܢܳܐ: ܘܗܳܝܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܢܶܗܘܘܽܢ ܠܡܐܟܘܽܠܬܳܐ ܠܟܘܽܠܗܘܽܢ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܡܶܫܬܰܚܪܝܺܢ ܐܳܦ ܐܰܪܥ݁ܳܐ ܬܶܬܶܠ ܦܺܐܪܶܝܗ ܚܰܕ݂ ܒܪܶܒܘܽ: ܘܰܚܕ݂ܳܐ ܓ݁ܦܶܬܳܐ ܢܶܗܘܝܳܢ ܒܳܗ ܐܳܠܶܦ ܫܒܰܫ̈ܬܳܐ: ܘܰܚܕ݂ܳܐ ܫܒܶܫܬ݁ܳܐ ܬ݁ܶܥܒܺܝܕ݂ ܐܳܠܶܦ݂ ܣܓܘ̈ܽܠܝܢ: ܘܚܰܕ݂ ܣܓܘܿܠܵܐ ܢܶܥܒܶܕ݂ ܐܳܠܶܦ ܥܶܢ̈ܒܳܬܳܐ ܘܰܚܕ݂ܳܐ ܥܶܢܒܬ݁ܳܐ ܬ݁ܶܥܒ݁ܶܕ݂ ܟܘܽܪܳܐ ܕܚܰܡܪܳܐ. ܘܐܺܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܰܟܦܶܢܘ
XXIX. And he answered and said to me: That which will happen then will be of the whole earth. Therefore, all who live will perceive it. At that time, however, I will protect only those who are found in those days in this land. And it shall be, after that which is to be in those parts has been completed, then the Messiah will begin to be revealed. And Behemoth will be revealed from his place, and Leviathan will ascend from the sea, two great monsters which I created on the fifth day of creation and reserved until that time; and then they will be for food for all who are left. Also, the earth will give its fruits, one in ten thousand; and on one vine there will be a thousand branches, and one branch will produce a thousand clusters, and one cluster will produce a thousand grapes, and one grape will produce a cor of wine. And those who have hungered will rejoice, and again


[p. 1115] {#po.0.p1115}


[p. 1116] {#po.0.p1116}
ܢܶܬܒܰܣܡܘܽܢ: ܘܐܳܦ ܕ݁ܶܝܢܿ ܢܶܚܙܘܽܢ ܬ݁ܶܕ݂ܡܬ݁ܳܬܳܐ ܒܟܽܠܝܽܘܡ. ܪ̈ܘܽܚܶܐ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܢܶܦ݁ܩܳܢ ܡܶܢ ܩܕ݂ܳܡܰܝ ܠܡܰܝܬܳܝܘܽ ܒܟ݁ܽܠ ܨܦܰܪ ܘܰܨܦܰܪ ܪܺܝܚܳܐ ܕܦܺܐܪ̈ܶܐ ܕܒܶܣ̈ܡܳܢܶܐ: ܘܰܒܢܘܽܠܳܝܶܗ ܕ݁ܝܰܘܡܳܐ ܥܢܳܢ̈ܶܐ ܕܪܳܣܳܢ ܛܰܝܠܳܐ ܕܐܳܣܝܘܽܬܳܐ. ܘܢܶܗܘܶܐ ܒܶܗ ܒܗܰܘ ܙܰܒ݂ܢܳܐ ܢܶܚܬܶܬ ܬܘܒ ܡܶܢ ܠܥܶܠ ܐܰܘܨܪܶܐ ܕܡܰܢܢܳܐ ܘܢܶܐܟܠܘܽܢ ܡܶܢܶܗ
they will see wonders daily. For spirits will go forth from my presence to bring every morning the fragrance of aromatic fruits, and at the end of the day, clouds dropping the dew of health. And it shall be at that time, the treasury of manna will descend again from above, and they will eat of it in those years, since they are the ones who have arrived at the end of the time.
ܒܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܫܢܰ̈ܝܳܐ: ܡܶܛܽܠ ܕܗܶܢܘܽܢ ܐܶܢܘܽܢ ܕ݁ܡܰܛܝܽܘ ܠܫܘܽܠܳܡܶܗ ܕܙܰܒܢܳܐ܀ ܘܢܶܗܘܶܐ ܡܶܢ ܒܳܬܰܪ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ: ܐܶܡܰܬܝ ܕܢܶܬܡܠܶܐ ܙܰܒܢܳܐ ܕܡܶܐܬ݂ܝܺܬ݁ܶܗ ܕܰܡܫܺܝܚܐ ܘܢܶܗܦܘܽܟ݂ ܒܬܶܫܒܘܽܚܬܳܐ: ܗܳܝܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܟܘܽܠܗܘܽܢ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕ݁ܰܕ݂ܡܶܟ݂ܘ ܒܣܰܒܪܶܗ ܩܳܝܡܝܿܢ. ܘܢܶܗܘܶܐ ܒܗܰܘ ܙܰܒ݂ܢܳܐ ܢܶܬܦܰܬܚܘܽܢ ܐܰܘܨܪ̈ܶܐ ܗܳܢܘܢ ܕܰܒܗܘܽܢ ܐܶܬܢܛܰܪ ܡܶܢܝܳܢܳܐ ܕܢܰܦ݁̈ܫܳܬ݁ܳܐ ܕܙܰܕ̈ܝ̈ܩܶܐ ܘܢܶܦ݁ܩܳܢ ܘܢܶܬܚܙܶܐ ܣܝܘܽܓܳܝܗܶܝܢ ܕܢܰܦ݁̈ܫܳܬ݂ܳܐ ܐܰܟܚܕ݂ܳܐ ܒܚܰܕ݂ ܟܶܢܫܳܐ ܕ݁ܰܚܕ݂ܳܐ ܬܰܪܥܝܳܬܳܐ. ܘܢܶܚܕ݁̈ܝܳܢ ܩܰܕ݁̈ܡܳܝܳܬܳܐ: ܘܰܐܚܪ̈ܳܝܳܬ݁ܳܐ ܠܳܐ ܢܶܬܬܥܢّܶܩܳܢ. ܝܳܕ݂ܥܳܢ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܕܰܡܛܳܝܐ ܙܰܒܢܳܐ ܕܐܶܬܡܰܪ ܥܠܰܘܗ̱ܝ ܕܫܘܽܠܳܡܗܘܽܢ ܕܙܰܒ̈ܢܳܐ. ܢܰܦ݁̈ܫܳܬܗܘܽܢ ܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܕܪ̈ܰܫܝ̈ܥܶܐ: ܟ݁ܰܕ݂ ܢܶܚܙܝܳܢ ܟ݁ܽܠܗܶܝܢ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ: ܗܳܝܕ݁ܶܝܢ
XXX. And it shall be after these things, when the time of the advent of the Messiah is fulfilled and he returns in glory, then all who have slept in hope of him will rise again. And it shall be at that time, the storehouses in which the number of the souls of the righteous was guarded will be opened, and they will go forth, and the multitude of souls will appear together in one assembly, of one mind, and the former will rejoice, and the latter will not be saddened. For they know that the time has come, of which it was said that it is the end of the times. The souls of the impious, however, when they see all these things, will then waste away all the more. For they know that their punishment has come and their destruction has arrived.


[p. 1117] {#po.0.p1117}
ܝܰܬܝܰܪܳܐܝܺܬ ܢܳܚ̈ܬܳܢ: ܚܳܕ݁ܥܳܢ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܕܰܡܛܳܝܐ ܬܰܫܢܝܺܩܗܘܽܢ ܘܐܶܬ݂ܳܐ ܐܰܒܕܳܢܗܘܽܢ܀ ܘܰܗܘܳܐ ܡܶܢ ܒܳܬܰܪ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ: ܐܶܙܰܠ̱ܬ ܐܶܢܳܐ ܠܘܳܬ ܥܰܡܳܝܐ ܘܐܶܡܰܪܬ ܠܗܘܽܢ: ܟܰܢܶܫܘ ܠܝܺ ܟܘܽܠܗܘܽܢ ܣܳܒܰܝ̈ܟܘܽܢ ܘܐܶܡܰܪ ܠܟܘܢ ܡܶܠܶܐ. ܘܐܶܬܟܰܢܰܫܘ ܟܘܽܠܗܘܽܢ ܠܢܰܚܠܳܐ ܕܩܶܕܪܘܽܢ. ܘܰܥܢܺܝܬ ܘܐܶܡܰܪܬ ܠܗܘܿܢ: ܫܡܰܥ ܐܝܺܣܪܳܝܶܠ ܘܐܳܡܰܪ ܠܳܟ: ܘܐܵܢ̱ܬ ܙܰܪܥܶܗ ܕܝܰܥܩܘܽܒ ܨܘܽܬ ܘܐܰܪܕܶܝܟ. ܠܳܐ ܐܶܛܥܘܽܢ ܠܨܲܗܝܘܽܢ ܐܶܠܳܐ ܗܘܰܝܬܘܽܢ ܥܰܗܝܺܕ݂ܝܺܢ ܠܥܳܩ̈ܳܬܳܗ ܕܐܘܿܪܺܫܠܶܡ. ܗܳܐ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܝܰܘ̈ܡܳܬܳܐ ܐܳܬ݂ܶܝܢ ܘܢܶܬܢܣܶܝܒ ܠܰܚܒܳܠܳܐ
XXXI. And it came to pass after these things, I went to the people and said to them: Gather for me all your elders, and I will speak words to you. And they were all gathered in the valley of Kidron. And I answered and said to them: Hear, O Israel, and I will speak to you; and you, seed of Jacob, listen, and I will instruct you. Do not forget Zion, but be mindful of the anguish of Jerusalem. For behold, the days are coming and everything that has been will be taken into corruption, and it will be as if it had not been.


[p. 1118] {#po.0.p1118}
ܟܽܠ ܥܺܒܕ݁ܶܡ ܕܰܗܘܳܐ: ܘܢܶܙܘܶܐ ܐܝܟ ܕܠܳܐ ܗܘܳܐ܀ ܐܰܬܘܽܢ ܕ̈ܝܢ: ܐܶܢ ܡܥܰܬܕܝܺܢ ܐܵܢ̱ܬܘܽܢ ܠܒܰܫܟܘܿܢ ܕܬܶܙܪܥܘܽܢ ܒܗܶܝܢ ܦܺܐܪ̈ܰܘܗ̱ܝ ܕܢܳܡܘ̈ܣܳܝܐ: ܡܰܓ݁ܶܢ ܥܠܰܝܟܘܽܢ ܒܗܰܘ ܙܰܒ݂ܢܳܐ ܕܰܥܬܝܰܕ݂ ܚܰܝܠܬ݁ܳܢܳܐ ܕܰܢܙܝܰܥ ܠܟܽܠܳܗ ܒܪܝܺܬܳܐ. ܡܶܛܽܠ ܕ݁ܡܶܢ ܒܳܬܰܪ ܩܰܠܺܝܠ ܙܰܒܢܳܐ ܢܶܬܬܙܝܰܥ ܒܶܢܝܳܢܶܗ ܕܨܶܗܝܘܽܢ ܕܢܶܬܒܢܶܐ ܬܘܽܒ: ܐܶܠܳܐ ܠܳܐ ܡܩܰܘܶܐ ܗܽܘ ܗܰܘ ܒܶܢܝܳܢܳܐ. ܐܶܠܳܐ ܘܬܽܘܒ ܡܶܢ ܒܳܬܰܪ ܙܰܒܢܳܐ ܡܶܬܥܩܰܪ ܘܰܡܩܰܘܝܳܐ ܟܰܕ ܚܰܪܒ݁ܳܐ ܥܕ݂ܰܡܳܐ ܠܙܰܒ݂ܢܳܐ. ܘܡܶܢ ܒܳܬ݁ܰܪܟ݁ܶܢ ܡܶܬܒ݁ܰܥܝܳܐ ܠܰܡܬܚܰܕ݂ܳܬܘܽ ܒܬܶܫܒܘܽܚܬ݁ܳܐ ܘܡܶܫܬܰܟܠܠܳܐ ܠܥܳܠܰܡ܂ ܠܳܐ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܡܶܬܒܰܥܝܳܐ ܠܰܢ ܕܢܶܬܬܥܝّܩ ܗܳܢܳܐ ܟ݁ܽܠܶܗ ܥܰܠ ܒ݁ܺܝܫܬ݁ܳܐ ܕܐܶܬܳܬ ܗܳܟܳܐ ܐܰܟ ܕܥܰܠ ܡܶܕܶܡ ܕܰܥܬܝܺܕ݂. ܡܶܢ ܬܰܪ̈ܬܶܝܢ ܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܥܳܩ̈ܳܬ݁ܳܐ ܪܰܒ ܗ̱ܘܵ ܐܰܓܘܳܢܳܐ: ܐܶܡܰܬܝ ܕܰܥܬܝܰܕ݂ ܚܰܝܠܬ݁ܳܢܳܐ ܕܰܢܚܰܕܶܬ ܒܶܪ̈ܝܳܬܶܗ. ܘܗܳܫܳܐ ܠܳܐ ܐܶܩܰܪܒܘܽܢ ܠܝܺ ܒܕ݂ܰܡܳܝܐ ܠܩܰܠܺܝܠ
XXXII. You, however, if you prepare your hearts to sow in them the fruits of the law, he will protect you at that time when the Mighty One is about to shake all creation. [For after a little time the building of Zion will be shaken, so that it may be built again. But that building itself will not remain; but again after a time it will be uprooted and will remain desolate until a time. And afterwards it must be renewed in glory and will be perfected forever.] We ought not, therefore, to be so greatly saddened by the evil that has now come, as by that which is to be. But of the two tribulations, the struggle will be greater when the Mighty One is about to renew his creation. And now, do not approach me for a few days, nor visit


[p. 1119] {#po.0.p1119}


[p. 1120] {#po.0.p1120}
ܝܰܘ̈ܡܳܬ݂ܳܐ: ܘܠܳܐ ܬܶܦܩܕܘܽܢܳܢܝ ܒܕܰܡܳܝܐ ܕܐܺܬܶܐ ܠܘܳܬܟܘܽܢ. ܘܰܗܘܳܐ ܕܟܰܕ݂ ܐܶܡܪܶܬ ܠܗܘܽܢ ܟ݁ܽܠܗܶܝܢ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܡܶܠܶܐ: ܐܶܙܰܠ݂ܬ ܐܶܢܳܐ ܒܰܪܘܽܟ݂ ܠܐܘܪ̈ܚܝ. ܘܟܰܕ݂ ܚܙܳܢܝ ܥܰܡܳܐ ܕܐܳܙܶܠ ܐ̱ܢܳܐ: ܐܰܪܝܰܡܘ ܩܳܠܗܘܽܢ ܘܐܰܝܠܶܠܘ ܘܐܶܡܰܪܘ: ܠܰܐܝܟܳܐ ܐܳܙܶܠ ܐ݇ܢܸܬ ܠܳܟ ܡܶܢܰܢ ܒܰܪܘܽܟ݂: ܘܫܳܒܩܰܬ
me until I come to you. And it came to pass, when I had spoken all these words to them, I, Baruch, went on my way; and when the people saw me departing, they lifted up their voice and wailed and said: Where are you going from us, Baruch, and leaving us like a father who leaves his children orphans and departs from them?
ܠܰܢ ܐܰܝܟ ܐܰܒܳܐ ܕܫܳܒܶܩ ܠܰܒܢܰܘ̈ܗܝ ܝܰܬܡܶܐ ܘܳܐܙܶܠ ܡܶܢ ܠܘܳܬܗܘܽܢ܀ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܐܶܢܘܽܢ ܦܘܩܕ݁ܳܢܶܐ ܕܦܰܩܕ݂ܳܟ݂ ܚܰܒܪܳܟ ܐܶܪܰܡܝܳܐ ܢܒܺܝܫܐ ܘܐܶܡܰܪ ܠܳܟ: ܚܙܝܺ ܠܗܳܢܳܐ ܥܰܡܳܐ ܥܕ݂ܰܡܳܐ ܕܐܳܙܶܠ ܐ̱ܢܳܐ ܘܐܰܬܩܶܢ ܫܰܪܟ݁ܳܝܐ ܕܐܰܚܰܝܺܝܢ ܒܒܳܒܶܠ: ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܕܰܢܦ݁ܰܩ ܥܠܰܝܗܘܽܢ ܓ݁ܙܳܪ ܕܝܺܢܳܐ ܕܢܶܫܬܒܘܿܢ. ܘܗܳܫܳܐ ܐܶܢ ܗܘܽ ܕܐܳܦ ܐ݇ܢܸܬ ܫܳܒܩܰܬ ܠܰܢ: ܛܳܒ ܙܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܠܰܢ ܕܰܢܡܘܽܬ ܟܽܠܰܢ ܩܰܕ݂ܡܳܝ ܘܗܳܝܕ݁ܶܝܢ
XXXIII. Are these the commands which your companion, Jeremiah the prophet, commanded you, and said to you: Look after this people while I go and confirm the remaining brothers in Babylon, against whom the sentence has gone forth that they should be led into captivity? And now, if you also leave us, it is better for us that we all die first, and then you be taken from us.


[p. 1121] {#po.0.p1121}
ܬܪܚܶܩ ܡܶܢܰܢ܀ ܘܰܥܢܺܝܬ ܘܐܶܡܪܶܬ ܠܶܗ ܠܥܰܡܳܐ: ܚܳܣ ܠܺܝ ܕܐܶܫܒܘܽܩܟܘܽܢ ܐܰܘ ܕܐܰܪܚܶܩ ܡܶܢܟܘܽܢ: ܐܶܠܳܐ ܒܰܠܚܘܽܕ݂ ܐܺܙܰܠ ܥܕ݂ܰܡܳܐ ܠܒܶܝܬ ܩܘܽܕ̈ܫܶܐ ܩܘܽܕ̈ܫܶܐ ܕܐܶܒܥܶܐ ܡܶܢ ܚܰܝܠܬ݁ܳܢܳܐ ܥܠܰܝܟܘܽܢ ܘܥܰܠ ܨܶܗܝܘܽܢ: ܕܐܶܢ ܐܶܬܢܗܰܪ ܡܶܕܶܡ ܝܰܬܝܰܪ.
XXXIV. And I answered and said to the people: Far be it from me to leave you or to be taken from you, but I will only go to the Holy of Holies, that I may ask the Mighty One for you and for Zion, if perhaps I may be enlightened somewhat more; and afterwards I will return to you.
ܘܡܶܢ ܒܳܬܰܪ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܗܳܦܶܟ݂ ܐ̱ܢܳܐ ܠܘܳܬܟܘܽܢ܀ ܘܐܶܙܶܠ̱ܬ ܐܶܢܳܐ ܒܰܪܘܽܟ ܥܕ݂ܰܡܳܐ ܠܰܐܬܪܳܐ ܩܰܕ݂ܝܺܫܳܐ ܘܝܶܬܒܶܬ ܥܰܠ ܡܰܡܦ݁ܺܠܳܬ݂ܶܗ ܘܰܒܟܝܬ ܘܐܶܡܪܶܬ: ܐܳܘ̄ ܥܰܝܢܰܝ̈ ܗܘܳܝܶܝܢ ܡܰܒܒܺܘܽܥܶܐ: ܘܐܶܡܝ̈ܶܐ ܕܥܰܝܢܰܝ̈ ܡܥܝܢܳܐ ܕܕܶܡ̈ܥܳܐ, ܐܰܝܟ݁ܰܢܳܐ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܐܶܬܬܰܢܰܚ ܥܰܠ ܨܶܗܝܘܽܢ ܘܐܰܝܟܰܢܳܐ ܐܶܬܬܰܐܒܰܠ ܥܰܠ ܐܘܽܪܰܫܠܶܡܢ ܡܶܛܽܠ ܕ݁ܰܒ݂ܗܳܢܐ ܐܰܬܪܳܐ ܐܰܚܟ݁ܳܐ ܕܡܶܬܓ݂ܰܪܓ݁ܰܚ ܐ̱ܢܳܐ ܗܳܫܳܐ: ܡܶܢ ܩܕ݂ܝܳܡ ܪܰܒ ܟܳܗܢܶܐ ܡܩܰܪܶܒ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܩܘܽܪ̈ܒ݁ܳܢܶܐ ܩܰܕ̈ܝ̈ܫܶܐ ܘܣܳܐܶܡ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܒܗ ܥܶܛܪܳܐ ܕܒܶܣ̈ܡܶܐ ܕܪ̈ܝܺܚܳܢܶܐ ܒܰܣ̈ܝܺܡܶܐ.
XXXV. And I, Baruch, went to the holy place and sat upon its ruins and wept and said: O my eyes, be springs, and the eyelids of my eyes, a fountain of tears. For how shall I groan over Zion and how shall I lament over Jerusalem? Because in this place, where I am now prostrate, the High Priest once offered holy oblations and placed in it the smoke of the incense of sweet spices. Now, however, our glory has become dust, and the desire of our soul, sand.


[p. 1122] {#po.0.p1122}
ܗܳܫܳܐ ܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܐܶܬܥܒܶܕ݂ ܥܰܦܪܳܐ ܫܘܽܒܗܳܪܰܢ: ܘܪܶܓ݂ܬ݁ܳܐ ܕܢܰܦܫܰܢ ܚܶܠܳܐ܀ ܘܟܰܕ݂ ܐܶܡܪܶܬ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ: ܕܶܡܟܶܬ ܬ݁ܰܡܳܢ ܘܰܚܙܝܺܬ ܒܠܰܠܝܳܐ ܚܶܙܘܳܐ. ܘܗܳܐ ܥܳܒܳܐ ܕܐܝ̈ܠܳܢܶܐ ܕܰܢܨܝܺܒ ܒܰܦܩܰܥܬ݁ܳܐ ܘܰܚܕ݂ܝܰܪܝܺܢ ܗ̱ܘܰܘ ܠܶܗ ܛܘܽܪ̈ܶܐ ܪܳܡܶܐ ܘܰܩܫ̈ܰܝܳܐ ܕܰܦܩ̈ܝܺܥܶܐ. ܘܐܰܬܪܳܐ ܣܰܓ݁ܝܳܐܳܐ ܐܰܚܺܝܕ݂ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܗܰܘ ܥܳܒܳܐ. ܘܗܳܐ ܡܶܢ ܠܩܘܽܒ݁ܠܳܗ ܣܶܠܩܰܬ ܓ݁ܦܶܬܳܐ ܚܕ݂ܳܐ. ܘܡܶܢ ܬܚܶܝܬܳܝܗ ܢܳܦܳܩ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܡܰܒܘܽܥܳܐ ܒܰܢܚܳܚܳܝܐ. ܘܰܡܛܳܝܐ ܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܗܰܘ ܡܰܒܘܽܥܳܐ ܥܕ݂ܰܡܳܐ ܠܥܳܒܳܐ ܘܰܗܘܳܐ ܠܓܰܠ̱ܓܰܠܶܐ ܪܰܘܪ̈ܒ݂ܶܐ. ܘܐܰܛܚܺܦܘ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܓܰܠ̱ܓܰܠܶܐ ܠܗܰܘ ܥܳܒܳܐ ܘܡܶܢ ܫܶܠܝܳܐ ܥܩܰܪܘ ܠܣܘܽܓܐܶܗ ܕܥܳܒ݁ܳܐ ܗܰܘ ܘܰܣܚܰܦܘ ܠܟ݁ܽܠܗܘܽܢ ܛܘܪ̈ܶܐ ܕܰܚܕ݂ܳܪ̈ܰܘܗ̱ܝ. ܘܪܰܘܡܶܗ ܕܥܳܒܳܐ ܡܶܬܡܰܟ݁ܰܟ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ: ܘܪܶܝܫܰܝܗܘܽܢ ܕܛܘܽܪ̈ܐ ܐܶܬܡܰܟܰܟ. ܘܛܳܒ ܐܶܬܚܰܝܰܠ ܡܰܒܘܽܥܳܐ ܗܰܘ: ܕܠܳܐ ܢܶܫܒܘܽܩ ܡܶܕܶܡ ܡܶܢ ܗܰܘ ܥܳܒܳܐ ܣܰܓܝܳܐܳܐ: ܐܶܠܳܐ ܚܰܕ݂ ܐܰܪܙܳܐ ܒܰܠܚܘܽܕ݂. ܐܳܦ ܠܶܗ ܟ݁ܰܕ݂ ܐܰܪܡܝܺ ܘܰܠܣܘܽܓܶܐܗ ܕܥܳܒܳܐ ܐܰܘܒܶܕ݂ ܘܰܥܩܰܪ: ܕܡܶܕ݁ܶܡ ܠܳܐ ܢܶܫܬܒܶܩ ܡܶܢܶܗ: ܐܳܦ ܠܳܐ ܐܰܬ݂ܪܶܗ ܢܶܬܝܺܕ݂ܰܥ. ܗܝ ܕܝܢ ܗܳܝ ܓ݁ܦܶܬܳܐ ܐܳܬܝܳܐ ܣܰܓ݁ܝܳܐܳܐ: ܘܐܶܬܳܬ ܥܕ݂ܰܡܳܐ ܠܰܐܬ݂ܪܳܐ ܚܰܕ݂ ܕܠܳܐ ܪܰܚܺܝܩ
XXXVI. And when I had said these things, I fell asleep there and saw a vision in the night; and behold, a forest of trees planted in a plain, and high mountains and crags of rocks surrounded it, and the forest occupied much space; and behold, opposite it, one vine ascended, and from beneath it a fountain was going forth in tranquility; the fountain arrived at the forest and became great waves, and the waves flooded that forest and suddenly uprooted the multitude of that forest and overturned all the mountains in its circuit. And the height of the forest was humbled, and the summit of the mountains was humbled, and that fountain prevailed greatly, so that it did not leave anything of that great forest except one cedar only. Even this, when it had cast it down and had destroyed and uprooted the multitude of that forest, so that nothing was left of it, nor its place known, then that vine came with the fountain and much tranquility, and it came


[p. 1123] {#po.0.p1123}


[p. 1124] {#po.0.p1124}
ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܡܶܢ ܐܰܪܙܳܐ: ܘܰܠܰܐܪܙܳܐ ܟܰܕ݂ ܪܡܶܐ ܩܰܪܒܘܽܗ̱ܝ ܠܘܳܬܳܗ. ܘܰܚܙܺܝܬ ܘܗܳܐ ܦܶܬܚܰܬ ܦܘܺܡܳܗ ܓܦܳܬܳܐ ܗܳܝ ܘܡܰܠ̱ܠܰܬ݂ ܘܐܶܡܪܰܬ ܠܰܐܪܙܳܐ: ܠܳܐ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܐܰܢ̱ܬ ܗ̱ܘ ܐܰܪܙܳܐ ܕܐܶܫܬܚܰܪܬ ܡܶܢ ܥܳܒܳܐ ܕܒܝܺܫܬܳܐ܆ ܒܐܝܺܕ݂ܳܟ ܐܰܡܚܺܝܢܳܐ ܗ̱ܘܳܬ ܒܝܳܫܬܳܐ ܘܡܶܬܥܰܒܕ݂ܳܐ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܟܘܽܠܗܶܝܢ ܫܢ̈ܶܐ: ܘܛܳܝܒܘܽܬܳܐ ܠܳܐ ܡܶܡܬܘܽܡ܆ ܘܡܶܬܚܰܝܠܰܬ ܥܰܠ ܕܠܳܐ ܕܺܝܠܳܟ ܐܳܦ ܠܳܐ ܥܰܠ ܕܺܝܠܳܟ ܡܶܡܬܘܽܡ ܪܚܰܡܬ: ܘܡܳܬܰܚ ܗ̱ܘܰܝܬ ܐܘܺܚܕ݁ܳܢܳܟ ܥܰܠ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܪܰܚܺܝܩܺܝܢ ܡܶܢܳܟ: ܘܠܰܐܝܠܶܝܢ ܕ݁ܡܶܬܩܰܪܒ݁ܝܢ ܠܳܟ ܒܰܡܨܒܰܕ݂ܳܬ݁ܳܐ ܕܪܶܫܥܳܟ ܐܳܚܶܕ݂ ܗ̱ܘܰܝܬ: ܘܰܡܪܺܝܡ ܗ̱ܘܰܝܬ ܢܰܦܫܳܟ ܒܟܽܠ ܙܒܰܢ: ܐܰܝܟ ܗܰܘ ܕܠܳܐ ܡܶܫܟ݁ܰܚ ܕܢܶܬܥܩܰܪ. ܗܳܟܳܝܐ ܕܶܝܢ ܣܰܝܗܰܦ ܙܰܒܢܳܟ ܘܡܰܛܝܽ ܥܶܕ݁ܳܢܳܟ. ܙܶܠ ܗܳܩܺܝܠ ܐܳܦ ܐܰܢ̱ܬ ܐܰܪܙܳܐ ܒܳܬܰܪ ܥܳܒܳܐ ܕܐܶܙܰܠ ܩܕ݂ܳܡܰܝܟ ܘܰܗܘܺܝ ܥܰܡܶܗ ܚܳܠܳܐ ܘܥܰܦܪܟܘܽܢ
to a place which was not far from the cedar, and they brought the cedar that had been cast down to it. And I saw, and behold, that vine opened its mouth, and it spoke and said to that cedar: Are you not the cedar that was left from the forest of malice, and through your hands malice was constant and was perpetrated all these years, and goodness never, and you prevailed over that which was not yours, nor did you ever have pity on that which was yours, and you extended your power over those who were far from you, and those who were approaching you, you held in the nets of your impiety, and you always lifted up your soul, as if it could not be uprooted. Now, however, your time has hastened, and your hour has come. Go, therefore, you also, O cedar, after the forest that has gone before you, and become sand with it, and let your dust be mixed together. And sleep now in anguish and rest in torment, until your final time comes, when you will come again and be tormented more.
ܢܶܬܚܰܠܰܛ ܐܰܟܚܕܳܐ. ܘܰܕ݂ܡܰܟܘ ܗܳܫܳܐ ܒܥܳܝܩܬ݁ܳܐ ܘܰܟܠܰܘ ܒܬܰܫܢܝܺܩܳܐ ܥܕ݂ܰܡܳܐ ܕܳܐܬܶܐ ܙܰܒܢܳܟ ܐ̱ܚܪܳܝܳܐ ܕܒܶܗ ܬܘّܒ ܬ݁ܺܐܬ݁ܶܐ ܘܝܰܬܝܺܪܳܐܝܺܬ ܬܶܫܬܰܢܰܩ܀ ܘܡܶܢ ܒܳܬܰܪ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܚܙܺܝܬ݂ܶܗ ܠܗܰܘ ܐܰܪܙܳܐ ܟ݁ܰܕ݂ ܝܳܩܶܕ݂ ܘܠܰܓܦܶܬ݁ܳܐ ܟܰܕ݂ ܪܳܒܝܳܐ: ܗܺܝ ܘܟܽܠ ܕܰܚܕܳܪܶܝܗ ܗܘܳܐ ܦܩܰܥܬ݁ܳܐ ܕܡܰܠܝܳܐ ܗ̈ܰܒܳܒ̈ܶܐ
XXXVII. And after these things I saw that cedar burning and the vine growing, itself and all things in its circuit, a field full of unfading flowers. I, however, awoke and arose.


[p. 1125] {#po.0.p1125}
ܕܠܳܐ ܚܳܡܶܝܢ. ܘܐܶܢܳܐ ܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܐܶܬܬܠܺܝܥܶܬ ܘܩܳܡܶܬ܀ ܘܨܰܠܺܝܬ ܘܐܶܡܪܶܬ: ܡܳܪܝܳܐ ܡܳܪܝ: ܐܰܢ̱ܬ ܗ̱ܘܽ ܒܟܽܠܙܒܰܢ ܡܰܢܗܰܪ ܐܰܢ̱ܬ ܠܰܐܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܰܒܣܘܽܟܳܠܳܐ ܡܶܬܕܰܒܪܝܺܢ. ܢܳܡܘܽܣܳܟ݂ ܚܰܝ̈ܶܐ ܗ̱ܘ: ܘܚܶܟܡܬ݁ܳܟ ܬܪܝܰܨܘܽܬܳܐ ܗ̱ܝ. ܐܰܘܕ݁ܰܥܰܝܢܝ ܗܳܫܳܐ ܦܘܽܫܳܩܶܗ ܕܗܳܢܳܐ ܚܶܙܘܳܐ. ܐܢ̱ܬ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܝܳܕ݂ܰܥ ܐܢ̱ܬ ܕܢܰܦܫܝ ܒܟܽܠܙܒܰܢ ܒܢܳܡܘܽܣܳܟ݂ ܡܶܬܗܰܦܟܳܐ
XXXVIII. And I prayed and said: O Lord, my Master, O Lord, you at all times enlighten those who are versed in understanding. Your law is life, and your wisdom is righteousness. Show me, therefore, the interpretation of this vision. For you know that my soul has at all times been versed in your law, and from my days I have not been far from your wisdom.


[p. 1126] {#po.0.p1126}
ܗ̱ܘܳܐ: ܘܡܶܢ ܝܰܘܡܳܬܳܟ: ܡܶܢ ܚܶܟܡܬܳܟ ܐܰܪܚܩܶܬ܀ ܘܰܥܢܳܐ ܘܐܶܡܰܪ ܠܝܿ: ܒܰܪܘܳܟ ܗܳܢܰܘ ܦܘܽܫܳܩܶܗ ܕܚܶܙܘܳܐ ܕܰܚܙܰܝܬ: ܐܰܝܟ ܕܰܚܙܰܝܬ ܥܺܝܒܳܐ ܣܰܓܝܳܐܐ ܗܰܘ ܕܰܚܕ݂ܰܪܝܺܢ ܗ̱ܘܰܘ ܠܗ ܛܘܽܪܶܐ ܪ̈ܳܡܶܐ ܘܰܩܫ̈ܰܝܳܐ: ܗܳܕܳܐ ܗ̱ܝ ܡܶܠܬܳܐ: ܗܳܐ ܝܰܘ̈ܡܳܬܳܐ ܐܳܬܶܝܝܢ ܘܐܶܬܚܰܒܰܠ ܗܳܕ݂ܶܐ ܡܰܠܟ݁ܘܽܬܳܐ: ܗܳܝ ܕܒ݁ܰܙܒ݂ܰܢ ܚܰܒܠܰܬ ܠܨܲܗܝܘܽܢ: ܘܐܶܫܬܰܥܒ݁ܰܕ݂ ܠܗܳܝ ܕܐܳܬܝܳܐ ܒܳܬܪܳܗ. ܬܘܽܒ ܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܐܳܦ ܗܝܺ ܒܳܬܰܪ ܙܰܒ݂ܢܳܐ ܡܶܬܚܰܒܠܳܐ ܘܰܬܩܘܽܡ ܐ̱ܚܪܳܬܳܐ ܬܠܝܳܬܳܝܬܳܐ ܘܐܶܫܬܰܠܰܛ ܐܳܦ ܗܳܝ ܒܙܰܒܢܳܗ ܘܬܶܬܚܰܒܰܠ. ܘܡܶܢ ܒܳܬܰܪ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܬܩܘܽܡ ܡܰܠܟ݁ܘܽܬܳܐ ܪܒܺܝܥܳܝܬܳܐ ܕܐܘܽܚܕܳܢܳܗ ܗܳܘܶܐ ܩܫܶܝܐ ܘܒܰܚܝܳܫ ܝܰܬܝܺܪ ܡܶܢ ܙܕ̈ܰܝܚܳܠܶܝܢ ܕܰܗܘܰܝ̈ ܩܕ݂ܳܡܶܝܗ. ܘܰܡܕ݂ܰܒ݁ܪܳܐ ܙܰܒ݁ܢܶܐ ܣܰܓܝ̈ܰܐܶܐ ܐܰܝܟ ܥܺܝ̈ܒܶܐ ܕܰܦܩܰܥܬܳܐ ܘܬܶܐܚܘܽܕ݂ ܙܰܒ݁ܢܶܐ: ܘܬܶܬܬܪܺܝܡ ܝܰܬܝܺܪ ܡܶܢ ܐܰܪ̈ܙܶܐ ܕܠܶܒܢܳܢ. ܘܢܶܬܛܰܫܰܐ ܒܳܗ ܫܪܳܪܳܐ: ܘܢܶܥܪܩܘܽܢ ܠܘܳܬܳܗ ܟܘܽܠܗܘܽܢ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܰܡܫܰܠܚܺܝܢ ܒܥܰܘܠܳܐ: ܐܰܝܟ ܕܥܳܪ̈ܩܺܝܢ ܚܰܝܘܳܬܳܐ ܒܫܰܦܳܬܳܐ
XXXIX. And he answered and said to me: Baruch, this is the interpretation of the vision which you saw: As you saw a great forest, which high and craggy mountains surrounded, this is the word: Behold, the days are coming and this kingdom will be corrupted which once corrupted Zion, and it will be subjected to that which is to come after it. Again, however, that one also will be corrupted after a time; and another third one will arise and will also rule in its time and will be corrupted. And after these, a fourth kingdom will arise, whose power will be worse and harder than those which were before it, and it will rule for many times, like the forests of the plain, and it will hold the times and will be lifted up more than the cedars of Lebanon. And the truth will be hidden from it, and those who are defiled with iniquity will flee to it, as evil beasts flee and creep into the forest. And it shall be, when


[p. 1127] {#po.0.p1127}


[p. 1128] {#po.0.p1128}
ܘܚܳܠܕ݂ܳܢ ܒܥܳܒܳܐ. ܘܢܶܗܘܶܐ ܡܳܐ ܕܰܩܪܶܒ ܙܰܒ݂ܢܳܐ ܕܫܘܽܠܳܡܳܗ ܕܬܶܦܿܠ: ܗܳܝܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܬ݁ܶܬܓ݁ܠܶܐ ܪܺܝܫܝܽܘܬܶܗ ܕܰܡܫܺܝܚܝ: ܗܳܝ ܕܕܳܡܝܳܐ ܠܡܰܒܘܽܥܳܐ ܘܠܰܓܦܶܬܳܐ: ܘܡܳܐ ܕܐܶܬܓܰܠܝܰܬ: ܬܶܥܩܘܽܪ ܣܘܽܓ݂ܳܐܐ ܕܟܶܢܫܶܗ. ܘܗܳܝ ܕܰܚܙܰܝܬ ܐܰܪܙܳܐ ܕܰܪܡܶܐ ܗܰܘ ܕܐܶܫܬܚܰܪ ܡܶܢ ܗܰܘ ܥܺܝܒܳܐ ܘܥܰܠ ܗܳܕܶܐ ܕܡܰܠܶܠܰܬ ܥܰܡܶܗ ܓܦܶܬܳܐ ܡܶܠܶܐ
the time of its end approaches, that it falls, then the principate of my Christ will be revealed, which will be like a fountain and a vine, and when it is revealed, it will uproot the multitude of its congregation. And that which you saw, the high cedar, which was left from that forest, and concerning this, that the vine spoke to it the words which you heard, this is the word:
ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܕܰܫܡܰܥܬ: ܗܳܕܳܐ ܗ̱ܝ ܡܶܠܬܳܐ: ܡܕ݂ܰܒܪܳܢܳܐ ܐ̱ܚܪܳܝܳܐ ܕܗܳܝܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܢܶܫܬܚܰܪ ܟ݁ܰܕ ܚܝ ܟ݁ܰܕ݂ ܢܶܬܚܰܪܒܘܽܢ ܣܘܽܓܳܐܐ ܕܟܶܢܫܰܘ̈ܗ̱ܝ: ܘܢܶܬܦܟ݁ܰܪ ܘܢܰܝܬܶܝܘܗ̱ܝ ܥܰܠ ܛܘܽܪܳܐ ܕܨܗܝܘܽܢ ܘܰܡܫܺܝܚܝ ܡܰܟܣܶܣ ܠܶܗ ܥܰܠ ܟܘܽܠܗܘܽܢ ܪ̈ܰܫܥܰܘܗ̱ܝ ܘܰܢܟ݁ܰܢܶܫ ܘܰܢܣܺܝܡ ܩܕ݂ܳܡܰܘܗ̱ܝ ܟܽܠܗܘܽܢ ܥܒܳܕܼ̈ܰܝܗܘܽܢ ܕܟܶܢܫܰܘ̈ܗ̱ܝ. ܘܡܶܢ ܒܳܬ݁ܰܪ ܟܶܢ ܢܶܩܛܽܠܝܘܗ̱ܝ: ܘܢܰܓܶܢ ܥܰܠ ܟܰܪܟ݁ܶܝܗ ܕܥܰܡܝ ܗܰܘ ܕܡܶܫܬܟ݁ܰܚ ܒܐܰܬ݂ܪܳܐ ܕܰܓܒܰܝܬܿ ܘܬܶܗܘܶܐ ܪܺܝܫܽܘܬܶܗ ܩܳܝܡܳܐ ܠܥܳܠܰܡ: ܒܕ݂ܰܡܳܐ ܕܢܶܫܠܰܡ ܥܳܠܡܳܐ
XL. The last leader, who will then be left alive, when the multitude of his congregations is devastated, will be bound, and they will bring him upon Mount Zion, and my Christ will convict him of all his impieties, and will gather and set before him all the works of his hosts. And afterwards he will kill him and will protect the rest of my people, who are found in the place which I have chosen. And his principate will stand forever, until the world of corruption is consumed and until the aforementioned times are fulfilled. This is your vision and this is its explanation.
ܕܰܚܒܺܝܠܳܐ: ܘܰܒܕ݂ܰܡܳܐ ܕܢܶܬܡܠܘ̇ܢ ܙܰܒ݁ܢܶܐ ܕܰܡܧܰܝ ܕ݂ܡܶܢ ܐܰܡܚܰܪܝܺܢ. ܗܳܢܰܝܘ ܚܶܙܘܳܟ܂ ܘܗܳܢܰܘ ܦܘܽܫܳܩܶܗ܀ ܘܰܥܢܺܝܬ ܘܐܶܡܰܪܬ: ܠܡܰܢܘܽ ܘܠܰܟ݁ܡܳܐ ܗܳܘ̈ܝܳܢ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܐܰܘ ܡܰܢܘܽ ܕܫܳܘܶܐ ܕܢܺܚܶܐ ܒܗܰܘ ܙܰܒ݂ܢܳܐܢ ܐܶܡܰܠܶܠ ܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܩܕܳܡܰܝܟ ܟ݁ܽܠ ܕܡܶܬܚܰܫܰܒ ܐ̱ܢܳܐ: ܘܐܶܒܥܶܐ ܡܶܢܳܟ݂ ܥܰܠ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܪܳܢܝܐ ܐ̱ܢܳܐ̇ ܡܶܛܽܠ ܕܗܳܐ ܚܳܙܶܐ ܐ݈ܢܳܐ ܣܰܓ̈ܳܐܶܐ ܡܶܢ ܥܰܡܳܟ݂ ܕܐܰܪܚܶܩܘ ܡܶܢ ܩܝܳܡܰܝܟ: ܘܰܢܝܕ݂ܰܘ ܡܶܢܗܘܽܢ ܢܺܝܪܶܗ ܕܢܳܡܘܽܣܳܟ. ܐ̱ܚܪ̈ܳܢܶܐ ܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܬܘܒ ܚܙܝܺܬ ܕܰܫܒܰܩܘ ܣܪܝܺܩܘܽܬܗܘܽܢ ܘܰܥܪܰܩܝܘ ܬܚܶܝܬ݀ ܟܶܢ̈ܦܰܝܟ. ܡܳܢܳܐ ܗܳܟܝܶܠ ܗܳܘܶܐ ܠܗܘܽܢ ܠܗܳܠܶܝܢܢ ܐܰܘ ܐܰܝܟܰܢܳܐ ܡܩܰܒܶܠ ܠܗܘܿܢ ܗܰܘ ܙܰܒܢܳܐ ܐ̱ܚܪܳܢܳܐ܆ ܐܰܘ ܕܰܠܡܳܐ ܡܶܬܬܰܩܰܠܘܽ ܡܶܬܬܰܩܰܠ ܙܰܒܢܗܘܽܢ ܕܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܘܐܰܝܟ ܕܢܳܬܰܥ ܡܰܬܩܳܠܳܐ:
XLI. And I answered and said: To whom and how many will these things be? Or who will be worthy to live at that time? But I will speak in your presence all that I think, and I will ask you about those things which I meditate upon. For behold, I see many of your people who have departed from your covenants and have cast off from themselves the yoke of your law. But others I have seen again who have abandoned their vanity and have fled under your wings. What then will be for these? Or how will the final time itself receive them? Or will the time of these perhaps be weighed in a balance, and as the weight inclines, so will they be judged?


[p. 1129] {#po.0.p1129}


[p. 1130] {#po.0.p1130}


[p. 1131] {#po.0.p1131}
ܘܰܥܢܳܐ ܘܐܶܡܰܪ ܠܺܝ: ܐܳܦ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܐܶܢܳܐ ܐܰܚܰܘܶܝܟ. ܡܶܛܘܽܠ ܕܐܳܡܰܪܬ ܠܡܰܢܘܽ ܘܠܰܟ݁ܡܳܐ ܗܳܘ̈ܝܳܢ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ: ܠܰܐܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܗܰܝܡܶܢܘ ܗܳܘܳܐ ܛܳܒܬ݁ܳܐ ܕܰܡܩܰܕ݂ܡܳܐ ܐܶܡܰܪܬ: ܘܠܰܐܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܫܳܛܚܢ ܗܳܘܝܳܐ ܕܰܠܩܘܽܒܠܳܐ ܕܗܳܠܶܝܢ. ܘܕܐܶܡܰܪܬ ܥܰܠ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܐܶܬܩܰܪܰܒ݂ܘ ܘܥܰܠ ܐܰܝܠܺܝܢ ܕܐܶܬܪܰܚܰܩܘ: ܗܳܕܳܐ ܗ̱ܝ ܡܶܠܬܳܐ: ܗܳܢܘܽܢ ܕܡܶܢ ܩܕ݂ܝܰܡ ܐܶܫܬ݁ܰܥܒ݁ܰܕ݂ܘ ܘܡܶܢ ܒܳܬ݁ܰܪܟ݁ܶܢ ܐܰܪܚܶܩܘ ܘܐܶܬ݂ܚܰܠܰܛܘ ܒ݁ܙܰܪܥܳܐ ܕܥܰܡ̱ܡܶܐ ܕܰܚܒܝܟܝܢ: ܕܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܙܰܒܢܗܘܢ ܩܰܕ݂ܡܳܝܳܐ ܐܝܺܬܰܘܗ̱ܝ ܘܪܳܡܳܬܳܐ ܡܶܬܚܰܫܰܒ. ܘܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܡܶܢ ܩܕܝܺܡ ܠܳܐ ܝܺܕܰܥܘ ܘܡܶܢ ܒܳܬ݁ܰܪܟ݁ܶܢ ܝܺܕ݂ܰܥܘ ܚܰܝ̈ܶܐ ܘܐܶܬܚܰܠܰܛܝܘ ܒܙܰܪܥܳܐ ܕܥܰܡܳܐ ܕܰܦܪܰܫ: ܕܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܙܰܒܢܗܘܽܢ ܐ݈ܚܪܳܝܳܐ ܪܳܡܳܬ݂ܳܐ ܡܶܬܚܰܫܰܒ: ܘܝܳܪܬ݁ܝܺܢ ܙܰܒ݂ܢܶܐ
XLII. And he answered and said to me: These things also I will show you. As for what you said, to whom and how many these things will be: to those who have believed, the predicted good will be, and to those who despise, the opposite of these will be. And as for what you said about those who have drawn near and those who have departed, this is the word: Those who were previously subject and afterwards departed and mingled themselves with the seed of the mixed peoples, the time of these is considered first and high; and those who previously were ignorant and afterwards knew life and were mingled with the seed of the people who separated themselves, the [last] time of these thinks high things, and times will inherit times,


[p. 1132] {#po.0.p1132}
ܠܙܰܒܢܶܐ ܘܥܺܕ݂ܳܢܶܐ: ܠܥܺܕ݁ܳܢܶܐ ܘܚܰܕ݂ ܡܶܢ ܚܰܕ݂ ܡܩܰܒܠܝܺܢ: ܘܗܳܝܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܠܫܘܽܠܳܡܳܐ ܢܶܬܦ݁ܰܚܰܡ ܟܘܽܠ ܡܶܕ݁ܶܡ: ܐܰܝܟ ܡܫܘܽܚܬ݁ܳܐ ܕܙܰܒ݁ܢܶܐ ܘܐܝܟ ܥܺܕ݂ܳܢܶܐ ܕܥܶܕ݁ܳܢܶܐ. ܕܳܒܰܪ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܚܒܺܝܠܳܐ ܠܰܐܝܠܶܝܢ ܕ݂ܕܝܽܠܶܗ ܐܶܢܘܽܢ: ܘܚܰܝ̈ܶܐ ܠܰܐܝܠܺܝܢ ܕܕܝܳܠܗܘܽܢ ܐܶܢܘܽܢ. ܘܡܶܬܩܪܶܐ ܥܰܦܪܳܐ ܘܡܶܬܬܶܡܰܪ ܠܶܗ: ܦܰܢܳܐ ܡܶܕܶܡ ܕܠܳܐ ܗܘܳܐ ܕ݁ܝܽܠܳܟ ܗ̱ܘܵ: ܘܐܰܩܺܝܡ ܟܽܠܕ݁ܶܡ ܕܰܢܛܰܪܬ݁ ܠܙܰܒܢܶܗ܀
and ages will inherit ages, and one will receive from another, and then everything will be equated to the end, as the measure of times and as the hours of the ages. For corruption will take those who are its own, and life those who are its own. And the dust will be called, and it will be said to it: Give back what is not yours, and restore everything that you have guarded until its time.
ܐܰܢ̱ܬ ܕܶܝܢ ܒܰܚܪܘܽܟ ܐܰܬ݁ܩܶܢ ܠܶܒܳܟ ܠܡܶܕܶܡ ܕܐܶܬܶܐܡܰܪ ܠܳܟ: ܘܐܶܣܬ݁ܰܟ݁ܰܠ ܐܰܝܠܺܝܢ ܕܐܶܬܚܰܘܝܘ ܠܳܟ: ܡܶܛܽܠ ܕܐܝܺܬ ܠܳܟ ܒܘܽܝܳܐܶܐ ܣܰܓܺܝ̈ܐܶܐ ܕ݁ܰܠܥܳܠܰܡ. ܐܳܙܰܠ ܐܰܢ̱ܬ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܡܶܢ ܐܰܬܪܳܐ ܗܳܢܳܐ ܘܬܶܥܒܰܪ ܐܰܢ̱ܬ ܡܶܢ ܐܰܬܪ̈ܰܘܳܬܳܐ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕ݁ܡܶܬܚܙܶܝܢ ܠܳܟ ܗܳܫܳܐ ܘܬܶܛܥܶܐ ܠܡܶܕ݂ܶܡ ܕܡܶܬܚܰܒܰܠ ܘܠܳܐ ܡܶܬܕܟܰܪ ܐܰܢ̱ܬ ܬܘܽܒ ܠܰܐܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܒܰܝܢܳܬ ܡܳܝ̈ܽܘܬܶܐ ܐܰܢ̱ܬܶܝܢ. ܙܶܠ ܗܳܟܝܽܠ ܘܦܰܩܶܕ݂ ܠܥܰܡܳܟ ܘܬܳܐ ܠܰܐܬ݂ܪܳܐ ܗܳܢܳܐ: ܘܡܶܢ ܒܳܬܰܪܟ݁ܶܢ
XLIII. But you, Baruch, strengthen your heart for that which has been said to you, and understand those things which have been shown to you, because you have many consolations forever. For you will depart from this place and will pass from the regions which are now visible to you, and you will forget all corruptible things, nor will you again remember those things which are among mortals. Go therefore and command your people, and come to this place, and afterwards fast for seven days; and then I will come to you and speak with you.


[p. 1133] {#po.0.p1133}
ܨܽܘܡ ܫܰܒܥܳܐ ܝܰܘ̈ܡܝܺܢ: ܘܗܳܝܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܐܶܬܶܐ ܠܘܳܬܳܟ݂ ܘܐܶܡܰܠܶܠ ܥܰܡܳܟ ܀ ܘܐܶܙܰܠ̱ܬ ܡܶܢ ܬܰܡܳܢ ܐܶܢܳܐ ܒܰܚܪܘܽܟ ܘܐܶܬܺܝܬ ܠܘܳܬ ܥܰܡܝ: ܘܰܩܪܺܝܬ ܠܒܺܪܝ ܒܘܽܟܪܝ ܘܰܠܓ݂ܕܰܠܝܳܐ ܪܳܚܡܝ ܘܰܠܫܰܒܥܳܐ ܡܶܢ ܣܳܒ̈ܶܐ ܕܥܰܡܳܐ ܘܐܶܡܪܶܬ ܠܗܘܽܢ: ܗܳܐ ܐܶܢܳܐ ܐܳܙܶܠ ܐ̱ܢܳܐ ܠܝ ܠܘܳܬ ܐܰܒܳܗܰܝ̈ ܐܰܝܟ ܐܘܽܪܚܳܐ ܕܟܘܽܠܳܗ ܐܰܪܥܳܐ. ܐܰܢ̱ܬܘܽܢ ܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܠܳܐ ܬܶܪܚܩܘܽܢ ܡܶܢ ܐܘܽܪܚܶܗ ܕ݁ܢܳܡܘܽܣܳܐ: ܐܶܠܳܐ ܢܰܛܰܪܘ ܘܙܰܗܰܪܘ ܠܥܰܡܳܐ ܕܐܶܫܬܚܰܪ ܕܠܳܐ ܢܶܪܚܩܘܽܢ ܡܶܢ ܦܘܽܩܕ݂ܳܢܰܘ̈ܗ̱ܝ ܕ݁ܚܰܝܠܬ݁ܳܢܳܐ. ܚܳܙܶܝܢ ܐܰܢ̱ܬܘܽܢ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܕܙܰܕ݂ܝܺܩ ܗ̱ܘܳ ܗܰܘ ܕܠܶܗ ܦܳܠܚܺܝܢ ܚ̱ܢܰܢ ܘܒܐܰܦ݁̈ܶܐ ܠܳܐ ܢܳܣܶܒ ܓܳܒܘܽܠܰܢ. ܘܰܚܙܰܘ ܡܶܕܶܡ ܕܓܰܕ݂ܫܳܗ ܠܨܶܗܝܘܽܢ ܘܡܳܢܳܐ ܡܛܳܗܿ ܠܐܘܿܪܰܫܠܶܡ. ܡܶܛܽܠ ܕܢܶܬܝܺܕ݂ܰܥ ܕܝܳܢܶܗ ܕܚܰܝܠܬ݁ܳܢܳܐ ܘܐܘܽܪ̈ܚܳܬܶܗ ܕܠܳܐ ܡܶܬܥܰܩ̈ܒܳܢ ܘܰܐܪ̈ܝܺܨܳܬܳܐ ܐܶܢܶܝܢ. ܐܶܢ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܬܣܰܝܒܪܘܽܢ ܘܰܬܩܰܘܘܽܢ ܒܕܶܚܠܬܶܗ ܘܠܳܐ ܬܶܛܥܘܽܢ ܢܳܡܘܽܣܶܝܗ: ܡܶܬܚܰܠܦܺܝܢ ܥܠܰܝܟܘܽܢ ܙܰܒ݂̈ܢܶܐ ܠܛܳܒ̈ܳܬܳܐ ܘܬܶܚܕ݁ܘܽܢ ܒܒܘܽܝܳܐܳܗ ܕܨܶܗܝܘܽܢ. ܡܶܛܽܠ ܕ݁ܡܶܕ݁ܶܡ ܕܗܳܘܶܐ ܗܳܟܳܐ ܠܳܐ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܡܶܕܶܡ: ܡܶܕܶܡ ܕܶܝܢ ܕܰܥܬܝܰܕ݂ ܕܢܶܗܘܶܐ: ܗܘܺܝܘܽ ܪܰܒ ܣܰܓܝ. ܥܳܒܰܪ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܟܽܠܕ݁ܶܡ ܕܡܶܬܚܰܒܰܠ ܘܐܳܙܶܠ ܟ݁ܽܠ ܡܳܐ ܕ ܕ݁ܡܳܐܶܬ݂ ܘܡܶܬܛܥܶܐ ܟܘܽܠܶܗ ܙܰܒ݂ܢܳܐ ܕܗܳܫܳܐ ܘܠܳܐ ܗܳܘܶܐ ܕܘܽܟܪܳܢ ܠܙܰܒܢܳܐ ܕܗܳܫܳܐ ܕܰܡܦܰܠܦܰܠ ܒܒ̈ܝܫܳܬܳܐ. ܗܰܘ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܕܪܳܗܶܛ ܗܳܫܳܐ ܠܰܣܪܝܺܩܘܽܬܳܐ ܗ̱ܘ ܪܳܗܶܛ: ܘܗܰܘ ܕܡܰܨܠܰܚ ܒܰܥܓܰܠ ܢܳܦܶܠ ܘܡܶܬܡܰܟܰܟ݂. ܡܶܕܶܡ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܕܰܥܬܝܺܕ݂ ܗܰܘ ܗ̱ܘܳ ܡܶܬܒܥܶܐ: ܘܡܶܕ݂ܶܡ ܕܒܳܬܰܪ ܟܶܢ ܐܳܬܶܐ ܥܰܠ ܗܰܘ ܗ̱ܘْ ܡܣܰܒܪܝܺܢܰܢ. ܐܝܺܬ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܙܰܒ݂ܢܳܐ ܕܠܳܐ ܥܳܒ݁ܰܪ ܘܐܳܬ݂ܶܐ ܥܶܕܳܢܳܐ ܗܰܘ ܕܰܡܩܰܘܶܐ ܠܥܳܠܰܡ ܘܥܳܠܡܳܐ ܚܕ݂ܬ݁ܳܐ ܗܰܘ ܕܠܰܐܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܰܒ݂ܪܺܫܶܗ ܐܳܙܠܺܝܢ ܠܳܐ ܡܰܗܦܶܟ ܠܗܘܽܢ ܠܰܚܒܳܠܳܐ: ܘܥܰܠ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ
XLIV. And I, Baruch, went from there and came to my people and called my firstborn son and Gedaliah my friend and seven of the elders of the people and said to them: Behold, I am going to my fathers according to the way of all the earth. But you, do not depart from the way of the law, but guard and admonish the people who remain, that they do not depart from the precepts of the Most High. For you see that he whom we serve is just, and our Creator does not accept persons. And see what has happened to Zion and what has befallen Jerusalem. For the judgment of the Most High will be made known, and his ways are unsearchable and just. For if you endure and remain in his fear, the times will be changed for you into good, and you will see the consolation of Zion. For what is now is nothing; but what is to come is very great. For whatever is corrupted will pass away, and whatever dies will depart, and the whole present time will be handed over to oblivion, nor will there be any memory of the present time, which is now defiled by misfortunes. For he who runs now, runs in vain, and he who prospers will quickly fall and be humbled. For what is to come, that is desired; and what comes afterwards, for that we hope. For there is a time that will not pass away; and an age will come that will remain forever, and a new world, which will not turn into corruption those who have penetrated into its beginning, and over those who will go


[p. 1134] {#po.0.p1134}


[p. 1135] {#po.0.p1135}


[p. 1136] {#po.0.p1136}
ܠܬܰܫܢܝܺܩܳܐ ܠܳܐ ܡܪܰܚܺܡ: ܘܠܰܐܝܠܝܢ ܕ݁ܚܰܝܺܝܢ ܒܶܗ ܠܳܐ ܡܰܘܒܶܠ ܠܰܒ݂ܕ݁ܳܢܳܐ. ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܐܶܢܘܽܢ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܕܰܥܬܝܰܕ݂ܝܺܢ ܕܢܺܐܪܬܘܽܢ ܗܳܢܳܐ ܙܰܒ݂ܢܳܐ ܕܐܶܬܶܡܰܪ: ܘܕܝܺܠܗܘܿܢ ܗ̱ܝ݇ ܚܳܪܬܘܽܬܳܐ ܕܙܰܒܢܐ ܕܰܡܠܝܺܟ. ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܐܰܬܩܰܢܘ ܠܗܘܿܢ ܐܰܘܨܪܶܐ ܕܚܶܟܡܬܳܐ: ܘܣܝܳܡ̈ܬܳܐ ܕܣܰܟܘܽܠܬܳܢܘܽܬܳܐ ܠܘܳܬܗܘܽܢ ܐܶܫܬܟܰܚ: ܘܡܶܢ ܪ̈ܰܚܡܶܐ ܠܳܐ ܐܰܪܚܶܩܘ ܘܰܫܪܳܪܶܗ ܕܢܺܝܡܘܽܣܳܐ ܢܛܰܪܘ.
to punishment, it will not have mercy, and those who live in it, it will not lead to perdition. For these are they who will inherit this time which has been spoken of; and theirs is the inheritance of the promised time. These are they who have prepared for themselves storehouses of wisdom; and treasures of intelligence have been found with them, and they have not departed from mercy and have guarded the truth of the law. For to these will the world to come be given; but the dwelling of the many others will be in fire.
ܕܐܳܬܶܐ: ܡܰܥܡܪܰܗܘܽܢ ܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܕܫܰܪܟ݁ܳܝܐ ܕܣܰܓ̈ܺܝܶܐܐ ܒܢܘܽܪܳܐ ܗܳܘܶܐ܀ ܐܰܢ̱ܬܘܽܢ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܟܡܳܐ ܕܡܶܫܟܚܝܢ ܐܢ̱ܬܘܽܢ ܐܰܪܬܰܐܘܽܗ̱ܝ ܠܥܰܡܳܐ: ܡܶܛܽܠ ܕܕܝܺܠܰܢ ܗܝ݇ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܗܳܕ݂ܶܐ ܠܐܳܒܳܐ. ܐܶܢ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܬܰܠܦܘܽܢ
XLV. You therefore, as much as you can, exhort the people; for this labor is ours. For if you teach them, you will give them life.
ܐܶܢܘܽܢ: ܡܰܚܶܝܢ ܐܰܢ̱ܬܘܽܢ ܠܗܘܽܢ܀ ܘܰܥܢܰܘ ܒܶܪܝ ܘܣܳܒܰܘ̈ܗ̱ܝ ܕ݁ܥܰܡܳܐ ܘܐܶܡܰܪܘ ܠܝܺ: ܥܕ݂ܰܡܳܐ ܠܗܳܟܰܢܳܐ ܡܰܟܟܰܢ ܚܰܝܠܬ݁ܳܢܳܐ ܕܢܶܣܒܳܟ ܡܶܢܰܢ ܥܰܠ ܒܰܥܓ݂ܰܠ ܘܢܰܪܝܺܪܳܐܝܺܬ ܢܶܗܘܶܐ ܕܰܝܪ̈ܶܐ ܒܚܶܫܘܽܟܳܐ: ܘܢܘܽܗܪܳܐ ܠܳܐ ܢܶܗܘܶܐ ܠܥܰܡܳܐ ܕܐܶܫܬܚܰܪܘ. ܐܰܝܟܳܐ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܬܘܒ ܢܶܒܥܶܐ ܢܳܡܘܽܣܳܐ܆ ܐܰܘ ܡܰܢܘܽ ܡܦܰܪܶܫ ܠܰܢ ܒܶܝܬ ܡܘܬܳܐ ܠܚܰܝ̈ܶܐ܆ ܘܐܶܡܪܶܬ ܠܗܘܽܢ ܕܠܘܽܩܒܰܠ ܬ݁ܪܽܘܢܳܘܣ ܕܚܰܝܠܬܳܢܳܐ ܠܳܐ ܡܶܫܟ݁ܰܚ ܐ̱ܢܳܐ ܕܐܶܩܘܺܡ: ܒܪܰܡ ܕܶܝܢ ܠܳܐ ܚܣܰܪ ܠܐܝܺܣܪܳܝܺܠ ܚܰܟܺܝ̈ܡܶܐ: ܘܠܳܐ ܒܰܪ ܢܳܡܘܽܣܳܝܐ ܠܓ݂ܶܢܣܶܗ ܕܝܰܥܩܘܽܒ. ܒܰܠܚܘܽܕ݂ ܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܐܰܢ̱ܬܘܽܢ ܛܰܝܶܒܘ ܠܒܰܝ̈ܟܘܽܢ ܕܐܶܝܬܰܘܽܢ ܠܢܳܡܘܽܣܳܐ ܘܐܶܫܬܰܥܒܕܘܽܢ ܠܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܒ݁ܕܶܚܠܬ݁ܳܐ ܚܰܟܺܝ̈ܡܝ̈ܢ ܘܣܰܟܘܽܠܬܳܢܝܺܢ ܘܛܰܝܶܒܘ ܢܰܦܫܝܟܘܽܢ ܕܠܳܐ ܬܶܗܘܘܽܢ ܡܰܪܚܩܺܝܢ ܡܶܢܗܘ̇ܢ. ܐܶܢ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܬܶܥܒܕܘܽܢ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ: ܢܺܐܬ݂ܝܳܢ ܠܟܘܢ ܣܰܒ݁ܪ̈ܳܬܳܐ ܘܳܠܳܐ ܕܩܰܕ݂ܡܶܬ ܐܶܡܪܶܬ ܠܟܘܽܢ ܘܳܠܳܐ ܢܳܦܠܝܺܢ ܐܢ̱ܬܘܽܢ ܒܬܰܫܢܝܺܩܳܐ ܕܩܰܕ݂ܡܶܬ ܐܰܣܗܪܶܬ ܠܟܘܿܢ. ܥܰܠ ܡܶܠܬ݁ܳܐ ܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܕܡܶܬ݂ܢܣܶܒ ܐ̱ܢܳܐ: ܠܳܐ ܐܰܘܕܥܶܬ ܐܶܢܘܽܢ ܐܳܦ݂ ܠܳܐ ܠܒܶܪܝ܀
XLVI. And my son and the elders of the people answered and said to me: Has the Most High humbled us so much that he takes you from us quickly? And truly we will be in darkness, and there will be no light for the people who have been left. For where shall we again seek the law? Or who will discern for us death and life? And I said to them: I cannot resist against the throne of the Most High; nevertheless, the wise Israel will not fail, nor will the son of the law of the race of Jacob. But only prepare your hearts, that you may obey the law and be subject to those who are wise and understanding with fear; and prepare your soul, that you may not be departing from them. For if you do these things, the announcements which I have predicted to you will come to you, nor will you fall into the punishment which I have already testified to you. [But about the word that I was to be taken up, I did not indicate to them, not even to my son.]


[p. 1137] {#po.0.p1137}


[p. 1138] {#po.0.p1138}


[p. 1139] {#po.0.p1139}
ܘܟ݁ܰܕ݂ ܢܶܦ݂ܩܶܬ ܘܰܥܕ݂ܰܪܬ ܐܶܢܘܽܢ: ܐܶܙܿܠ̱ܬ݁ ܠܝܺ ܡܶܢ ܐܰܡܳܢ ܘܐܡܪ ܠܗܘܢ: ܗܳܐ ܐܶܢܳܐ ܐܳܙܰܠ ̱ܢܳܐ ܥܕ݂ܰܡܳܐ ܠܚܰܝܒܪܘܽܢ: ܠܬ݁ܰܡܳܢ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܥܰܕ݂ܪܰܢܝ ܚܰܝܠܬ݁ܳܢܳܐ. ܘܐܶܬܺܝܬ ܠܗܰܘ ܐܰܬܪܳܐ ܕܐܶܬܡܰܠܠ ܥܰܡܝ ܐܰܡܳܢ ܘܢܬܒܳܝܬ ܬܰܡܳܢ ܘܕܳܡܶܬ
XLVII. And when I had gone out and dismissed them, I went from there and said to them: Behold, I am going as far as Hebron. For the Most High sent me there. And I came to that place where the word had been spoken to me, and I sat there and fasted for seven days.

### THE PRAYER OF BARUCH
ܫܰܒܥܳܐ ܝܰܘܡܝܺܢ܀ ܨܠܘܽܬܳܐ ܕܒܰܪܘܽܟ܂ ܘܰܗܘܳܐ ܡܶܢ ܒܳܬܰܪ ܝܰܘܡܳܐ ܫܒܺܝܥܳܝܳܐ ܕܰܨܠܺܝܬ ܩܕ݂ܳܡ ܚܰܝܠܬ݁ܳܢܳܐ ܘܐܶܡܪܶܬ: ܐܳܘ̄ ܡܳܪܝܳܐ: ܩܳܪܶܐ ܐܰܢ̱ܬ ܡܶܐܬܝܺܬܗܘܽܢ ܕܙܰܒ݂̈ܢܶܐ ܣܺܝܡܝܳܢ ܩܕܳܡܰܝܟ: ܡܰܥܒܰܪ ܐ݇ܢܰܬ ܐܘܺܚܕܳܢܗܘ̇ܢ ܕܥܳܠܡܳܐ ܘܠܳܐ ܥܳܨܶܝܢ ܠܳܟ: ܡܦܰܪܢܶܣ ܐ݇ܢ̱ܬ ܙܰܒܢܰܝܗܘܽܢ ܕܥܺܕ݁ܳܢܶܐ ܘܡܶܫܬܰܡܥܝ݁ܢ ܠܳܟ. ܐܰܢ̱ܬ ܒܰܠܚܘܽܕܰܝܟ ܝܳܕ݂ܰܥ ܐܰܢ̱ܬ ܡܶܬܚܗܘ̇ܢ ܕܕ݂ܳܪܶܐ ܘܠܳܐ ܓ݁ܳܠܶܐ ܐܰܢ̱ܬ ܪܳܐܙܰܝܟ ܠܣܰܓܔܺܝ̈ܐܶܐ. ܡܰܘܕ݁ܰܥ ܐܰܢ̱ܬ ܣܘܽܓܿܳܐܐ ܕܢܘܽܪܳܐ: ܘܩܰܠܺܝܠܘܽܬܳܐ ܕܪܘܽܚܳܐ ܬܩܺܝܠ ܐܰܢ̱ܬ: ܒܳܕܶܩ ܐ݇ܢ̱ܬ ܣܰܝܦܗܘ̇ܢ ܕܪ̈ܰܘܡܶܐ:
XLVIII. And it happened after the seventh day I prayed before the Most High and said: O Lord, you call the coming of the times and they stand before you, you transfer the power of the ages, nor do they resist you; you arrange the reason of the times and they obey you; you alone know the span of the generations, nor do you reveal your mysteries to many. You indicate the multitude of fire and weigh the lightness of the spirit; you investigate the end of the heights, and you search the depths of the darkness; you command the number


[p. 1140] {#po.0.p1140}
ܘܥܰܘ̈ܝܺܩܳܬܳܐ ܕܚܶܫܘܽܟܳܝܐ ܒܳܨܶܐ ܐ݇ܢ̱ܬ: ܦ݁ܳܩܶܕ݂ ܐܰܢ݈ܬ݁ ܠܥܶܒܪܳܢܳܐ ܕܥܳܒܰܪ ܘܡܶܬܢܛܰܪ: ܘܰܡܥܰܬ݁ܕܰܬ ܒܶܝܬ ܡܰܥܡܪܳܐ ܠܰܐܝܠܺܝܢ ܕܰܥܬܝܰܕ݂ܝܺܢ ܕܢܶܗܘܘܽܢ. ܐܰܢ̱ܬ ܥܳܗܝܺܕ݂ ܐܰܢ̱ܬ ܪܺܝܫܝܺܬ݁ܳܐ ܕܰܥܒܰܕܬ: ܘܐܰܒ݂ܕ݁ܳܢܳܐ ܕ݁ܰܥܬܝܺܕ݂ ܠܳܐ ܛܳܥܶܐ ܐܰܢ̱ܬ: ܦܳܩܶܕ݂ ܐܰܢ̱ܬ ܒ݁ܪܶܡܙܶܐ ܕ݂ܕ݂ܶܚܠܬܳܐ ܘܕܰܙܥܺܝܦܘܽܬܳܐ ܠܫܰܠܗܶܒ̈ܫܳܬܳܐ ܘܡܶܬܚܰܠܦܳܢ ܠܙܘܽܚܶܐ: ܘܰܒܡܶܠܬ݁ܳܐ ܡܩܺܝܡ ܐܰܢ̱ܬ ܡܶܝܕ݁ܶܡ ܕܠܰܝܬ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܘܐܰܚܺܝܕ ܐ݇ܢ̱ܬ ܠܘܽܥܒܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܕܰܥܕ݂ܰܟܝܺܠ ܠܳܐ ܐܶܬܳܐ ܒܚܰܝܠܳܐ ܪܰܒ݁ܳܐ. ܡܰܠܦܰܬ ܒܶܪ̈ܝܳܬ݁ܳܐ ܒܣܰܟܘܽܠܬ݁ܳܢܘܽܬܳܟ: ܘܰܡܚܰܟܡ ܐ݇ܢ̱ܬ ܟܽܘܙܠܶܐ ܕܰܢܫܰܡܫܘܽܢ ܒܛܲܟܣܰܝ̈ܗܘܿܢ. ܡܰܡܥܰܪ̈ܝܳܬ݁ܳܐ ܕܠܳܐ ܡܶܢܝܳܢ ܩܳܝܡܝܳܢ ܩܕ݂ܳܡܰܝܟ ܘܰܡܫܰܡܫܝܳܝܢ ܒܛܲܟܣܰܝ̈ܗܘܽܢ ܢܝܺܚܳܐܝܺܬ ܠܪܶܡܙܳܟܿ. ܫܡܰܥ ܠܥܰܒܕܳܟ ܘܨܘܽܬ ܠܒܳܥܘܽܬܝ: ܡܶܛܽܠ ܕ݁ܰܒ݁ܩܰܠܺܝܠ ܙܰܒܢܳܐ ܐܶܬܝܺܠܶܕ݂ܢܰܢ ܘܰܒܙܰܒܢܳܐ ܩܰܠܺܝܠܳܐ ܗܳܦܟܝܺܢܰܢ. ܠܘܳܬܳܟ݂ ܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܫܳܡܶܐ ܐܰܝܟ ܙܰܒܢܳܐ: ܘܝܰܘ̈ܡܳܬ݁ܳܐ ܐܰܝܟ ܕܳܪܶܐ. ܠܳܐ ܗܳܟܝܶܠ ܬ݁ܶܪܓ݁ܰܙ ܥܰܠ ܒܰܪܢܳܫܳܐ: ܡܶܛܽܠ ܕܠܳܐ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܡܶܕ݁ܶܡ ܗ̱ܘ. ܘܠܳܐ ܬܶܬܚܰܫܰܒ ܥܰܠ ܥܒܳܕ݂ܰܝܺܢ: ܡܳܢܳܐ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܐܺܝܬܰܝܢ ܚܢܰܢ܆ ܗܳܐ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܒܡܰܘܗܰܒܬܰܟ ܐܶܬܰܝܢ ܠܥܳܠܡܳܐ: ܘܐܳܙܠ̱ܝܢܰܢ ܕܠܳܐ ܒܨܶܒ݂ܝܳܢܰܢ. ܠܳܐ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܐܶܡܰܪܢܰܢ ܠܰܐܒܳܗܰܝ̈ܢ: ܕܐܰܘ̈ܠܶܕ݂ܘܰܢ: ܐܳܦ݂ܠܳܐ ܥܰܕܰܪܢ ܠܰܟܝܳܢܳܐ ܘܐܶܡܰܪܢ: ܕܩܰܒܶܠܰܝܢ. ܡܳܢܳܐ ܗܘ ܗܳܟܶܠ ܚܰܝܠܰܢ ܕ݁ܢܶܛܥܰܢ ܪܘܽܓ݂ܙܳܟ ~ ܐܰܘ ܡܳܢܳܐ ܚܢܰܢ ܕܰܢܣܰܝܒܰܪ ܕܝܺܢܳܟ܆ ܐܰܢ̱ܬ ܐܰܓܶܢ ܥܠܰܝܢ ܒܪ̈ܰܚܡܰܝܟ: ܘܒ݁ܰܚܢܺܝܢܝܟ ܣܰܝܰܥܰܝܢ. ܚܘܽܪ ܒܰܙܥܘܽܪܶܐ ܕܐܶܫܬܰܥܒܰܕ݂ܘ ܠܳܟ: ܘܰܦܪܘܽܩ ܠܟ݁ܽܠ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ
