ܕܡܶܬܩܰܪܒ݁ܺܝܢ ܠܳܟ. ܘܠܳܐ ܬܶܦܣܘܽܩ ܣܰܒܪܶܗ ܕܥܰܡܰܝܢ: ܘܠܳܐ ܬܶܩܛܘܽܥ ܙܰܒ݂ܢ̈ܶܐ ܕܥܘܽܕܪܳܢܰܢ. ܗܳܢܰܘ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܥܰܡܳܝܐ ܕܰܓܒܰܝܬ: ܘܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܐܶܢܘܽܢ ܥܰܡܳܐ ܕܠܳܐ ܡܶܫܟܚܰܬ ܐܰܟܘܳܬܶܗ. ܐܶܠܳܐ ܐܶܡܰܠܶܠ ܩܕ݂ܳܡܰܝܟ ܗܳܫܳܐ ܘܐܺܡܰܪ ܐܰܟ ܕܡܶܬܚܰܫܰܒ ܠܶܒܝ: ܥܠܰܝܟ ܬܟܝܺܠܝܺܢܰܢ ܕ݁ܗܳܐ ܢܳܡܘܽܣܳܟ݂ ܠܘܳܬܰܢ: ܘܝܳܕ݂ܥܝܢܰܢ ܕܠܳܐ ܢܳܦܠܺܝܢܰܢ ܟܡܳܐ ܕܰܦܩܽܘܕ݂ܰܝ̈ܟ ܐܰܚܺܝܕ݂ܝܺܢ ܚ݈ܢܰܢ. ܒܟ݁ܽܠܙܒ݂ܰܢ ܛܘܽܒܰܝܢ: ܐܳܦ݁ܶܢ ܒ݁ܗܳܕ݁ܶܐ ܕܠܳܐ ܐܬܚܰܠܰܛܝܢ ܒܥܰܡ̱ܡ̈ܶܐ. ܟ݁ܽܠܰܢ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܐܝܺܬܰܝܢ ܚܰܕ݂ ܥܰܡܳܐ ܡܫܰܡܗܳܐ ܕܰܢܣܰܒܢ ܚܰܕ݂ ܢܳܡܘܽܣܳܐ ܡܶܢ ܚܰܕ݂: ܘܗܰܘ ܢܳܡܘܽܣܳܐ ܕܒܰܝܢܳܬܰܢ ܡܥܰܨܰܪ ܠܰܢ: ܘܚܶܟܡܬ݁ܳܐ ܡܚܰܬ݁ܰܪܬ݁ܳܐ ܕܐܺܝܬ ܒܰܝܢܳܬܰܢ ܬܣܰܝܥܰܢ. ܘܟܰܕ݂ ܨܰܠܺܝܬ ܘܐܶܡܪܶܬ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ: ܐܶܬܪܰܫܠܶܬ ܣܰܓܝܳܐܝܺܬ. ܘܰܥܢܳܐ ܘܐܶܡܰܪ ܠܝ ܐܰܢ̱ܬ ܦܫܝܛܳܐܝܺܬ ܨܰܠܝܺܬ: ܐܿܘ̄ ܒܰܪܘܽܟ: ܘܐܶܫܬ݁ܡܰܥ ܟܘܽܠܗܶܝܢ ܡܶܠܰܝ̈ܟ. ܐܶܠܳܐ ܕܝܺܢܝ ܬ݁ܳܒ݂ܰܥ ܕܝܽܠܶܗ: ܘܢܳܡܘܽܣܝ ܬ݁ܳܒ݂ܰܥ ܙܶܕ݂ܩܶܗ. ܡܶܢ ܡܶܠܰܝ̈ܟ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܡܰܬܝܺܒ ܐ̱ܢܳܐ ܠܳܟ݂: ܘܡܶܢ ܨܠܘܽܬܳܟ݂ ܐܶܡܰܠܶܠ ܥܰܡܳܟ. ܗܳܝ ܗܝ݇ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܗܳܕ݁ܶܐ ܕܠܳܐ ܗܘܳܐ ܡܶܕܶܡ ܗܰܘ ܕܡܶܬܚܰܒܰܠ: ܘܐܰܪܫܰܥ ܐܰܟ
passing by and it is guarded; and you prepare a dwelling for those who are to be. You remember the beginning which you made, and the perdition which is to be, you do not forget; you command with nods of fear and flames of indignation and they are changed into winds; and by a word you excite what was not, and you hold what has not yet come, with great power; you instruct the creatures with your intelligence and you make the spheres wise, that they may minister in their orders. Innumerable armies stand before you and minister in their orders quietly at your nod. Hear your servant and attend to my supplication. For we were born in a short time and in a short time we return. But with you the hours are as a time and the days as generations. Do not therefore be angry against man, because he is nothing, nor reconsider our works. For what are we? For behold, by your gift we have come into the world and we depart without our will. Nor did we say to our parents: beget us, nor did we send into Hades saying: Receive us! What then is our power, that we may bear your wrath? Or what are we, that we may sustain your judgment? You protect us with your mercy and help us with your compassion. Look upon the little ones, who have subjected themselves to you, and save all who draw near to you, nor take away the hope of our people, nor cut off the times of our help. For this is the people whom you chose, and these are the people to whom you will find no equal. But I will speak before you now, and I will say, as my heart thinks. In you we trust, because behold your law is with us and we know that we will not fall, while we hold your sanctions. We will always be blessed at least in this, that we have not been mingled with the nations. For we are all one illustrious people, who have received one law from one; and the law, which is among us, will help us, and the excellent wisdom, which is in us, will assist us. And when I had prayed and said these things, I was weakened greatly. And he answered and said to me: You have prayed simply, O Baruch, and all your words have been heard. But my judgment requires its own and my law requires its right. For from your words I will answer you and from your prayer I will speak to you. For this is the very thing: there is nothing that


[p. 1141] {#po.0.p1141}


[p. 1142] {#po.0.p1142}


[p. 1143] {#po.0.p1143}


[p. 1144] {#po.0.p1144}
ܗܰܘ ܕܰܡܨܶܐ ܕܢܶܥܒܶܕ݂ ܡܶܕܶܡ ܘܠܳܐ ܐܶܬܕܟܰܪ ܛܰܝܒܘܽܬܝ: ܘܠܳܐ ܩܰܒܶܠ ܢܰܓܺܝܪܘܽܬܝ ܪܘܺܚܝ. ܡܶܛܽܠ ܗܳܢܳܐ ܡܶܬܢܣܰܒ ܐܰܢ݈ܬ: ܐܰܝܟ ܕܩܰܕܼܡܶܬ ܐܶܡܪܶܐ ܠܳܟ. ܘܐܳܬ݂ܶܐ ܙܰܒܢܳܐ ܕܐܶܡܪܶܐ ܠܳܟ ܘܰܢܩܘܽܡ ܗܰܘ ܙܰܒܢܳܐ ܕܐܳܠܶܨ. ܢܺܐܬ݂ܶܐ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܘܢܶܥܒܰܪ ܒܚܺܝܐܦܳܐ ܚܰܪܝܺܦܳܐ: ܘܢܶܬܕ݁ܰܠܰܚ ܟܰܕ݂ ܐܳܬ݁ܶܐ ܒܚܶܝܡܬܳܐ ܕܰܙܥܺܝܦܘܽܬܳܐ. ܘܢܶܗܘܶܐ ܒܗܳܢܘܽܢ ܝܰܘ̈ܡܳܬܳܐ: ܢܶܬܬܢܝܺܚܘܽܢ ܟܘܽܠܗܘܽܢ ܥܳܡܘܽܪ̈ܶܝܗ ܕܰܐܪܥܳܐ ܚܰܕ ܥܰܠ ܚܰܕ݂: ܡܶܛܽܠ ܕܠܳܐ ܝܳܕ݂ܥܝܺܢ ܕܰܩܪܶܒ݂ ܕܝّܢܝ. ܠܳܐ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܣܰܓ̈ܺܝܶܐ
is corrupted and has acted impiously, just as if it could accomplish something, nor did it remember my grace nor did it receive my long-suffering. Because of this you will be taken up, as I predicted to you, and the time will come, which I said to you, and the time will arise, which will tribulate. For it will come and will pass with a vehement impulse and the coming one will be disturbed with the fury of indignation. And it will be in those days: all the inhabitants of the earth will rest one upon another not knowing that my judgment is approaching. For not many wise will be found at that time, and the understanding ones will be a few, but those who know, will especially be silent. And there will be many hearings and not a few rumors and the works of phantoms will be manifested and not a few promises will be told,


[p. 1145] {#po.0.p1145}
ܘܣܰܟ݁̈ܘܽܠܬ݁ܳܢܶܐ ܚܰܕ݂ܚܕܳܢܶܝܐ ܢܶܗܘܘܽܢ: ܐܶܠܳܐ ܐܳܦ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܝܺܕ݂ܥܝܺܢ ܝܰܬܝܺܪܳܐܝܺܬ ܢܶܫܬܩܘܿܢ. ܘܢܶܪܗܘܘܽܢ ܫܶܡܰܥܶܐ ܣܰܓ̈ܺܝܶܐ ܘܛܶܒ̈ܶܐ ܕܠܳܐ ܙܥܘܿܪܝܺܢ: ܘܰܥܒܳܕ݂ܶܐ ܦ݁ܰܢܛܰܣܺܝܶܐ ܢܶܬܚܰܘܘܽܢ ܘܢܶܬܬܰܢܘܽܢ ܡܘܽܠܟܳܢܶܐ ܠܳܐ ܙܥܘܿܪܝܳܢ: ܡܶܢܗܘܽܢ ܣܪܝܺܩܝܽܢ: ܘܡܶܢܗܘܿܢ ܢܶܫܬܰܪܪܘܽܢ. ܘܢܶܗܦܘܽܟ ܐܝܺܩܳܪܳܐ ܠܨܰܥܪܳܐ: ܘܢܶܬܡܰܟ݁ܰܟ݂ ܥܘܽܫܢܳܐ ܠܫܝܳܛܘܽܬܳܐ. ܘܬܶܫܬܪܶܐ ܬܰܩܢܘܽܬܳܐ: ܘܫܰܦܝܺܪܘܽܬܳܐ ܬ݁ܶܗܘܶܐ ܠܒܰܣܝܳܬܳܐ. ܘܢܺܐܡܪܘܽܢ ܣܰܓ̈ܺܝܶܐ ܠܣܰܓ̈ܺܝܶܐ ܒܗܰܘ ܙܰܒ݂ܢܳܐ: ܐܰܝܟܳܐ ܐܶܬܟܰܣܝܰܬ ܠܳܗ ܣܘܽܓܳܐܐ ܕܣܰܟܘܽܠܬ݁ܳܢܘܽܬ݂ܳܐ ܘܠܰܐܝܟܳܐ ܫܰܢܝܰܬ ܠܳܗ ܣܘܽܓܳܐܐ ܕܚܶܟܡܬܳܐ܆ ܘܟ݁ܰܕ݂ ܗܶܢܘܽܢ ܡܶܬܚܰܫܒܝܺܢ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ: ܗܳܝܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܢܶܕ݂ܢܰܚ ܛܢܳܢܳܐ ܒܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܠܳܐ ܣܳܒܪܝܺܢ ܗ̱ܘܰܘ ܥܠܰܝܗܘܽܢ: ܘܚܰܫܳܐ ܢܶܐܚܘܰܕ݂ ܠܰܐܝܢܳܐ ܕ݁ܰܫܠܶܐ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ. ܘܢܶܬܬܙܝܰܥܘܽܢ ܣܰܓ̈ܺܝܶܐ ܒܪܘܺܓܙܳܐ ܕܢܰܒܶܐܫܘܽܢ ܠܣܰܓ̈ܺܝܶܐ ܘܰܢܥܺܝܪܘܽܢ ܠܚܰܝ̈ܠܰܘܳܬܳܐ ܕ݁ܢܶܐܕܘܰܢ ܕܡܐ ܘܥܰܡܗܘܽܢ ܠܚܰܪܬ݁ܳܐ ܐܰܟ݂ܚܕܳܐ ܢܰܐܒ݂ܕ݂ܘܽܢ. ܘܢܶܗܘܶܐ ܒܶܗ ܒܗܰܘ ܙܰܒ݂ܢܳܐ ܢܶܬܚܙܶܐ
of which some will be vain and others will be confirmed. And honor will be turned into ignominy and strength will be humbled even to contempt and firmness will be dissolved and beauty will be in disdain. And many will say to many at that time: Where has the multitude of intelligence hidden itself and whither has the multitude of wisdom migrated? And while they will consider these things, then zeal will arise against those, of whom they did not think, and passion will seize him who was tranquil, and many will be moved because of wrath, to harm many, and they will excite armies, to shed blood, and with them in the end they will perish together. And it will be at that time, the change of times will appear manifestly to every man, for the reason that in all these times they were being defiled and were oppressing and each one was walking in his own works and they were not remembering the law of the Most High. Because of this fire will devour their thoughts and by flame the cares of their reins will be examined. For the judge will come and will not delay, because each of the inhabitants of the earth knew, when he was acting iniquitously, and they did not know my law because of their pride. But many will then weep truly over the living more than over the dead. And I answered and said: O what have you done, Adam, to all who have been begotten from you! And what will be said to the first Eve, who obeyed the serpent? For this whole


[p. 1146] {#po.0.p1146}
ܕܙܰܒ݂ܢܶܐ: ܚܠܦ ܕܰܒܟܘܽܠܗܘܽܢ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ
multitude goes to corruption, nor is a number given of those whom the fire devours! But I will speak again before you. You, Ruler Lord, know what is
ܘܐܳܙܠ̱ܝܢ ܗ̱ܘܰܘ ܐ̱ܢܫ ܐ̱ܢܫ ܒܰܥܒܳܕܰܘܗ̱ܝ: ܘܢܶܡܘܽܣܶܗ ܕܚܰܝܠܬ݁ܳܢܳܐ ܠܳܐ ܡܶܬ݁ܕܰܟܪܝܺܢ ܗ̱ܘܰܘ: ܡܶܛܽܠ ܗܳܢܳܐ ܢܘܽܪܳܐ ܡܰܚ̈ܫܒܳܬܗܘܽܢ ܐܳܟܠܳܐ: ܘܰܒܫܰܠܗܶܒ݂ܬܳܐ ܢܶܬܒܰܕ݁ܳܢ ܡܰܪ̈ܢܝܳܬܗܶܝܢ ܕܟܽܠܚܕܳܐܝܬܗܘܿܢ: ܢܺܐܬܶܐ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܕܰܝܳܢܳܐ ܘܠܳܐ ܢܶܫܬܰܘܚܰܪ. ܡܶܛܽܠ ܕܟܽܠ ܚܕ ܚܰܕ݂ ܡܶܢ ܥܳܡܘܽܪܶܝܗ ܕܐܰܪܥܳܝܐ ܝܳܕ݂ܰܥ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܟ݁ܰܕ ܡܥܰܘܶܠ: ܘܢܳܡܘܽܣܝ ܠܳܐ ܝܺܕܰܥܘ: ܡܶܛܽܠ ܪܳܡܘܽܬܗܘܽܢ. ܣܰܓܝ̈ܺܐܶܐ ܕܶܝܢ ܗܳܝܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܢܶܒܟܘܿܢ ܫܰܪܝܺܪܳܐܝܺܬ: ܥܰܠ ܚܰܝ̈ܶܐ ܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܚܰܬܝܰܪ ܕܥܰܠ ܡܦ̈ܳܬ݁ܶܐ. ܘܰܥܢܳܬ ܘܐܶܘܒܪܶܐ: ܐܰܘ̈ ܡܳܢܳܐ ܥܒܰܕ݂ܬ ܐܳܕܳܡ ܠܟܘܽܠܗܘܽܢ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܐܶܬ݂ܝܺܠܶܕ݂ܘ ܡܶܢܳܟܡ܆ ܘܡܳܢܳܐ ܢܶܬܶܐܡܰܪ ܠܚܰܝܘܳܐ ܩܰܕ݂ܡܳܝܬ݁ܳܐ ܕܰܠܚܶܘܝܳܐ ܐܶܫܬܰܡܥܰܝܬ܆ ܕܗܳܢܳܐ ܟ݁ܽܠܶܗ ܣܘܽܓܳܐܐ ܐܶܙܰܠ ܠܰܚܒܳܠܳܐ: ܘܠܰܝܬ ܡܶܢܝܳܢܳܐ ܠܰܐܚ̈ܠܶܝܢ ܕܢܘܽܪܳܐ ܐܳܟܠܳܐ. ܐܶܠܳܐ ܬܘܒ ܐܰܡܰܪ ܩܕ݂ܳܡܰܝܟ: ܐܰܢ̱ܬ ܡܳܪܝܳܐ ܡܳܪܝ ܝܳܕ݂ܥܰܬ ܡܶܕ݁ܶܡ ܒܰܒܪܝܺܬܳܟ݂. ܐܰܢ̱ܬ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܡܶܢ ܩܕ݂ܳܡ ܦܩܰܕ݂ܬ ܠܥܰܦ݁ܪܳܐ ܕܢܶܬ݁ܶܠ
in your creature. For you long ago commanded the dust, that it should give Adam, and you know


[p. 1147] {#po.0.p1147}


[p. 1148] {#po.0.p1148}
ܐܳܕܳܡ: ܘܐܰܢ̱ܬ ܝܳܕ݂ܰܥ ܐܰܢ̱ܬ ܡܶܢܝܳܢܗܘܽܢ ܕܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܐܶܬ݂ܝܺܠܶܕ݂ܘ ܡܶܢܶܗ: ܘܰܟܡܳܐ ܚܛܰܘ ܩܕ݂ܳܡܰܝܟ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕ݁ܰܗܘܰܘ ܘܠܳܐ ܐܰܘܕܝܽܘ ܠܳܟ݂ ܠܥܳܒ݂ܘܽܕܗܘܽܢ. ܘܥܰܠ ܟ݁ܽܠܗܶܝܢ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ: ܚܰܪܬܗܘܽܢ ܡܰܟܣܳܐ ܠܗܘܿܢ: ܘܢܳܡܘܽܣܳܟ ܗܰܘ ܕܰܥܒܰܪܘ ܦܳܪܰܥ ܠܗܘܢ ܒܚܰܘܡܳܟ. ܗܳܫܳܐ ܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܢܰܪܦܶܐ ܠܪ̈ܰܫܝܳܥܶܐ ܘܥܰܠ ܙܰܕ݁̈ܝܺܩܶܐ ܢܶܒܥܶܐ. ܘܐܶܬܢܶܐ ܛܘ̈ܒܝܗܘܿܢ ܘܠܳܐ ܐܶܫܬܘܰܩ ܬܶܫܒܘܽܚܬܗܘܽܢ ܠܰܡܫܰܒܳܚܘܽ ܗܳܝ ܕ݁ܰܢܛܝܺܪܳܐ ܠܗܘܿܢ. ܫܰܪܝܺܪܳܐܝܺܬ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܐܰܝܟ ܕ݁ܰܒ݂ܙܰܒܢܳܐ ܩܰܠܝܺܠ ܒ݁ܗܳܢܳܐ ܥܳܠܡܳܐ ܕܥܳܒܰܪ ܕ݁ܚܰܝܺܝܬܽܘܢ ܐܢ̱ܬܽܘܢ ܒܶܝܗ: ܠܶܐܘܺܝܳܐ ܣܰܓܝܳܐܬ݂ܳܐ
the number of those who have been begotten from him and how greatly they have sinned before you, those who were and have not confessed you as their maker. And concerning all these, their end will convict them and your law, which they have transgressed, will repay them on your day. [But now let us dismiss the iniquitous and let us inquire about the just. And I will narrate their blessedness and I will not be silent to celebrate their glory, which is reserved for them. For truly as for a little time in this world, which passes away, in which you live, you have endured much labor, so in that world, which has no end, you will receive great light.]


[p. 1149] {#po.0.p1149}
ܣܰܝܒܰܪܬܘܽܢ: ܗܳܟܰܢܳܐ ܒܥܳܠܡܳܐ ܗܰܘ ܕܣܘܽܦ ܠܝܬ ܠܶܗ: ܬܩܰܒܠܘܽܢ ܢܘܽܗܪܳܐ ܪܰܒܳܐ܀ ܒܚܰܪܬܳܐ ܕܝܢ ܬܘܽܒ ܐܶܒܥܶܐ ܡܶܢܳܟ ܚܰܝܠܬ݁ܳܢܳܐ: ܘܐܶܒܥܶܐ ܪ̈ܰܚܡܶܐ ܡܶܢ ܗܰܘ ܕܰܥܒ݁ܰܕ݂ ܟܽܠ. ܒܐܺܝܕ݂ܳܐ ܟ݁ܰܝ ܕܡܘܽܬܳܐ ܢܺܐܚܘܽܢ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܣܰܝܒܺܝܢ ܒܚܰܘܡܳܝܟ܆ ܐܰܘ ܐܰܝܟ݁ܰܢܳܝܐ ܡܩܰܝܡܶܐ ܙܝܽܘܙܗܘܽܢ ܕܡܶܢ ܒܳܬܰܪ ܗܳܝܕܶܝܢ܆ ܠܗܳܕ݂ܶܐ ܟܰܝ ܨܘܽܪܬܳܐ ܗܳܕܶܐ ܢܳܣܒܝܢ ܗܳܝܕܶܝܢ܆ ܘܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܗܰܕ݁ܳܡܶܐ ܕܐܣܘܽܪ̈ܶܐ ܠܒܘܫܢ: ܗܳܢܘܽܢ ܕܗܳܫܿܶܐ ܒܒ̈ܝܺܫܳܬܳܐ ܐܝܺܬܰܝܗܘܽܢ ܘܰܒܗܘܽܢ ܡܶܫܬܰܡܠܝܳܢ ܒܝܺܫܳܬܳܐܢ܆ ܐܰܘ ܪܰܡܳܐ ܡܚܰܠܶܦ ܐܰܢ̱ܬ
XLIX. Nevertheless I will again beseech you, Most High, and I will ask for mercy from you, who made all things. In what form will the living live on your day? Or how will their later splendor remain! Will they resume this figure of this time then and will they clothe these members of bonds, which are now in evils and in them evils are fulfilled, or will you perhaps change those things which were in the world, just as also the world?


[p. 1150] {#po.0.p1150}
ܘܰܥܢܳܐ ܘܐܶܡܰܪ ܠܝܿ: ܫܡܰܥ ܒܰܪܘܽܟ ܡܶܠܬ݁ܳܐ ܗܳܕܶܐ ܘܰܒܬܘܽܒ ܒܥܘܽܗܕ݁ܳܢܶܗ ܕܠܶܒܳܟ ܟ݁ܽܠ ܕܝܳܠܶܦ ܐܰܢ̱ܬ. ܡܦܰܢܳܝܘܽ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܡܦܰܢܝܳܐ ܐܰܪܥܳܐ ܗܳܝܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܠܡ̈ܺܝܬ݁ܶܐ ܕܰܡܩܰܒܠܳܐ ܗܳܟܶܢܳܐ ܕܐܶܛܰܪ ܐܶܢܘܽܢ: ܟܰܕ݂ ܡܶܕ݁ܶܡ ܠܳܐ ܡܚܰܠܦܳܐ ܒܨܘܽܪܰܬܗܘܽܢ: ܘܐܰܝܟ ܕܐܰܫܠܡܶܬ ܐܶܢܘܽܢ ܠܳܗ: ܗܳܟ݁ܰܢܳܐ ܐܳܦ
L. And he answered and said to me: Hear, Baruch, this word and write in the memory of your heart all that you will learn. For by restitution the earth will then restore the dead, whom it has received now, that it may guard them, changing nothing in their figure and, as I handed them over to it, so also it will set them forth. For then it will be necessary to show to those who live, that the dead have lived, and that those who have departed have come.
ܡܩܺܝܡܳܐ ܠܗܘܽܢ. ܗܳܝܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܡܶܬܒ݂ܥܶܐ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܠܰܡܚܰܘܳܝܘܽ ܠܰܐܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܚܰܝܝܺܢ ܕ݁ܰܚܝܰܘ ܡ̈ܺܝܬܶܐ: ܘܐܰܬܰܘ ܗܳܢܘܽܢ ܕܐܶܙܰܠܘ ܗ̱ܘܰܘ. ܘܢܶܗܘܶܐ ܡܳܐ ܕܐܶܫܬܰܘܕ݁ܰܥܘ ܚܰܕ݂ ܠܚܰܕ݂ ܙܰܕܺܝܩܶܝܢ ܕ݁ܗܳܫܳܐ ܝܳܕܥܺܝܢ: ܗܳܝܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܢܶܥܫܰܢ ܕܺܝܢܳܐ ܘܢܺܐܬܶܝܢ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ
And it will be, when they know one another, those whom they now know, then judgment will grow strong and those things which were predicted will come.


[p. 1151] {#po.0.p1151}
ܕ݁ܰܡܩܰܕ݁̈ܡܳܢ ܐܶܬ݂ܡܰܠܰܠܝܺܢ܀ ܘܰܢܗܘܶܐ ܡܶܢ ܒܳܬ݁ܰܪ ܕܥܳܒܰܪ ܗܰܘ ܝܰܘܡܳܐ ܕܩܰܝܡܳܐ: ܗܳܝܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܡܶܢ ܒܳܬܰܪ ܟܶܢ ܡܶܬܚܰܠܦܳܐ ܕܡܘܳܬܗܘܽܢ ܕܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܡܶܬܚܰܝܒܺܝܢ: ܐܳܦ݂ ܬܶܫܒܘܽܚܬܗܘܽܢ ܕܙܰܕܺܝܩܶܝܢ ܕܡܶܙܕܰܕܩܝܢ. ܡܶܬܥܰܒܕ݂ܳܐ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܕܡܘܳܬܗܘܽܢ ܕܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕ݁ܗܳܫܳܐ ܡܰܪܫܥܝܢ ܒܺܝܫ ܡܶܢ ܡܳܝܐ ܕܐܝܺܬܶܝܗ: ܐܰܝܟ݂ ܕܰܢܣܰܝܒܪܘܽܢ ܬܰܫܢܺܝܩܳܝܐ. ܐܳܦ݂ ܬܶܫܒܘܽܚܬܗܘܽܢ ܕܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܗܳܫܳܐ ܐܶܙܕܰܕܰܩܘ ܒܢܳܡܘܽܣܝ: ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܕܐܺܝܬ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܠܗܘܽܢ ܣܘܽܟܳܠܬܳܐ ܒܚܰܝ̈ܰܝܗܘܽܢ: ܘܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܕ݁ܰܢܨܰܒ݂ܘ ܒܠܶܒܗܘܽܢ ܥܶܩܳܪܳܗ ܕܚܶܟܡܬܳܐ. ܗܳܝܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܢܶܫܬ݁ܰܒ݁ܰܚ ܙܝܳܘܗܘܽܢ ܒܫܘܽܚܠܳܦܶܐ: ܘܐܶܬܗܦܺܝܟ ܕܡܘܽܬܳܐ ܕܐܰܦܰܝ̈ܗܘܽܢ ܠܢܘܽܗܪܳܐ ܕܝܳܐܝܘܽܬܗܘܽܢ: ܕܢܶܫܟܚܘܽܢ ܕܢܶܣܒܘܽܢ ܘܰܢܩܰܒܠܘܽܢ ܥܳܠܡܳܐ ܕܠܳܐ ܡܳܐܶܬ݂ ܕܗܳܝܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܡܠܺܝܟ ܠܗܘܽܢ. ܒܗܳܕ݂ܶܐ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܝܰܬܝܺܪܳܐܝܰܬ ܢܶܬܬܰܢܚܘܽܢ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ
LI. And it will be, after that appointed day has passed, then afterwards the form of those who have been condemned will be changed, and the glory of those who have been justified. For the form of those who now act impiously will become worse than it is, that it may sustain punishment. Also the glory of those who now have been justified by my law, who had intelligence in their life, and who planted in their heart the root of wisdom: then their splendor will be glorified in changes and the figure of their face will be turned into the light of their beauty, that they may be able to possess and receive the world, which does not die, promised to them then. For this especially


[p. 1152] {#po.0.p1152}
ܕܐܳܬ݁ܶܝܢ ܗܳܝܕ݁ܶܝܢ: ܥܰܠ ܕܐܰܣܠܺܝܘ ܢܳܡܘܽܣܝ ܘܣܰܟ݁ܰܪܘ ܐܶܕ݂ܢܰܝ̈ܗܘܽܢ ܕܠܳܐ ܢܶܫܡܥܘܽܢ ܚܶܟܡܬܳܐ ܘܠܳܐ ܢܩܰܒܠܘܽܢ ܣܘܽܟܳܠܳܐ ܟ݁ܰܕ݂ ܗܳܟܝܶܠ ܢܶܚܙܘܽܢ ܠܰܐܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܗܳܫܳܐ ܐܶܬܬܪܺܝܡܘ ܥܠܰܝܗܘܽܢ ܕܗܳܝܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܡܶܬܬܪܺܝܡܝܺܢ ܘܡܶܫܬܰܒܚܺܝܢ ܝܰܬܝܰܪ ܡܶܢܗܘܽܢ ܘܡܶܬܚܰܠܦܺܝܢ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܘܗܳܠܶܝܢ: ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܠܙܝܰܘܳܐ ܕܡܰܠܰܐܟ̈ܶܐ: ܘܗܳܢܘܽܢ ܠܬܶܡܗܳܐ ܕܚܶܙܘܳܢܶܐ ܘܠܰܚܙܳܬܳܐ ܕܕܶܡܘܳܬܳܐ: ܝܰܬܝܺܪܳܐܝܺܬ ܢܫܘܽܚܘܽܢ. ܠܘܽܩܕ݂ܰܡ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܚܳܙܶܝܢ: ܘܒܳܬ݁ܰܪܟ݁ܶܢ ܐܳܙܠ̱ܝܺܢ ܕܢܶܫܬܰܢܩܘܽܢ. ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܕܐܶܬܦܰܨܚܽܘ ܒܰܥܒܳܕ݂ܰܝ̈ܗܘܽܢ ܘܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܰܗܘܳܐ ܠܗܘܽܢ ܗܳܫܳܐ ܢܳܡܘܽܣܳܐ ܣܰܒܪܳܐ ܘܣܰܟܘܽܠܬܳܢܘܽܬܳܐ ܣܘܽܟܳܠܳܐ ܘܚܶܟܡܬ݁ܳܐ ܗܰܝܡܳܢܘܽܬܳܐ: ܢܶܬܚܰܙܝܳܢ ܠܗܘܽܢ ܬܡܺܝܗܳܬܳܐ ܒܙܰܒܢܗܶܝܢ. ܚܳܙܶܝܢ ܓܶܝܪ ܗܰܘ ܥܳܠܡܳܐ ܕܠܳܐ ܡܶܬܚܙܶܐ ܠܗܘܢ ܗܳܫܳܐ: ܘܢܶܚܙܘܽܢ ܠܙܰܒ݂ܢܳܐ ܕܗܳܫܳܐ ܟܣܶܝܐ ܡܶܢܗܘܽܢ. ܘܬܘܒ ܠܳܐ ܢܰܥܬ݁ܶܩ ܐܶܢܘܽܢ ܙܰܒܢ̈ܶܐ. ܒܰܡܪ̈ܰܘܡܰܘܗ̱ܝ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܕܗܰܘ ܥܳܠܡܳܝܐ ܢܶܥܡܪܘܽܢ ܘܡܶܬܕܰܡܺܝܢ ܠܡܰܠܰܐܟ݁ܶܐ ܘܡܶܬܟܰܚܡܝܺܢ ܠܟܰܘ̈ܟ݁ܒܶܐ: ܘܗܳܘܶܝܢ ܡܶܬܚܰܠܦܝܺܢ ܒܟܘّܠ ܕܡܘܽܬܳܐ ܕܨܳܒܶܝܢ: ܡܶܢ ܫܘܽܦܪܳܐ ܠܚܳܐܘܽܬܳܐ: ܘܡܶܢ ܢܘܽܗܪܳܐ ܠܙܝܰܘܳܐ ܕܬܶܫܒܘܽܚܬܳܐ. ܢܶܬܦܰܫܛܘܽܢ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܩܕܳܡܝܗܘܽܢ ܦܬܳܝܰܬ̈ܗܘܽܢ ܕܦܰܪܕܰܝܣܳܐ: ܘܢܶܬܚܰܘܶܐ ܠܗܘܽܢ ܫܘܽܦܪܳܐ ܕܪܰܒܘܽܬܳܐ ܕܚܰܝ̈ܳܬ݂ܳܐ ܕܰܬܚܶܝܬ ܬ݁ܪܽܘܢܳܘܣ. ܘܟܘܽܠܗܘܿܢ ܚܰܝܠܰܘܳܬܳܐ ܕܡܰܠܰܐܟ݁ܶܐ ܕܐܰܚܺܝܕ݂ܝܺܢ ܗܳܫܳܐ ܒܥܶܡܠܳܬܝ: ܐܰܝܟ ܕܠܳܐ ܢܶܬܚܙܘܽܢ: ܘܐܰܚܺܝܕܝܺܢ ܒܦܘܺܩܕ݂ܳܢܳܝܐ ܐܰܝܟ݂ ܕܰܢܩܘܽܡܘܽܢ ܒܐܰܬܪ̈ܰܘܳܬܗܘܽܢ ܥܕ݂ܰܡܳܐ ܕܺܐܬ݂ܶܐ ܡܶܐܬܝܺܬܗܘܿܢ. ܘܰܡܝܰܬܪܘܳܬܳܐ ܕܶܝܢ ܬ݁ܶܗܘܶܐ ܗܳܝܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܒܙܰܕ̈ܝܺܩܶܐ ܝܰܬܝܰܪ ܡܶܢ ܕܰܒܡܰܠܰܐܟ̈ܶܐ. ܢܩܰܒܠܘܽܢ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܩܰܕ݁̈ܡܳܝܶܐ ܠܰܐܚܪܳܝܶܐ ܠܗܳܢܘܽܢ ܕ݁ܰܡܣܰܟܶܝܢ ܗ̱ܘܵܘ ܠܗܘܽܢ: ܘܰܐܚܪ̈ܳܝܶܐ ܠܰܐܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܫܳܡܥܝܺܢ ܗ̱ܘܰܘ ܕܰܥܒܰܪܘ. ܐܶܬܦܰܨܚܺܝܘ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܡܶܢ ܗܳܢܳܐ ܥܳܠܡܳܐ ܕܐܘܽܠܨܳܢܳܐ ܘܐܰܢܺܝܚܘ ܡܶܢܗܘܽܢ ܛܰܥܢܳܐ ܕܥܳܩ̈ܳܬܳܐ. ܒܡܳܢܳܝܐ ܗܳܟܶܢ ܐܰܘܒܶܕ݂ܘ ܢܰܦܫܗܘܽܢ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܒܰܐܪܥܳܐ ܗܘܰܘ ܘܒܰܡܢܳܝܐ
will those groan, who will come then, that they despised my law and stopped their ears, that they might not hear wisdom, nor receive intelligence. When therefore they will see those, over whom they now exalted themselves, those then to be exalted and glorified more than themselves and themselves and those to be changed: those into the splendor of angels and themselves into the stupor of visions, and into the sight of figures: they will waste away most of all. For they will see first and afterwards they will go, that they may be tormented. But those who have been saved in their works and for whom the law was now hope and intelligence expectation, and wisdom trust: wonders will appear to them in their time. For they will see the world, which is invisible to them now, and they will see the time, which is now hidden from them. And time will no longer make them old. For they will dwell in the heights of that world and will be assimilated to the angels and will be equaled to the stars, and they will be transmuted into every form, which they will have wished, from beauty into comeliness and from light into the splendor of glory. For the breadths of paradise will be expanded before them and the beauty of the majesty of the living ones, which are under the throne, will be shown to them, and all the hosts of the angels, who are now held back by my word, that they may not be seen, and are held back by precept, that they may stand in their places, until their coming comes. But excellence will be then in the just more than that which [is] in the angels. For the first will receive the last, those whom they were expecting, and the last those, whom they heard had passed away. For they have been freed from this world of tribulation and have laid down the burden of straits. By what [thing] then did men lose their life and with what did they exchange their soul, those who were on the earth? For then they chose for themselves this time, which cannot pass beyond straits, and they chose for themselves a time, whose exits are full of groans and evils, and they denied the world, which does not make old those who arrive at it, and they rejected also the time of glory, that they might not arrive at the honor, which I predicted to you.


[p. 1153] {#po.0.p1153}


[p. 1154] {#po.0.p1154}


[p. 1155] {#po.0.p1155}
ܚܰܠܶܦܘ ܢܰܦܫܗܘܽܢ. ܗܳܝܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܓܒܰܝܘ ܠܗܘܽܢ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܙܰܒ݂ܢܳܐ ܗܳܢܳܐ ܕܰܠܒܰܪ ܡܶܢ ܥܳܩ̈ܳܬܳܐ ܕܢܶܥܒܰܪ ܠܳܐ ܡܶܫܟ݁ܰܚ: ܘܰܓܒܰܘ ܠܗܘܽܢ ܙܰܒ݂ܢܳܐ ܗܰܘ ܕܡܰܦܩܳܢܰܘ̈ܗ̱ܝ ܬ݁ܶܐܢܚܳܬܳܐ ܘܒܺܝܫܬ݁ܳܐ ܡܠܶܝܢ: ܘܰܟ݂ܦܰܪܘ ܒܥܳܠܡܳܐ ܕܠܳܐ ܡܰܥܬ݁ܶܩ ܠܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܐܬܶܝܢ ܘܐܰܣܠܚܽܘ ܘܙܰܒܢܳܐ ܕܬܶܫܒܘܽܚܬܳܐ: ܕܠܳܐ ܢܺܐܬܘܰܢ ܠܐܺܝܩܳܪܳܐ ܕܩܰܕܡܶܬ ܐܶܡܪܶܐ ܠܳܟ݂܀ ܘܰܒܢܰܝܬ ܘܐܶܡܪܶܬ: ܐܰܝܟ݁ܢܳܐ ܛܳܥܶܝܢܰܢ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܗܳܝܕ݂ܶܝܢ ܘܳܝܳܐ ܢܛܝܰܪ ܠܗܘܽܢ܆ ܘܰܠܡܳܢܳܐ
LII. And I answered and said: How will we forget those, for whom then woe is reserved


[p. 1156] {#po.0.p1156}
ܗܳܟܝܶܠ ܡܶܬܰܐܒ݂ܠܺܝܢܰܢ ܬܘܽܒ ܥܰܠ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܡܺܝܬܝܺܢ܆ ܐܰܘ ܠܡܳܢܳܐ ܒܳܟܶܝܢܰܢ ܥܰܠ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܐܳܙܠ݈ܝܺܢ ܠܰܫܝܘܽܠ܆ ܢܶܬܢܰܛܪܳܢ ܐܘܽܠܝ̈ܳܬܳܐ ܠܪܺܝܫܶܝܗ ܕܗܰܘ ܬܰܫܢܺܝܩܳܝܐ ܕܰܥܬܝܺܝܕ: ܘܢܶܬܬܣܺܝܡܳܢ ܕܶܡ̈ܥܶܝܐ ܠܡܶܐܬ݂ܝܺܬ݁ܶܗ ܕܗܰܘ ܐܰܒܕ݂ܳܢܳܐ ܕܗܳܝܕܶܝܢ. ܐܶܠܳܐ ܐܳܦ ܠܘܽܩܒܰܠ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܐܰܡܰܪ. ܘܙܰܕ݁ܺܝܩܶܐ ܡܳܢܳܐ ܢܶܥܒܕܘܽܢ ܗܳܫܳܐ܆ ܨܒܰܘ ܒܚܰܫܳܐ ܕܚܳܫܚܺܝܢ ܐܢ̱ܬܘܽܢ ܗܳܟܳܝܐ. ܘܰܠܡܳܢܳܐ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܚܳܝܪܝܺܢ ܐܢ̱ܬܘܽܢ ܒܡܶܨܛܰܠܚܳܢܘܽܬܗܘܽܢ ܕܣܳܢܰܐܝ̈ܟܘܽܢ܆ ܛܰܝܶܒܘ ܢܰܦܫܟܘܽܢ ܠܡܶܡܕܶܡ ܕܰܢܛܝܺܪ ܠܟܘܽܢ: ܘܥܰܬ݁ܶܕ݂ܘ ܢܰܦ̈ܫܳܬܟܘܽܢ
is? And why therefore do we mourn again for those who die? Or why do we weep for those who depart into hell? Let lamentations be reserved for the beginning of that torment, which is to be, and let tears be stored up for the coming of the destruction of that time. But also against these things I will speak. And what do the just do now? [Delight in the passions, which you suffer now. For why do you look into the decline of your enemies? Prepare your soul for those things, which are reserved for you, and prepare your souls for the reward, which is laid up for you.] And when I had said these things, I fell asleep there.


[p. 1157] {#po.0.p1157}
ܠܐܰܓ݂ܪܳܐ ܕܣܺܝܡ ܠܟܘܽܢ. ܘܟܰܕ ܐܶܡܪܶܬ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ: ܕ݁ܶܡܟܶܬ݂ ܬܰܡܳܢ܀ ܘܰܚܙܝܺܬ ܚܶܙܘܳܐ: ܘܗܳܐ ܥܢܳܢܳܐ ܚܕ݂ܳܐ ܣܳܠܩܳܐ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܡܶܢ ܝܰܡܳܐ ܪܰܒܳܐ ܣܰܓܝ̈: ܘܚܳܐܰܪ ܗ̱ܘܝܺܬ ܒܳܗ. ܘܗܳܐ ܡܠܝܳܐ ܗ̱ܘܳܬ ܡܰܝ̈ܳܐ ܚܶܘܳܪܶܐ ܘܐܘܽܟܳܡܶܐ: ܘܓܰܘ̈ܢܶܐ ܣܰܓܺܝܐܶܐ ܐܝܺܬ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܒܗܘܽܢ ܒܡܰܝ̈ܳܐ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܘܐܰܝܟ ܕܡܘܽܠܳܐ ܕܒܰܪܩܳܐ ܪܰܒ݁ܳܐ ܡܶܬܚܙܶܐ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܒܪܺܝܫܳܗ. ܘܰܚܙܝܺܬܳܗ ܠܰܥܢܳܢܳܐ ܗܳܝ ܕܥܳܒܪܳܐ ܚܰܪܺܝܦܳܐܝܺܬ ܒܪܶܗܛܶܐ ܩܰܠܺܝܠܶܐ: ܘܟ݁ܰܣܝܰܬܳܗ ܠܟ݁ܽܠܳܗ ܐܰܪܥܳܐ. ܘܰܗܘܳܐ ܡܶܢ ܒܳܬܰܪ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ: ܫܰܪܝܰܬ ܗ̱ܘܳܬ ܥܢܳܢܳܐ ܗܳܝ ܡܰܡܛܪܳܐ ܥܰܠ ܐܰܪܥܳܐ ܡܰܝ̈ܳܐ ܕܐܝܺܬ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܒܳܗ. ܘܰܚܙܝܺܬ ܕܠܳܐ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܚܕ݂ܳܐ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܕܡܘܽܬܗܘܽܢ ܕܡܰܝ̈ܳܐ ܗܳܢܘܽܢ ܕܢܳܚܬܝܺܢ ܗ̱ܘܰܘ ܡܶܢܳܗ. ܥܘܽܪܳܝܳܐ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܩܰܕ݂ܡܳܝܳܐ ܐܘܰܟܳܡܝܺܢ ܗ̱ܘܰܘ ܣܰܓܝ̈ ܥܕ݂ܰܡܳܐ ܠܥܶܕ݁ܳܢܳܐ. ܘܡܶܢ ܒܳܬܰܪܟ݁ܶܢ ܚܙܝܺܬ ܕܢܳܗܪܝܺܢ ܗ̱ܘܰܘ ܡܰܝ̈ܳܐ: ܐܶܠܳܐ ܠܳܐ ܣܰܓ݁ܝܺܐܝܺܢ ܗ̱ܘܰܘ. ܘܡܶܢ ܒܳܬܰܪ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܬܘܽܒ ܚܙܝܺܬ ܐܘܰܟܳܡܶܐ ܘܡܶܢ ܒܳܬܰܪ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܬܘܽܒ ܢܰܗܝܺܪ̈ܶܐ. ܘܬܘܽܒ ܐܘܰܟܳܡܶܐ ܘܬܘܽܒ ܢܰܗܝܺܪܶܐ. ܗܳܕܶܐ ܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܗܘܳܬ ܥܕ݂ܰܡܳܐ ܠܬܰܪܬ݁ܰܥܺܣܪ݁ܶܐ ܙܰܒܢ̈ܺܝܢ. ܐܶܠܳܐ ܐܘܰܟܳܡܶܐ ܣܰܓ݁ܝܺܐܝܺܢ ܗ̱ܘܰܘ ܒܟܽܠܙܒܰܢ ܡܶܢ ܢܰܗܝܺܪ̈ܶܐ. ܘܰܗܘܳܐ ܒܫܘܽܠܳܡܳܗ ܕܰܥܢܳܢܳܐ: ܘܗܳܐ ܐܰܡܛܪܰܬ ܡܰܝ̈ܳܐ ܐܘܰܟܳܡܶܐ ܘܚܶܫܘܽܟܝܺܢ ܗ̱ܘܰܘ ܝܰܬܝܰܪ ܡܶܢ ܟܽܠܗܘܽܢ ܐܰܝ̈ܠܶܝܢ ܩܰܕ݁̈ܡܳܝܶܐ ܕܰܗܘܰܘ ܘܢܘܽܪܳܐ ܡܚܰܠܛܳܐ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܒܗܘܽܢ. ܘܐܰܝܟܳܐ ܕ݁ܢܳܚܬܝܺܢ ܗ̱ܘܰܘ ܡܰܝ̈ܳܐ ܗܳܢܘܽܢ: ܥܳܒܕ݁ܝܺܢ ܗ̱ܘܰܘ ܚܒܺܝܠܳܐ ܘܐܰܒܕ݂ܳܢܳܐ. ܘܰܚܙܝܺܬ ܡܶܢ ܒܳܬܰܪ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܠܗܰܘ ܒܰܪܩܳܐܝܳܐ ܕܰܚܙܝܺܬ ܗ̱ܘܝܺܬ ܒܪܺܝܫܳܗ ܕܰܥܢܳܢܳܐ ܕܐܰܚܕ݂ܳܗ ܘܐܰܚܬ݁ܳܗ ܒܕ݂ܰܡܝܳܐ ܠܰܐܪܥܳܐ. ܘܝܰܬܝܺܪܳܐܝܺܬ ܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܢܶܗܰܪ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܒ݁ܰܪܩܳܐ ܗܰܘ: ܐܰܝܟ ܕܢܰܢܗܰܪ ܠܟ݁ܽܠܳܗ ܐܰܪܥܳܐ. ܘܐܰܣܝܳܐ ܠܗܳܢܘܢ ܐܰܬܪܰܘܳܬ݂ܳܐ ܐܰܝܟ݁ܳܐ ܕܰܢܚܶܬܘ ܡܰܝ̈ܳܐ ܐ̱ܚܪ̈ܳܝܶܐ ܘܚܰܒܶܠܘ. ܘܐܰܚܕ݂ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܠܟ݁ܽܠܳܗ ܐܰܪܥܳܐ ܘܐܶܫܬܰܠܰܛ ܥܠܶܝܗ. ܘܰܚܙܝܺܬ ܡܶܢ ܒܳܬܰܪ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ: ܘܗܳܐ ܬܪܶܥܣܰܪ̈ ܢܰܗܪ̈ܰܘܳܢ ܣܳܠܩܺܝܢ ܗ݈ܘܰܘ ܡܶܢ ܝܰܡܳܐ ܘܰܚܕ݂ܝܰܪܝܺܢ ܗ̱ܘܰܘ ܠܶܗ ܠܗܰܘ ܒ݁ܰܪܩܳܐ ܘܡܶܫܬܰܥܒܕ݁ܝܺܢ ܗ̱ܘܰܘ ܠܶܗ. ܘܐܶܢܳܐ ܡܶܢ ܕ݁ܶܚܠܰܬܝ ܐܶܬܬܥܝܳܪܶܬ܀
LIII. And I saw a vision; and behold, one cloud was ascending from the sea very great, and I was looking at it, and behold, it was full of white and black waters and many colors were in these waters and as it were the likeness of a great lightning was appearing in its extremity. And I saw that cloud, which was passing quickly with swift courses and covered the whole earth. And it happened after these things, that cloud had begun to rain upon the earth the waters, which were in it. And I saw that there was not one likeness of the waters, which were descending from it. For first in the beginning they were very black until a time, and afterwards I saw the waters become clear, but they were not many, and afterwards again I saw black and afterwards again clear and again black and again clear. But this happened up to twelve times, but the black were always more than the clear. And it was at the end of the cloud, and behold, it rained black waters, and they were darker than all the previous waters, and fire was mixed in them and, where those waters were descending, they were bringing corruption and destruction. And I saw after these things the lightning, which I had seen in the extremity of the cloud, which seized it and drew it down as far as the earth. But this lightning had shone most of all, so that it might illuminate the earth, and heal those regions, where the last waters had descended and had brought ruin. And it was occupying the whole earth and it dominated it. And I saw after these things, and behold, twelve rivers were ascending from the sea and were surrounding that lightning and were subject to it. And I, because of my fear, awoke.


[p. 1158] {#po.0.p1158}


[p. 1159] {#po.0.p1159}


[p. 1160] {#po.0.p1160}

### THE PRAYER OF BARUCH
ܨܠܘܺܠܳܐ ܕܒܰܪܘܵܟ݂ ܘܰܒܥܺܝܬ ܡܶܢ ܚܰܝܠܬ݁ܳܢܳܐ ܘܐܶܡܪܶܬ: ܐܰܢ̱ܬ ܡܳܪܝܳܐ ܒܰܠܚܘܽܕܰܝܟ ܝܳܕ݂ܰܥ ܐܰܢ̱ܬ ܩܕ݂ܳܡܳܝܳܐ
LIV. And I besought the Most High and said: You, Lord, alone foreknow the heights
ܕܥܳܠܡܳܝܳܐ: ܘܡܶܕ݁ܶܡ ܕܰܒܙܰܒ݂ܢ̈ܶܐ ܓܳܕܶܫ ܡܰܝܬ݁ܶܐ ܐܰܢ̱ܬ ܒܶܡܠܬ݁ܳܟ݂܂ ܘܠܘܰܩܒܰܠ ܥܒܺܝܕ݂ܰܝܗܘܽܢ ܕܥܳܡܘܽܪܶܝܗ ܕܐܰܪܥܳܐ ܡܣܰܪܗܒܰܬ ܪ̈ܶܫܰܝܗܘܽܢ ܕܙܰܒ̈ܢܶܐ: ܘܣܰܘܦܗܘܢ ܕܥܺܕ݁ܳܢܶܐ ܐܰܢ̱ܬ ܒܰܠܚܘܽܕܰܝܟ ܝܕ݂ܰܥ ܐܰܢ̱ܬ. ܗܰܘ ܕ݁ܡܶܕܶܡ ܠܳܐ ܥܛܶܠ ܠܶܗ: ܐܶܠܳܐ ܟܘّܠ ܡܶܕܶܡ ܦܫܝܩܳܐܝܺܬ ܒܪܶܡܙܳܐ ܥܳܒܶܕ݂ ܐܰܢ̱ܬ. ܗܰܘ ܕܥܘܽܡ̈ܩܶܐ ܐܰܝܟ ܪܰܘܡܶܐ ܐܳܬܶܝܢ ܠܘܳܬܳܟ݂: ܘܪ̈ܶܫܰܝ ܥܳܠܶܡܐ ܠܡܶܠܬ݁ܳܟ݂ ܡܫܰܡܫܝܺܢ. ܗܰܘ ܕܓܳܠܶܐ ܠܰܐܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܕܚܠܺܝܢ ܠܶܗ ܡܶܕܶܡ ܕܰܥܬܝܰܕ݂ ܠܗܘܽܢ: ܕܡܶܢ ܗܳܪܟ݁ܳܐ ܢܒܰܝܰܐ ܐܶܢܘܽܢ. ܡܰܘܕܰܥ ܓܰܒܳܪ̈ܘܳܬ݂ܳܐ ܠܰܐܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܠܳܐ ܝܳܕܥܺܝܢ: ܬ݁ܳܪܰܥ ܣܝܳܓ݂ܳܐ ܠܰܐܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܠܳܐ ܡܦܰܣܩܺܝܢ. ܘܡܰܒܢܗܰܪ ܚܶܫ̈ܘܽܟܳܬ݁ܳܐ ܘܓ݁ܳܠܶܐ ܟ݁ܰܣܝܳܬܳܐ ܠܰܐܝܠܶܝܢ ܕ݁ܰܕ݂ܠܳܐ ܡܘܽܡ ܐܶܢܘܽܢ: ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕ݁ܰܒ݂ܗܰܝܡܰܢܘܽܬ݂ܳܐ ܐܶܫܬܰܥܒܰܕ݂ܘ ܠܳܟ݂ ܘܰܠܢܳܡܘܽܣܳܟ݂. ܐܰܢ̱ܬ ܚܰܘܝܺܬ ܠܥܰܒܕ݂ܳܟ ܚܶܙܘܳܢܳܐ ܗܳܢܳܐ: ܓܰܠܳܐ ܠܺܝ ܐܳܦ ܦܘܽܫܳܩܶܗ. ܝܳܕ݂ܰܥ ܐ̱ܢܳܐ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܕܥܰܠ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܰܒܥܺܝܬ ܡܶܢܳܟ݂ ܢܶܣܒܶܬ ܦܶܬ݂ܓ݂ܳܡܳܐ ܘܥܰܠ ܡܶܕܶܡ ܕܰܒܥܺܝܬ ܓ݁ܠܰܝܬ ܠܺܝ ܘܐܰܘܕܰܥܬܰܢܝ: ܒ݁ܙܺܝܢܳܐ ܩܺܝܠܳܐ ܐܶܫܰܒܚܳܟ݂: ܐܰܘ ܡܶܢ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܗܰܕ݁ܳܡܶܐ ܐܰܣܶܩ ܠܳܟ ܬܶܫܒܘܽܚܬ݁ܳܐ ܘܗܘܽܠܳܠܳܐ. ܐܶܢ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܢܶܗܘܘܽܢ ܟܘܽܠܗܽܘܢ ܦܘܽܡ̈ܶܐ: ܘܟܘܽܠܗܽܘܢ ܕܪ̈ܝܺܫܰܝ ܩܳܠ̈ܶܐ: ܐܳܦ݂ ܠܳܐ ܗܳܟ݂ܰܢܳܐ ܡܶܫܟ݁ܰܚ ܐ̱ܢܳܐ ܕܐܶܦܪܥܳܟ݂ ܬܶܫܒܘܽܚܬ݁ܳܐ ܐܰܘ ܕܐܶܫܰܒܚܳܟ݂ ܐܰܝܟ݂ ܕܙܳܕ݂ܶܩ ܘܠܳܐ ܬܶܫܒܘܽܚܬ݁ܳܟ݂܂ ܡܶܫܟ݁ܰܚ ܐ̱ܢܳܐ ܕܐܶܬܢܳܐ ܐܰܘ ܕܐܰܡܰܪ ܗܶܕ݂ܪܳܐ ܕܝܳܐܝܘܽܬ݁ܳܟ݂̇ ܡܳܢܳܐ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܐܶܢܳܐ ܒܰܒܢܰܝ̈ܢܳܫܐ: ܐܰܘ ܡܳܢܳܐ ܡܶܬܚܫܶܒ ܐ̱ܢܳܐ ܒܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܰܡܝܰܬܪܝܺܢ ܡܶܢܝ ܕܫܶܡܥܶܬ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܟܘܽܠܗܶܝܢ ܬܡ̈ܺܝܗܳܬ݂ܳܐ ܡܶܢ ܡܪܰܝܡܳܐ: ܘܣܰܒܪ̈ܳܬܳܐ ܕܠܳܐ ܡܶܣܬ݁ܰܝܟܳܢ ܡܶܢ ܗܰܘ ܕܰܒܪܳܢܝ. ܛܘܽܒܶܝܗ̇ ܐܶܡܝ ܒܚܰܝܠܕ݂ܳܬܳܐ: ܘܐܶܫܬ݁ܰܒ݁ܰܚ ܒܢܶܫ̈ܶܐ ܝܳܠܕ݂ܳܬܝ. ܐܶܢܳܐ ܕܶܝܢ ܠܳܐ ܐܶܫܬܘܽܩ ܕܐܶܫܰܒ݁ܰܚ ܠܚܰܝܠܬ݁ܳܢܳܐ: ܐܶܠܳܐ ܒܩܳܠܳܐ
of the age, and what happens in the times, you bring about by your word, and against the works of the inhabitants of the earth you accelerate the beginnings of the times, and the end of the ages you alone know, to whom nothing is difficult, but all things are easy, you accomplish by a nod; to whom the deep things as the high things come together and the beginnings of the ages minister to your word, who reveal to those who fear you, what is prepared for them, that from then you may console them; you indicate strengths to those who do not know, you break the hedge for the ignorant and you illuminate the dark things and you reveal the hidden things to the immaculate, who by faith have subjected themselves to you and to your law: you have shown to your servant this vision; open to me also the interpretation of it. For I know that I have received an answer about those things which I asked from you, and that which I asked, you have revealed to me and indicated to me, with what voice I should praise you or from what members I should make a hymn and praise ascend to you. For if my members were mouths and the hairs of my head were voices, not even so could I render praise to you, or celebrate you as is fitting, nor could I narrate your praise, or say the magnificence of your beauty! For what am I among men, or what am I counted with those, who are more excellent than I, because I have heard all these wonders from the Most High, and infinite announcements from him, who created me? Blessed is my mother among those who bear and my mother will be praised among women. But I will not be silent to praise the Most High, but with a voice of praise I will narrate his wonders. For who will be assimilated to your wonders, God, or who will comprehend your deep knowledge.... of life? For you by your counsel govern all creatures, which your right hand created, and you have established every fountain of light with yourself and have prepared the treasures of wisdom under your throne. And justly do they perish, who have not loved your law, and the torment of judgment will receive those, who have not been subject to your power. For if Adam the first sinned and brought death upon all who were not in his time: yet also those, who have been begotten from him, each one of them has prepared for his soul the future torment; and again each one of them has chosen for himself the future glory. [For truly, he who believes will receive the reward. But now return to corruption, you impious, who now


[p. 1161] {#po.0.p1161}


[p. 1162] {#po.0.p1162}
ܕܫܘܽܒܚܳܚܳܐ ܐܶܬܢܶܝܐ ܬܶܕ݂ܡܪ̈ܳܐܶܗ. ܘܡܰܢܘܽ ܓܶܝܝܪ ܡܶܬ݁ܕܰܡܶܐ ܒܬܶܕ݂ܡܘ̈ܳܳܟ݂ ܐܰܠܳܗܳܐܢ ܐܰܘ ܡܰܡܢܘܽ ܘܰܕ݂ܪܶܟ݂ ܡܰܚܫܰܒܬܳܟ݂ ܥܰܡܚܺܩܬ݁ܳܐ ܕܚܰܝ̈ܶܐ܆ ܐܰܢ̱ܬ ܓܶܚܝܪ ܒܬܰܪܥܚܳܬܳܟ݂ ܡܕܰܒܪܰܐ ܟ݁ܽܠ ܒܶܕ̈ܝܺܬ݁ܳܐ ܕ݁ܰܒ݁ܪܳܐ ܝܰܡܝܺܢܳܡ: ܘܐܰܢ̱ܬ݁ ܟܘܽܠ ܡܰܒܘܽܥܳܐ ܕܢܘܽܗܪܳܐ ܠܘܳܬܳܟ݂ ܐܰܩܶܢܬ: ܘܐܰܘܨܪ̈ܶܐ ܕ݁ܚܶܟܡܬ݁ܳܐ ܬܚܶܝܬ ܬ݁ܪܽܘܢܳܘܣ ܕܝܽܠܳܟ ܥܰܬܶܕܬ. ܘܟܺܐܢܳܐܝܺܬ ܐܳܒܕ݂ܝܺܢ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܠܳܐ ܐܰܚܶܒܘ ܠܢܳܡܘܽܣܳܟ: ܘܐܰܫܢܚܺܣܳܝܐ ܕܕ݂ܚܺܢܳܐ ܡܩܰܒܶܠ ܠܰܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܠܳܐ ܐܶܫܬܰܥܒ݁ܰܕ݂ܘ ܠܐܘܰܚܕ݂ܳܢܳܟ. ܐܶܢ ܐܳܪܳܡ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܩܰܕ݂ܡܳܝ ܚܬܳܛܳܐ ܘܐܵܝܬܝܺ ܡܰܡܘܬܳܐ ܥܰܠ ܟ݁ܽܠ ܕܠܳܐ ܒܙܰܒܢܶܗ: ܐܶܠܳܐ ܐܳܦ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܐܶܬܝܺܠܶܕ݂ܘ ܡܢܶܗ: ܟܘܽܠ ܐ݈ܢܳܫ ܐ݈ܢܳܫ ܡܶܢܗܘܽܢ ܛܰܫܶܒ ܠܢܰܦ݂ܫܶܗ ܬܰܫܢܬܚܳܩܳܐ ܕܰܥܬܝܺܕ݂. ܘܬܘܰܒ ܐ݈ܢܳܫ ܐ̱ܢܳܫ ܡܶܢܗܘܽܢ ܓܒܳܐ ܠܶܗ ܬܶܫܒ݁̈ܚܳܬܳܐ ܕܰܥܬܝܘܲܢ. ܫܰܪܝܺܪܳܐܝܺܬ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܰܘ ܕ݁ܰܡܗܰܝܡܰܢ ܢܶܣܰܒ ܐܰܓܪܳܐ. ܗܳܢܳܝܐ ܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܗܦܘܽܟܘ ܠܰܚܟܺܝܠܳܐ: ܥܰܘܳܠܶܐ ܕܗܳܫܶܐ: ܡܶܛܽܠ ܕ݁ܚܰܪܚܺܦܳܐܝܺܬ ܡܶܬܦ݁ܰܩܕ݁ܝܺܢ ܐܢܸܬܘܵܢ ܕܒܰܝܙܒ݂ܰܢ ܐܰܣܠܚܵܬܘܵܢ ܐܵܢܸܬܘܽܢ ܣܰܝܟܘܽܠܬܳܢܘܽܬܶܗ ܕܰܡܪܰܝܡܳܐ. ܠܳܐ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܐܰܠܶܦܟܘܽܢ ܥܒܳܕ݂ܰܘ̈ܗ̱ܝ: ܐܳܦ ܠܳܐ ܐܰܦܚܺܣܰܬܟܘܽܢ ܐܘܰܡܝܢܘܽܬܳܐ ܕܰܒܪܝܺܬܶܗ ܕܰܒܟܺܠܙܒܰܢ. ܠܳܐ ܐܝܺܬܰܘܗ̱ܝ ܗܳܟܝܶܠ ܐܳܕܳܡ܂ ܥܶܠܬ݁ܳܐ ܐܶܠܳܐ ܠܢܰܦܫܶܗ ܒܰܠܚܘܽܕ݂. ܟ݁ܽܠܰܢ ܕܶܝܢ ܐ̱ܢܳܫ ܐ̱ܢܳܫ ܗܘܳܐ ܠܢܰܦܫܶܗ ܐܳܕܳܡ. ܐܰܢ̱ܬ ܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܡܳܪܝܳܐ ܥܰܠ ܐܰܝܠܺܝܢ ܪܰܓ݁ܠܰܝܬ ܠܝܳ: ܦܰܫܶܩ ܠܝܽ: ܘܥܰܠ ܡܶܕܶܡ ܕܰܒܥܧܬ ܡܰܢܺܝܟ ܬܒ݁ܰܥܬ݁ܳܐ ܗܳܘܝܳܐ ܡܶܢ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܐܰܥܘܶܠܘ ܐܰܝܟ ܥܰܘܠܗܘܽܢ: ܘܰܡܫܰܒܰܚ ܐܰܢ̱ܬ ܠܰܡܗܰܝܡܰܢܶܐ ܐܝܟ ܗܰܝܡܳܢܩܺܐܗܘܽܢ. ܠܰܐܝܠܶܝܢ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܕ݁ܰܒܕ݂ܝܺܠܳܟ݂ ܐܶܢܘܽܢ ܐܰܢ̱ܬ ܘܒܕܰܒ݂ܪܰܬܐ: ܘܠܰܐܝܠܺܝܢ ܕܚܳܛܶܝܢ ܥܳܛܶܐ ܐܰܢ̱ܬ ܡܶܢ ܕܝܰܠܳܡ܀
are, because you will be visited bitterly, you who once rejected the intelligence of the Most High. For his works did not teach you nor did the everlasting artifice of his creature persuade you.] It is not therefore Adam the cause, except for himself only; but we all, each one has become an Adam to himself. But you, Lord, declare to me those things which you have revealed to me, and about that which I will ask from you, indicate to me. For at the end of the world there will be vengeance upon those, who have acted wickedly according to their wickedness, and you will glorify the faithful according to their faith. For those, who are among yours, you direct, and those, who sin, you blot out from yours.


[p. 1163] {#po.0.p1163}


[p. 1164] {#po.0.p1164}


[p. 1165] {#po.0.p1165}
ܘܰܗܘܳܐ ܕܟܰܕ݂ ܫܰܠܡܶܬ ܠܰܡܡܰܠܳܠܘܽ ܡܶܠܶܐ ܕܰܨܠܽܘܬܳܐ ܗܳܕܶܐ: ܝܶܬܒܶܬ ܐܰܡܳܢ ܬܚܶܝܬ ܐܝܺܠܳܢܳܐ ܕܐܶܬܬܢܰܚ ܒܬܶܛܠܳܠܳܐ ܕܣܰܘ̈ܟܶܐ ܘܡܶܬܕ݁ܰܡܰܪ ܗ̱ܘܝܺܬ ܘܡܶܬܬ݁ܰܡܰܗ ܘܡܶܬܪܰܢܶܐ ܒܪܶܥܝܳܢܝ ܥܰܠ ܣܘܺܓܐܳܐ ܕܛܳܒܘܽܬܳܐ ܕ݁ܰܕ݂ܚܰܩܘ ܡܢܗܘܢ ܚܰܛܳܝ̈ܶܐ ܕܥܰܠ ܐܰܪܥܳܐ ܘܥܰܠ ܬܰܫܢܺܝܩܳܝܐ ܣܰܓܝܳܐܳܐ ܕܒܰܣܰܪܘ ܥܠܰܘܗ̱ܝ ܕ݁ܰܚܕܳܝܺܢ ܕܡܶܫܬ݁ܰܢܩܺܝܢ ܥܰܠ ܕܚܳܛܝܢ. ܘܟܰܕ݂ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܘܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܕܳܡܝܳܢ ܠܗܶܝܢ ܡܶܬܚܰܫܰܒ ܗ̱ܘܝܺܬ: ܘܗܳܐ ܐܶܫܬܰܠܰܚ ܠܘܳܬܝ ܪܳܡܶܝܠ ܡܰܠܰܐܟ݂ܳܐ ܗܰܘ ܕܩܳܐܶܡ ܥܰܠ ܚܶܙܘܳܢܶܐ ܕ݁ܰܫܪܳܪܳܐ ܘܐܳܡܰܪ ܠܝ ܠܡܳܢܳܐ ܡܕ݂ܰܠܰܚ ܠܳܟ݂ ܠܶܒܳܟ ܒܰܪܘܽܟ: ܘܰܠܡܳܢܳܐ ܫܳܓܫܳܐ ܠܳܟ
LV. And it happened, when I had finished speaking the words of this prayer, I sat there under a tree that I might rest in the shade of its branches. And I was admiring and was stupefied and was reconsidering in my thoughts about the multitude of goodness, which sinners, who are upon the earth, have rejected from themselves, and about the much torment, which they despised, when they knew that they would be tormented, for the reason that they sin. And when I was thinking these and similar things, behold, Ramiel the angel, who is over true visions, was sent to me and said to me: Why does your heart trouble you, Baruch, and why does your thought move you. For if by hearing you have heard only about the judgment and have been so much moved: what, when you will see it with open eyes? And if by the expectation, with which you expect the day of the Most High, you have been so much dissolved: what when you will arrive at his coming? And if by the word of the rumor of the torment of those, who have transgressed, you have been so much disturbed: how much more, when the deed itself will reveal the wonders? And if the names


[p. 1166] {#po.0.p1166}
ܒܰܠܚܘܽܕ ܕܥܰܠ ܕ݁ܝܳܢܳܐ ܘܗܳܟܰܢܳܐ ܐܶܬܬܙܝܰܥܬ: ܡܳܢܳܐ ܟ݁ܰܕ݂ ܬ݁ܶܚܙܶܝܘܗ̱ܝ ܒܥܰܝܢܰܝ̈ܟ݂ ܦܰܓ݂ܠܶܐ: ܘܐܶܢ ܡܶܢ ܣܘܽܟܳܝܳܐ ܕܰܡܣܰܟ݁ܶܐ ܐܰܢ̱ܬ ܠܚܰܘܡܶܗ ܕܚܰܝܠܬ݁ܳܢܳܐ: ܗܳܟܰܢܳܐ ܐܶܫܬܪܝܺܬ: ܡܳܢܳܐ ܟ݁ܰܕ݂ ܬ݁ܶܡܛܶܐ ܐ݇ܢ̱ܬ ܠܘܶܒܐܬܝܺܬܶܗ܆ ܘܐܶܢ ܥܰܠ ܡܶܠܬ݁ܳܐ ܕܛܰܝܒ݁ܳܐ ܕܰܢܚܺܝܩܳܝܐ ܕܙܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܐܰܣܟܶܠܘ ܗܳܢܳܐ ܟ݁ܽܠܶܗ ܐܶܬܪܰܡܰܕܬ: ܚܰܕ݂ ܟܡܳܐ ܟ݁ܰܕ݂ ܗܘܽ ܥܒܳܕ݂ܳܐ ܢܶܓ݁ܠܶܐ ܬܕ݂ܰܡܪ̈ܳܬܳܐܢ ܘܐܶܢ ܫܡ̈ܳܗܳܐ ܕܛܳܒ̈ܳܬܳܐ ܘܰܕܒܝ̈ܰܫܳܬܳܐ ܕܐܳܬܝܳܢ ܗܳܝܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܫܡܰܥܬ ܘܐܬܬܠܝܰܨܬ: ܡܳܢܳܐ ܟ݁ܰܕ݂ ܬ݁ܶܚܙܶܐ ܡܶܕ݁ܶܡ ܕܓ݂ܳܠܝܳܐ ܪܰܒܘܽܬܳܐ: ܕ݁ܰܠܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܡܰܟ݁ܶܣ ܘܰܠܗܳܠܶܝܢ
You have heard of the goods and misfortunes then coming, and you were distressed: what will it be when you see that which the Majesty reveals, which will refute these and make those rejoice?


[p. 1167] {#po.0.p1167}
ܡܫܰܒܰܚ. ܒܪܰܡ ܕܶܝܢ ܡܶܛܺܝܠ ܕܰܒܥܰܝܬ ܡܶܢ ܡܪܰܚܡܳܢܳܐ ܕ݁ܢܶܓ݂ܠܶܐ ܠܳܟ ܦܘܽܫܳܩܶܝܗ ܕܚܶܙܘܳܐ ܕܰܚܙܰܝܬ: ܐܶܫܬܰܠܚܶܬ ܐܶܢܳܐ ܕܐܳܡܰܪ ܠܳܟ: ܕܡܰܥܘܕܳܥܘܽ ܐܰܘܕܥܳܟ݂ ܚܰܝܠܬ݁ܳܢܳܐ ܕܘܽܒܳܪ̈ܶܐ ܕܙܰܒ݂ܢ̈ܶܐ ܕܰܥܒܰܪܘ ܘܕܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܰܥܬܝܺܕ݂ܝܺܢ ܕܢܶܥܒܪܘܽܢ ܒܥܳܠܡܶܗ: ܡܶܢ ܪܺܝܫ ܒܪܝܺܬܶܗ ܘܰܕ݁ܰܡܳܐ ܠܫܘܽܠܳܡܶܗ: ܕܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܰܒܢܶܟܠܳܐ ܘܕܐܰܝܠܺܝܢ ܕܒܰܫܪܳܪܳܐ. ܐܰܝܟ݁ܰܢܳܐ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܕܰܚܙܰܝܬ ܥܢܳܢܳܐ ܪܰܒ݁ܬܳܐ
LVI. Nevertheless, because you have besought the Most High to reveal to you the interpretation of the vision which you have seen, I have been sent to tell you. For the Most Mighty has indicated to you the account of the times which have passed, and which are to pass in His world from the beginning of its creation and up to its consummation, of those,


[p. 1168] {#po.0.p1168}
ܕܣܶܠܩܰܬ ܡܶܢ ܝܰܡܳܐ ܘܐܲܙܰܠ̱ܬ݁ ܘܟܰܣܝܰܬ݁ܳܗ ܠܰܐܪܥܳܐ: ܗܘܺܝܘ ܐܘܽܪܟܺܝܗ ܕܥܳܠܡܳܝܐ ܗܰܘ ܕܰܥܒܰܝܕ݂ ܚܰܝܠܬ݁ܳܢܳܐ: ܟ݁ܰܕ݂ ܐܶܬܚܰܫܰܒ ܕܢܶܥܒ݁ܶܕ ܥܳܠܡܳܐ. ܘܰܗܘܳܐ ܕܟܰܕ ܢܶܦܩܰܬ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܡܶܠܬ݁ܳܐ ܡܶܢ ܩܕܳܡܰܘܗ̱ܝ: ܩܺܝܡ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܐܘܽܪܟܺܝܗ ܕܥܳܠܡܳܐ ܡܶܕܶܡ ܙܥܘܽܪ: ܘܐܶܬܬܰܩܰܢ ܐܰܝܟ ܣܘܽܓ݁ܪܳܐ ܕܣܘܽܟܳܠܶܝܗ ܕ݁ܗܰܘ ܕܫܰܠܚܶܝܗ. ܘܐܰܝܟ ܕܰܚܙܺܝܬ ܩܰܕ݂ܡܳܝ ܒܪܺܫܳܐ ܕܰܥܢܳܢܶܐ ܘܰܡ̈ܝܳܐ ܐܘܽܟܶܡܐ ܕܩܰܕ݂ܡܳܝ ܢܚܶܬܘ ܥܰܠ ܐܰܪܥܳܐ: ܗܝܺ ܗܝܺ ܡܰܥܒܪܳܢܘܽܬܳܐ ܕܰܥܒܰܪ ܐܳܕ݁ܳܡ ܒܰܪܢܳܫܳܝܐ ܩܰܕ݂ܡܳܝܐ. ܡܶܛܽܠ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܕܟܰܕ ܐܰܥܒܰܪ ܡܰܘܬܳܐ ܕܠܳܐ ܒܙܰܒܢܶܝܗ ܗܘܳܐ ܘܐܶܒܝܠܳܐ ܐܶܫܬ݁ܰܡܰܗ ܘܥܳܩܬܳܐ ܐܶܬܥܰܬܕ݁ܰܬ ܘܟܺܐܒܳܐ ܐܶܬܒܪܺܝ: ܘܥܰܡܠܳܐ ܐܶܫܬܰܟ݂ܠܠ: ܘܦܘܽܒ݁ܗܳܪܳܐ ܫܰܪܝܺ ܕܢܶܬܩܰܝܰܡ: ܘܰܫܝܘܽܠ ܬܰܒܝܥܳܝܐ ܕܬܶܬܚܰܕܰܬ ܒܰܕ݂ܡܳܐ: ܘܰܢܣܳܝܒܳܝܐ ܕܰܒܢܰܝ̈ܳܐ ܗܘܳܐ: ܘܪܶܬܚܳܐ ܕܐܰܒܳܗ̈ܳܬܳܐ ܐܶܬܥܒܶܝܕ: ܘܪܰܒܘܳܬܳܐ ܕܐ̱ܢܳܫܘܽܬܳܐ ܐܶܬܡܰܟܟܰܬ: ܘܛܰܝܒܘܽܬܳܐ ܚܰܠܝܰܬ. ܡܳܢܳܐ ܡܶܫܟ݁ܰܚ ܕܐܘܿܟܶܡ ܗ̱ܘܵ ܐܰܘ ܕܚܫܘܽܟ݂ ܡܶܢ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܡܶܫܟ݁ܰܚܝ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܕܢܶܗܘܶܐ܆ ܗܳܢܰܘ ܘܽܪܺܝܫܗܘܽܢ ܕܡܰܝ̈ܳܐ ܐܘܰܟ̈ܳܡܶܐ ܕܰܚܙܰܝܬ. ܘܡܶܢ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܐܘܰܟ̈ܳܡܶܐ ܬܘܒ ܐܘܰܟ̈ܳܡܐ ܡܶܬܝܰܠܕ݂ܺܝܢ ܓܶܝܪ ܠܢܰܦܫܶܗ ܗܘܳܐ ܩܰܢܕ݁ܘܺܢܳܘܣ: ܐܳܦ ܠܡܰܠܰܐܟ̈ܶܐ ܗܘܳܐ ܩܰܢܕ݁ܘܰܢܳܐ. ܬܘܒ ܓܶܝܪ ܒܗܰܘ ܙܰܒܢܳܐ ܕܐܶܬܼܒܪܺܝ ܒܶܗ: ܐܺܝܬ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܠܗܘܽܢ ܚܰܝܪܘܳܬܳܐ: ܘܰܢܚܶܬܘ ܡܶܢܗܘܽܢ ܘܐܶܬ݂ܚܰܠܰܛܘ ܥܰܡ ܢܶܫ̈ܶܐ. ܘܗܳܝܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܐܰܝܠܺܝܢ ܕܗܳܟ݂ܰܢܳܐ ܥܒܰܕ݂ܘ: ܒܚܰܒ̈ܫܘܽܪܶܐ ܐܶܫܬܰܢܰܩܘ. ܫܰܪܟ݁ܳܐ ܕܝܢ ܕܣܘܺܓ݁ܐܳܐ ܕܡܰܠܰܐܟ̈ܶܐ ܕܡܶܢܝܳܢ ܙܠܰܝܬܗܘܽܢ ܐܶܬܦܠܚܘ. ܘܐܰܝܠܺܝܢ ܕܥܳܡܪܝܺܝܢ ܗ̱ܘܰܘ ܒܐܰܪܥܳܐ ܐܶܒ݂ܰܕ݂ܘ ܐܰܟܚܕ݂ܳܐ ܒܰܝܕ݂ ܡܰܝ̈ܳܐ ܕܛܺܝܘܦܳܢܳܝܐ. ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܐܶܢܘܽܢ ܡܰܝ̈ܳܐ ܩܰܕ݁̈ܡܳܝܶܐ ܐܘܽܟّܶܡܐ܀
which are fraudulent, and those which are true. For just as you saw the great cloud which rose from the sea and went and covered the earth: this is the length of the age which the Most Mighty made when He thought to make the age. And it came to pass, when the word had gone forth from His presence, the length of the age stood, a small thing, and it was established according to the multitude of the understanding of Him who sent it. And as you saw first at the beginning of the cloud the black waters which first descended upon the earth: this is the transgression by which Adam, the first man, transgressed. For when he had transgressed, death came to be before its time, and mourning began to be named, and sadness was prepared, and pain was created, and labor was perfected, and boasting began to be established, and Hades began to demand to be renewed in blood, and the conception of children occurred, and the fervor of parents came to be, and the majesty of humanity was humbled, and kindness languished. What, therefore, could be blacker or darker than these? This is the beginning of the black waters which you saw. And from these black ones, black ones were again generated, and darkness of darknesses was made. For he who was a danger to his own soul became also a source of danger to the Angels. For at that time, when they were created, they had freedom, and some of them descended and mingled with women. And then those who had done so were tormented in chains. But the remaining multitude of angels, whose number was not [known], restrained themselves. And those who dwelt on the earth perished together by the waters of the flood. These are the first black waters.


[p. 1169] {#po.0.p1169}


[p. 1170] {#po.0.p1170}


[p. 1171] {#po.0.p1171}
ܘܡܶܢ ܒܳܬܰܪ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ: ܚܙܰܝܬ ܡܰܝ̈ܳܐ ܢܰܗܝܺܪ̈ܶܐ܆ ܗܳܢܰܘ ܡܰܒܘܽܥܶܝܗ ܕܐܰܒܪܳܗܳܡ ܐܳܦ ܬܰܘ̈ܠܕ݂ܳܬܶܗ ܘܡܶܐܬܝܺܬܶܗ ܕܰܒܪܶܗ ܘܰܕ݂ܒ݂ܰܪ ܒ݂ܪܶܗ ܘܕܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕ݂ܳܡܶܝܢ ܠܗܘܽܢ. ܡܶܛܽܠ ܕܰܒ݂ܗܰܘ ܙܰܒ݂ܢܳܐ ܕܠܳܐ ܟܬܳܒ݂ܳܐ ܢܳܡܘܽܣܳܐ ܠܘܳܬܗܘܽܢ ܡܶܫܬ݁ܰܡܰܥ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ:
LVII. And after these you saw the clear waters: this is the rise of Abraham, and his generations, and the coming of his son, and his son's son, and those who are like them. For at that time the written law was not named among them, and the works of the commandments were then being fulfilled, and the faith of the future judgment was then being generated, and the hope of the world, which will be renewed, was then being built, and the promise
ܘܥܒܳܕ݂ܶܐ ܕܦܽܘܩ̈ܕ݂ܳܢܶܐ ܗܳܝܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܡܶܬܬܡܰܠܝܺܢ ܗ̱ܘܰܘ: ܘܗܰܝܡܳܢܘܽܬܳܐ ܕܕܝܺܢܳܐ ܕܰܥܬܝܺܕ݂ ܗܳܝܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܡܶܬܝܰܝܠܕ݁ܳܐ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ: ܘܣܰܒܪܳܐ ܕ݂ܥܳܠܡܳܐ ܕܡܶܬܚܰܕ݂ܰܬ ܗܳܝܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܡܶܬܒܢܶܐ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ: ܘܡܘܽܠܟܿܳܢܳܐ
of the life which will come afterwards was being planted. These are the clear waters which you saw.
ܕܚܰܝ̈ܶܐ ܕܡܶܢ ܒܳܬܰܪ ܟܶܢ ܐܳܬ݁ܶܐ ܡܶܬܢܨܶܒ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ. ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܐܶܢܘܽܢ ܡܰܝ̈ܳܐ ܢܰܗܝܺܪ̈ܶܐ ܕܰܚܙܰܝܬ܀ ܘܡܰܝ̈ܳܐ ܬܠ̈ܺܬܳܝܶܐ ܐܽܘܟ̈ܳܡܶܐ ܕܰܚܙܰܝܬ: ܗܳܢܰܘ ܚܒܳܠܳܐ ܕܟ݁ܽܠ ܚܺܛܳܗܶܐ ܕܡܶܢ ܒܳܬܰܪܟ݁ܶܢ
LVIII. And the third black waters which you saw, this is the mixture of all the sins,


[p. 1172] {#po.0.p1172}
ܥܒܰܕ݂ܘ ܥܰܡ݈ܡܶܐ: ܡܶܢ ܒܳܬܰܪ ܡܰܘܬܗܘܿܢ ܕܗܳܢܘܽܢ ܙܰܕ݁ܺܝ̈ܩܶܐ ܘܪܘܽܫܥܳܗ ܕܐܰܪܥܳܐ ܕܡܶܨܪܶܝܢ ܕܡܰܪܫܥܝܢ ܗ̱ܘܰܘ: ܒܫܽܘܥܒܳܕ݂ܳܐ ܕܰܡܫܰܥܒܕ݁ܝܺܝܢ ܗ̱ܘܰܘ ܠܰܒܢܰܝ̈ܗܘܽܢ ܕܗܳܢܘܽܢ. ܒܪܰܡ ܕܶܝܢ ܐܳܦ ܗܶܢܘܽܢ
which the nations afterwards committed after the death of those righteous ones, and the wickedness of the land of Egypt, by which they acted wickedly in the subjection by which they subjected their children. Nevertheless, even they perished in the end.
ܐܶܒܰܕ݂ܘ ܠܰܐܚܪܳܝܬܳܐ܀ ܘܡܰܝ̈ܳܐ ܪ̈ܒܺܝܥܳܝܶܐ ܢܰܗܝܺܪ̈ܶܐ ܕܰܚܙܰܝܬ: ܗܳܝ ܗܺܝ ܡܶܐܬܝܺܬܶܗ ܕܡܘܽܫܶܐ ܘܕܐܵܗܪܘܽܢ ܘܕܡܰܪܝܰܡ܂ ܘܕܝܺܫܘܽܥ ܒܰܪܢܘܽܢ ܘܰܕ݂ܟܳܠܳܝܒ ܘܰܕܟܽܠܗܘܽܢ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕ݁ܕܳܡܶܝܢ ܠܗܘܽܢ. ܒܗܰܘ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܙܰܒܢܳܐ ܫܪܳܓ݁ܶܗ ܕܢܳܡܘܽܣܳܐ ܕ݁ܰܠܥܳܠܰܡ܂ ܐܰܢܗܰܪ ܠܟ݁ܽܠ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܝܳܬܒܺܝܢ ܗ̱ܘܰܘ ܒܥܺܝܦܘܽܛܳܝܐ: ܕܠܰܐܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܰܡܗܰܝܡܢܝܢ ܢܰܘܕ݁ܰܥ ܡܘܽܠܟܿܳܢܶܗ ܕܐܵܓܪܗܘܽܢ ܘܠܰܐܝܠܺܝܢ ܕܟܳܦܪܝܺܢ ܬܰܫܢܺܝܩܳܝܐ ܕܢܘܽܪܳܐ ܕܰܢܛܺܝܪ ܠܗܘܿܢ. ܐܶܠܳܐ ܐܳܦ݂ ܫܡܰܝ̈ܳܐ ܒܗܰܘ ܙܰܒ݂ܢܳܐ ܐܶܬܬܚܶܕܘ ܡܶܢ ܐܰܬܪܗܘܽܢ: ܕܰܬܚܶܝܬ ܬܪܽܘܢܳܘܣ ܕܚܰܝܠܬܳܢܶܐ ܐܶܬܬܙܝܽܥܘ: ܟ݁ܰܕ݂ ܐܰܚܶܕ݂ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܠܡܘܽܫܶܐ ܠܘܳܬܶܗ. ܚܰܘܝܶܗ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܙܘܽܗܳܪ̈ܶܐ ܣܰܓܺܝ̈ܐܶܐ ܥܰܡ ܕܘܽܒܳܪ̈ܶܐ ܕܢܳܡܘܽܣܶܐ ܘܫܘܽܠܳܡܳܐ ܕܙܰܒ݂ܢܳܐ ܐܰܝܟ ܕܐܳܦ ܠܳܟ. ܘܬܘܒ ܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܕܡܘܽܬܳܗ ܕܨܶܗܝܘܽܢ ܘܡܰܫܘܽܚܝܳܬܳܗ: ܕܡܶܬܥܰܒܕ݂ܳܐ ܒܰܕ݂ܡܘܽܬܳܐ ܕܒܶܝܬ ܡܰܩܕ݂ܳܐ ܕܗܳܫܳܝܐ. ܐܶܠܳܐ ܐܳܦ ܗܳܝܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܚܰܘܝܶܗ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܡܰܫܘܽܚܝܳܬܳܗ ܕܢܘܽܪܳܐ: ܐܳܦ ܥܘܽܡܩܰܝ̈ܘܽܗ̱ܝ ܕ݁ܰܐܗܘܳܡܳܐ ܘܚܘܽܩܪܗܶܝܢ ܕܪ̈ܘܽܚܶܐ: ܘܡܶܢܝܳܢܳܐ ܕܛܘܽܦܰܚ̈ܝ ܡܶܛܪܳܐ: ܘܐܰܚܳܕܶܗ ܕܪܘܽܓܙܳܐ: ܘܣܘܽܓ݂ܳܐܐ ܕܢܰܓܺܝܪܘܽܬ ܪܘܽܚܳܐ: ܘܰܫܪܳܪܶܗ ܕܕܝܺܢܳܐ: ܘܥܶܩܳܪܳܗ ܕܚܶܟܡܬܳܐ: ܘܥܘܽܬܪܳܐ ܕܣܽܘܟܳܠܬܳܢܘܽܬܳܐ: ܘܡܰܒܘܽܥܳܐ ܕܝܺܕ݂ܰܥܬܳܐ ܘܪܰܘܡܶܗ ܕܐܰܐܶܪ: ܘܪܰܒܘܽܬܶܗ ܕܦܰܪܕܰܝܣܳܐ: ܘܫܘܽܠܳܡܗܘܽܢ ܕܥܳܠܡ̈ܶܐ: ܘܪܺܝܫ ܝܰܘܡܳܐ ܕܕܝܺܢܳܐ ܘܡܶܢܝܳܢܳܐ ܕܩܘܽܪ̈ܒܿܳܢܶܐ: ܘܐܰܪ̈ܥܳܬ݁ܳܐ ܕ݁ܰܥܕ݂ܰܟ݂ܝܺܠ ܠܳܐ ܐܶܬܰܝ̈: ܘܦܘܽܡܳܗ ܕܓܺܗܰܢܳܐ ܘܰܩܝܳܡܳܐ ܕܰܬܒܰܥܬܳܐ: ܘܐܰܬܪܳܐ ܕܗܰܝܡܳܢܘܽܬܳܐ: ܘܕܘܽܟܬܳܐ ܕ݁ܣܰܒܪܳܐ ܘܰܕܡܘܳܬܳܐ ܕܰܫܢܺܝܩܳܐ ܕܰܥܬܝܺܕ:
LIX. And the fourth clear waters which you saw, this is the coming of Moses and Aaron and Miriam and Joshua the son of Nun and Caleb and all who are like them. At that time the lamp of the eternal law shone upon all who sat in darkness, which would announce to those who believe the promise of their reward, and to the unbelievers the torment of fire reserved for them. But also the heavens at that time were removed from their place, and those who were under the throne of the Most Mighty were moved when He had received Moses unto Himself. For He showed him many warnings with the institutions of the laws and the end of the time, just as also to you, and likewise the likeness of Zion and its measurements, which was to be made in the likeness of the present sanctuary. But also then He showed him the measurements of fire and the depths of the abyss, and the weight of the winds and the number of the drops of rain and the repression of anger and the multitude of long-suffering and the truth of judgment and the root of wisdom and the riches of understanding and the fountain of knowledge and the height of the air and the magnitude of Paradise and the end of the ages and the beginning of the day of judgment and the number of the offerings and the lands which have not yet come and the mouth of Gehenna and the covenant of vengeance and the region of faith and the place of hope and the likeness of the future torment and the multitude of angels, whose number is not [known], and the virtues of the flame and the splendor of the lightnings and the voice of the thunders and the orders of the archangels and the storehouses of light and the changes of the times and the investigations of the law. These are the fourth clear waters which you saw.


[p. 1173] {#po.0.p1173}


[p. 1174] {#po.0.p1174}
ܘܣܘܽܓܳܝܐܐ ܕܡܰܠܰܐܟ̈ܶܐ ܕ݂ܡܶܢܝܳܢ ܠܰܝܬ ܠܗܘܽܢ: ܘܚܰܠܶܠܳܬܳܐ ܕܫܰܠܗܽܒܝܳܬܳܐ: ܘܙܰܗܪܗܘܽܢ ܕܒܰܪ̈ܩܶܐ: ܘܩܳܠܳܐ ܕܪ̈ܰܥܡܶܐ: ܘܣܶܕܪ̈ܰܝ ܪ̈ܝܺܫܶܐ ܕܡܰܠܰܐܟ̈ܶܐ: ܘܐܰܘܨܪ̈ܶܐ ܕܢܘܽܗܪܳܐ: ܘܚܘܽܠܳܦܶܐ ܕܙܰܒܢ̈ܶܐ: ܘܥܘܽܩܳܒܶܐ ܕܢܳܡܘܽܣܳܐ. ܗܳܠܺܝܢ ܐܶܢܘܽܢ ܡܰܝ̈ܳܐ ܪ̈ܰܒܠܳܫܳܝܐ ܘܢܰܗܝܺܪܶܐ ܕܰܚܙܺܝܬ܀ ܘܡܰܝ̈ܳܐ ܚܰܡܝܺܫܶܐ ܐܘܽܟ̈ܳܡܶܐ ܕܰܚܙܰܝܬ ܕܡܰܥܡܛܪܝܺܢ: ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܐܶܢܘܽܢ ܥܒܳܕ݂ܶܐ ܕܥܳܒܕܝܺܢ ܗ̱ܘܰܘ ܐܳܡܘܽܪܳܝܶܐ ܘܠܘܽܚ̈ܫܳܬܳܐ ܕܚܶܪ̈ܫܰܝܗܘܽܢ ܕܥܳܒܕܝܺܢ ܗ̱ܘܰܘ: ܘܒܺܝܫܳܬܳܐ ܕܪ̈ܳܐܙܰܝܗܘܽܢ: ܘܰܚܒܳܟ݁ܳܐ ܕܛܰܢܦܘܽܬܗܘܽܢ. ܐܳܠܳܐ ܐܳܦ ܐܝܺܣܪܳܐܶܝܠ ܐܶܬ݁ܛܰܢܰܦ ܗܳܝܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܒܰܚܺܛܳܗܶܐ ܒܝܰܘ̈ܡܳܬܳܐ ܕܕ݂ܰܝܳܢܶܐ: ܟ݁ܰܕ ܚܳܙܶܝܢ ܗ̱ܘܰܘ ܐܳܬܘܳܬܳܐ ܣܰܓܝܺܐܳܬܳܐ ܕܗܳܘ̈ܝܳܢ ܗ̱ܘܰܝ̈
LX. And the fifth black waters which you saw raining, these are the works which the Amorites did, and the incantations of their magic which they performed, and the malice of their mysteries and the mixture of their uncleanness. But also Israel was defiled with sins in the days of the Judges when they saw the many signs which were done by Him who made them.
ܡܶܢ ܗܰܘ ܕܰܥܒܰܕ݂ ܐܶܢܘܽܢ܀ ܘܡܰܝ̈ܳܐ ܫܬܺܝܬܳܝܶܐ ܢܰܗܝܺܪ̈ܶܐ ܕܰܚܙܺܝܬ: ܗܳܢܰܘ ܗ̱ܘܽ ܙܰܒܢܳܐ ܕܐܶܬܝܺܠܶܕ݂ ܒܶܗ ܕܰܘܝܺܕ݂ ܘܰܫܠܺܝܡܘܽܢ
LXI. And the sixth clear waters which you saw, this is the time when was born


[p. 1175] {#po.0.p1175}


[p. 1176] {#po.0.p1176}
ܘܰܗܘܳܐ ܒܗܰܘ ܙܰܒܢܳܐ ܒܶܢܝܳܢܳܗ ܕܨܲܗܝܘܽܢ ܘܚܘܽܕ݁ܳܬ݂ܳܐ ܕܒܶܝܬ ܡܰܩܕܫܳܝܐ ܘܐܰܫܺܝܕ݂ ܕܡܳܝܐ ܣܰܓܝܺܐܳܐ ܕܥܰܡ̱ܡ̈ܶܐ ܕܰܚܛܺܝܘ ܗܳܝܕܶܝܢ: ܘܩܘܽܪ̈ܒ݁ܳܢܶܐ ܣܰܓ̈ܺܝܐܶܐ ܕܐܶܬܩܰܪܰܒܘ ܗܳܝܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܒܚܘܽܕܬܳܐ ܕܒܶܝܬ ܡܰܩܕܫܳܝܐ. ܘܰܫܠܳܡܳܐ ܘܫܰܝܢܳܐ ܒܗܰܘ ܙܰܒ݂ܢܳܐ ܩܳܐܶܡ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ: ܘܩܰܝܺܝܡܘܽܬܳܐ ܒܰܟܢܘܽܫܬ݁ܳܐ ܡܶܫܬܰܡܥܳܐ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ: ܘܥܘܺܬܪܳܐ ܕܣܰܟܘܽܠܬܳܢܘܽܬܳܐ ܒܟܶܢ̈ܫܶܐ ܡܶܬܪܰܘܪܰܒ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ: ܘܥܺܐܕ݁̈ܶܐ ܕܩܰܕܝܺܫܘܽܬܳܐ ܒܛܳܒܘܽܬܳܐ ܘܰܒܚܰܕ݂ܘܽܬܳܐ ܣܰܓܝܺܐܬܳܐ ܡܶܫܬܰܠܡܝܺܢ ܗ̱ܘܰܘ ܘܕܝܰܢܳܐ ܕܰܡܕ݂ܰܒܪ̈ܳܢܶܐ ܗܳܝܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܕܠܳܐ ܢܶܟܝܠܳܐ ܡܶܬܚܙܶܐ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܘܙܰܕܝܺܝܩܘܽܬܳܐ ܕܦܘܽܩܕܳܢܰܘ̈ܗ̱ܝ ܕܚܰܝܠܬܳܢܳܐ ܒܰܫܪܳܪܳܐ ܡܶܫܬܰܡܠܝܳܐ ܗ̱ܘܵܬ. ܘܐܰܪܥܳܐ ܕܐܰܬܪܰܚܡܰܬ ܗܳܝܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܒܗܰܘ ܙܰܒܢܳܐ: ܘܡܶܛܽܠ ܕܠܳܐ ܚܳܛܶܝܢ ܗ̱ܘܰܘ ܥܳܡܘܽܪ̈ܶܝܗ: ܝܰܬܝܺܪ ܡܶܢ ܟ݁ܽܠܗܶܝܢ ܐܰܪ̈ܥܳܬ݁ܳܐ ܡܶܫܬܰܒܚܳܐ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ. ܘܨܲܗܝܘܽܢ ܡܕܺܝܢ̱ܬ݁ܳܐ ܗܳܝܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܫܰܠܝܛܳܐ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܥܰܠ ܟ݁ܽܠ ܐܰܪ̈ܥܳܬ݂ܳܐ ܘܐܰܬܪ̈ܰܘܳܬ݂ܳܐ. ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܐܶܢܘܽܢ ܡܰܝ̈ܳܐ ܢܰܗܝܺܪ̈ܶܐ ܕܰܚܙܰܝܬ܀
David and Solomon. And there was at that time the building of Zion and the dedication of the sanctuary and the shedding of the blood of many peoples who sinned then, and many offerings which were offered then at the dedication of the sanctuary. And tranquility and peace existed at that time, and wisdom was heard in the synagogue, and the abundance of understanding was magnified in the congregations, and the feasts of holiness were celebrated with goodness and much joy, and the judgment of the rulers was then seen without fraud, and the justice of the commandments of the Most Mighty was performed in truth, and the land, because it had then obtained mercy at that time, and because its inhabitants were not sinning, was glorified above all lands, and the city of Zion then ruled over all lands and regions. These are the clear waters which you saw.


[p. 1177] {#po.0.p1177}
ܘܡܰܫ̈ܳܐ ܫܒܺܝܥܳܝܶܐ ܐܽܘܟܳܡܶܐ ܕܰܚܙܰܝܬ: ܗܝܺ ܗ̱ܝ ܗܦܟܳܝܢܘܽܬܳܐ ܕܡܰܚܫܰܒܬܶܗ ܕܝܳܪܳܒܥܳܡ ܕܐܶܬܚܰܫܰܒ ܕܢܶܥܒܶܕ ܬܪܶܝܢ ܥܶܓܠܺܝܢ ܕܕܰܗܒ݁ܳܐ ܘܟܽܠ ܪ̈ܘܽܫܥܳܐ ܕܐܰܪܫܰܥܘ ܡܰܠܶܟ݁ܶܐ ܕܰܗܘܰܘ ܡܶܢ ܒܳܬܪܶܗ ܘܚܶܪܡܳܗ ܕܐܺܝܙܰܒ݁ܶܠ: ܘܕܶܚܠܰܬ ܦܬܰܟܪ̈ܶܐ ܕܰܦܠܰܚ ܐܝܺܣܪܳܝܶܠ ܒܗܰܘ ܙܰܒ݂ܢܳܐ: ܘܰܟܠܳܝܶܗ ܕܡܶܛܪܳܐ: ܘܟܰܦ݁̈ܢܶܐ ܕ݁ܰܗܘܰܘ ܥܕ݂ܰܡܳܐ ܕܢܶܐܟܠܳܢ ܢܶܫ̈ܶܐ ܦܺܐܪܶܐ ܕܟܰܪ̈ܣܳܬܗܶܝܢ: ܘܙܰܒ݂ܢܳܐ ܕܰܫܒ݂ܝܽܘܬܗܘܽܢ ܗܰܘ ܕܐܶܬܿܳܐ ܥܰܠ ܬ݁ܶܫܥܳܝܐ ܫܒܺܝܛܺܝܢ ܘܦܶܠܓܶܗ: ܡܶܛܽܠ ܕܐܼܝܬܰܝܗܘܽܢ ܗ̱ܘܰܘ ܒ݁ܰܚ̈ܛܳܗܶܐ ܣܰܓ̈ܺܝܐܶܐ. ܘܐܶܬܳܐ ܣܰܠܡܰܢܰܨܰܪ ܡܰܠܟܿܳܐ ܕܐܳܬܘܽܪܳܝܶܐ ܘܰܫܒܳܐ ܐܶܢܘܽܢ. ܥܰܠ ܥܰܡ̈ܡܶܐ ܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܣܰܓܝܳܐ ܗ̱ܘܽ ܠܡܺܐܡܰܪ: ܕܰܟܡܳܐܝܺܬ ܡܥܰܘܠܺܝܢ ܗ̱ܘܰܘ
LXII. And the seventh black waters which you saw, this is the perversion of the thought of Jeroboam, who thought to make two golden calves, and all the iniquities which the kings who were after him wickedly perpetrated, and the abomination of Jezebel, and the idolatry which Israel practiced at that time, and the prohibition of rain and the famines which were such that women ate the fruit of their wombs, and the time of their captivity, which came upon the nine tribes and a half, because they were in many sins. And Shalmaneser the king of the Assyrians came and led them away captive. But as for those who remained, it is too much to say how impiously and wickedly they always acted and never showed themselves righteous. These are the seventh black waters which you saw.


[p. 1178] {#po.0.p1178}
ܘܡܰܪܫܥܺܝܢ ܒܟܽܠܙܒܰܢ ܘܠܳܐ ܡܶܡܬܘܺܡ܂ ܐܶܙܕܰܕܰܩܘ. ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܐܶܢܘܽܢ ܡܰܫ݁ܳܐ ܫܒܺܝܥܳܝܶܐ ܐܽܘܟܳܡܶܐ ܕܰܚܙܰܝܬ܀ ܘܡܰܫ݁ܳܐ ܬܡܢܺܝ̈ܢܳܝܶܐ ܢܰܗܝܺܪ̈ܶܐ ܕܰܚܙܰܝܬ: ܗܝܺ ܗ̱ܝ ܟܺܐܢܘܽܬܶܗ ܘܰܬܪܺܝܨܘܽܬܶܗ ܕܚܶܙܩܺܝܐ ܡܰܠܟ݁ܳܐ ܕܝܰܗܘܽܕܳܐ ܘܛܳܝܒܘܽܬܶܗ ܕ݁ܰܐܠܳܗܳܐ ܕܐܶܬܳܬ ܥܠܘܗ̱ܝ. ܟ݁ܰܕ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܐܶܬܬܙܝܰܥ ܣܰܢܚܶܝܪܝܰܒ ܕܢܺܐܒܰܕ݂ ܘܪܘܰܓ݂ܙܶܗ ܫܰܓܶܫ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܠܶܗ ܕܐܰܝܟ݁ܰܢܳܐ ܢܺܐܒ݂ܰܕ݂ ܘܐܳܦ ܣܘܓܳܐܐ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܕܥܰܡ̱ܡܶܐ ܕܐܝܼܬ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܠܰܘܳܬܶܗ. ܟ݁ܰܕ݂ ܕܝܢ ܫܡܰܥ ܚܶܙܩܺܝܐ ܡܰܠܟܳܐ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܕܡܶܬܚܰܫܰܒ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܐܳܬܘܽܪܳܝܳܐ ܡܰܠܟܿܳܐ ܕܢܺܐܬܶܐ ܘܢܶܐܚܕ݂ܝܰܘܗ̱ܝ ܘܢܰܘܒ݁ܶܕ݂ ܠܥܰܡܶܗ ܠܰܬܪܶܝܢ ܫܒܺܝܛܺܝܢ ܘܦ݁ܶܠܓ݁ܶܗ ܕܐܶܬܚܰܪܘ: ܐܶܠܳܐ ܘܐܳܦ ܨܶܗܝܘܽܢ ܨܳܒ݁ܶܐ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܕܢܶܣܚܘܽܦ. ܗܳܝܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܐܶܬܬܟܶܠ ܚܶܙܩܺܝܐ ܥܰܠ ܥܒ݂ܳܕ݂ܰܘܗ̱ܝ ܘܣܰܒܰܪ ܒܙܰܕܺܝܩܘܽܬܶܗ ܘܐܶܡܰܪ:
LXIII. And the eighth clear waters which you saw, this is the integrity and righteousness of Hezekiah the king of Judah and the goodness [of God] which came upon him. For when Sennacherib was moved to perish, his anger troubled him, how the multitude of the nations which was with him would also perish. But when Hezekiah the king had heard the things which the Assyrian king was thinking: that he would come and capture him and destroy his people, the two tribes and a half which remained, and indeed even wanted to destroy Zion: then Hezekiah trusted in his works and hoped in his righteousness and spoke with the Most Mighty


[p. 1179] {#po.0.p1179}


[p. 1180] {#po.0.p1180}
ܚܙܝ݇ ܕܗܳܐ ܡܛܰܝܰܒ ܣܰܢܚܶܪܝܺܒ ܕܢܰܘܒܕ݂ܰܢ: ܘܢܶܗܘܶܐ ܡܶܫܬ݁ܰܒ݂ܗܰܪ ܘܡܶܬܪܰܡܪܰܡ: ܡܳܐ ܕܐܰܘܒܕ݂ܳܗ ܠܨܶܗܝܘܽܢ. ܘܫܰܡܥܶܗ ܚܰܝܠܬ݁ܳܢܳܐ: ܚܰܟܝܺܡ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܚܶܙܩܺܝܐ: ܘܚܳܪ ܒܒܳܥܘܽܬܶܗ ܡܶܛܽܠ ܕܙܰܕܺܝܩ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ. ܘܰܦܩܰܕ݂ ܗܳܝܕܶܝܢ ܚܰܝܠܬ݁ܳܢܳܐ ܠܪܰܡܺܝܐܶܠ ܡܰܠܰܐܟܶܗ ܕܰܡܡܰܠܶܠ ܥܰܡܳܟ ܘܢܶܦܩܶܬ ܘܐܰܘܒܕ݂ܶܬ ܠܣܘܼܓܳܐܐ ܕܺܝܠܗܘܽܢ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܕ݂ܗܳܘܶܐ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܒܰܠܚܽܘܕ ܡܶܢܝܳܢܳܐ ܕܪܰܘܪ̈ܒܿܳܢܰܝܗܘܽܢ ܡܳܐܐ ܘܰܬܡܳܢܺܝܢ ܘܚܰܡܫܳܐ ܐܰܠܦ̈ܝܢ: ܘܰܠܟ݁ܽܠ ܚܰܕ݂ ܚܰܕ݂ ܡܶܢܗܘܽܢ ܗܳܟ݁ܰܢܳܐ ܐܝܺܬ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܠܶܗ ܒܡܶܢܝܳܢܳܐ. ܘܰܗܘܺܝܬ ܒܗܰܘ ܙܰܒ݂ܢܳܐ ܘܰܥܩܶܕ݂ ܦܰܓܪ̈ܰܝܗܘܽܢ ܡܶܢ ܠܓܰܘ: ܡܳܐܢ̈ܶܐ ܕܝܢ ܘܙܰܝܢܳܐ ܡܶܢ ܠܒܰܪ ܢܳܛܰܪ ܗ̱ܘܺܝܬ: ܡܶܛܽܠ ܕܝܰܬܝܺܪܳܐܝܺܬ ܬܶܕ݂ܡܘܳܬܶܗ ܕܚܰܝܠܬ݁ܳܢܳܐ ܢܶܬܚܰܙ̈ܝܳܢ: ܘܐܰܝܟ݁ܰܢܳܐ ܕܢܶܬܡܰܠܰܠ ܫܡܶܗ ܒܟܘܽܠܳܗ ܐܰܪܥܳܐ. ܐܶܬܦܰܪܩܰܬ ܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܨܶܗܝܘܽܢ: ܘܐܘܽܪܺܝܫܠܶܡ܂ ܐܶܬܦܰܨܝܰܬ: ܐܳܦ ܐܝܺܣܪܳܝܶܠ ܐܶܬܚܰܪܰܪ ܡܶܢ ܐܘܽܠܨܳܢܳܐ: ܘܰܚܕ݂ܺܝܘ ܟܘܽܠܗܘܽܢ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܐܝܺܬ ܗ̱ܘܰܘ ܒܐܰܪܥܳܐ ܩܰܕܺܝܫܬ݁ܳܐ ܘܐܶܫܬ݁ܰܒ݁ܰܚ ܫܡܶܗ ܕܚܰܝܠܬ݁ܳܢܳܐ ܐܰܝܟ ܕܢܶܬܡܰܠܰܠ. ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܐܶܢܘܽܢ ܡܰܫ̈ܳܐ
and said: See, behold Sennacherib is prepared to destroy us; and he will be proud and exalting himself when he has destroyed Zion. And the Most Mighty heard him, for Hezekiah was wise, and He regarded his supplication, because he was righteous. And the Most Mighty then commanded Ramiel His angel, who speaks with you, and I went out and destroyed their multitude, whose number of princes alone was one hundred and eighty-five thousand, and each one of them had such a number. And at that time I burned their bodies within, but I kept the garments and weapons without, so that the wonders of the Most Mighty might become the more conspicuous and [that] His name might be told in the whole earth. But Zion was saved, and Jerusalem was rescued, and Israel also was delivered from the tribulations. And all who were in the holy land rejoiced, and the name of the Most Mighty was glorified, so that it was told. These are the clear waters which you saw.
ܢܰܗܝܺܪܶܐ ܕܰܚܙܰܝܬ܀ ܘܡܰܝ̈ܳܐ ܕܬܶܫܥܳܐ ܐܘܿܟ̈ܡܶܐ ܕܰܚܙܰܝܬ: ܗܳܕܶܐ ܗ̱ܝ ܟܘܽܠܶܗ ܪܘܽܫܥܳܐ ܕܰܗܘܳܐ ܒܝܰܘ̈ܡܳܬܶܗ ܕܰܡܢܰܫܶܐ ܒܰܪ ܚܶܙܩܺܝܳܐ. ܡܶܛܽܠ ܕܣܰܓܺܝ ܐܰܪܫܰܥ ܘܙܰܕ̈ܝ̈ܩܶܐ ܩܛܰܠ ܘܕܺܝܢܳܐ ܨܠܳܐ ܘܰܕ݂ܡܳܐ ܙܰܟܺܝܳܐ ܐܶܫܰܕ݂ ܘܢܶܫܰܝ̈ ܓܰܒܪ̈ܶܐ ܒܰܩܛܺܝܪܳܐ ܛܰܡܰܐ ܘܣܰܚܶܦ ܡܰܕ݂ܒܰܚ̈ܶܐ ܘܩܘܽܪ̈ܒܳܢܰܝܗܘܽܢ ܒܰܛܶܠ: ܘܟܳܗ̈ܢܶܐ ܐܰܦܶܩ ܕܠܳܐ ܢܫܰܡܫܘܽܢ ܒܶܝܬ ܡܰܩܕ݂ܫܳܐ. ܘܰܥܒܰܕ݂ ܕܰܠܡܳܐ ܕܚܰܡܫܳܐ ܐܰܦܺܝܢ: ܐܰܪܒܰܥ ܡܶܢܗܶܝܢ ܚܳܝܪܳܢ ܠܰܐܰܪܒܰܥ ܙ̈ܘܽܚܚܺܝܢ: ܘܰܚܡܺܝܫܳܝܬܳܐ ܥܰܠ ܪܺܝܫܶܗ ܕܨܰܠܡܳܐ ܐܰܝܟ ܕܠܘܽܩܒܰܠ ܛܢܳܢܶܗ ܕܚܰܝܠܬ݁ܳܢܳܐ. ܘܗܳܝܕܶܝܢ ܢܦܰܩ ܪܽܘܓܙܳܐ ܡܶܢ ܩܕ݂ܳܡ ܚܰܝܠܬ݁ܳܢܳܐ ܐܰܝܟ ܕܐܶܬܥܰܩܪܰܬ ܨܶܗܝܘܽܢ: ܐܰܝܟ ܕܐܳܦ ܓܕܰܫ ܒܝܰܘ̈ܡܳܬܟܘܽܢ. ܐܶܠܳܐ ܐܳܦ݂ ܥܰܠ ܬ݁ܪܶܝܢ ܫܰܒ̈ܛܺܝܢ ܘܦ݁ܶܠܓ݁ܳܐ ܢܦܰܩ ܓ݁ܙܳܪ ܕܺܝܢܳܐ ܕܐܳܦ ܗܶܢܘܽܢ ܢܶܫܬܒܘܽܢ: ܐܰܝܟ ܕܰܚܙܰܝܬ ܗܳܫܳܐ. ܘܰܒܕܰܡܳܝܐ ܠܗܳܟܰܢܳܐ ܣܓܺܝ ܪܘܽܫܥܶܗ ܕܰܡܢܰܫܶܐ: ܐܰܝܟ݂ ܕܐܶܫܒܘܽܚܬܶܗ ܕ݁ܰܡܪܰܚܡܳܐ ܬ݁ܰܪܚܶܩ ܡܶܢ ܒܶܝܬ ܡܰܩܕ݂ܫܳܐ. ܡܶܛܽܠ ܗܳܢܳܐ ܡܢܰܫܶܐ ܒܗܳܢܐ ܙܰܒܢܳܐ ܐܶܫܬ݁ܰܡܰܗ ܪܰܫܺܝܥܳܐ: ܘܒ݁ܰܚܪܺܝܬ݁ܳܐ ܒܢܘܽܪܳܐ ܗܘܳܐ ܡܰܥܡܪܶܗ. ܟܰܕ݂ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܐܶܫܬܰܡܥܰܬ ܒܳܥܘܽܬܶܗ ܠܘܳܬ ܡܪܰܚܡܳܐ: ܒܰܚܪܳܝܬܳܐ ܟ݁ܰܕ݂ ܢܦܰܠ ܒܣܘܽܣܝܳܐ ܕܰܢܚܳܫܳܐ ܘܐܶܬܦܰܫܰܪ ܣܘܽܣܝܳܐ
LXIV. And the ninth black waters which you saw, this is all the impiety which was in the days of Manasseh the son of Hezekiah. For he acted very impiously and killed the righteous and turned aside judgment and shed innocent blood and violently defiled married women and overturned the altars and abolished their offerings and cast out the priests, that they might not minister in the sanctuary. And he made an image of five faces; four of them looked to the four winds and the fifth upon the top of the image as if against the zeal of the Most Mighty. And then anger went forth from the presence of the Most Mighty, so that Zion might be eradicated, just as also happened in your days. But also against the two tribes and a half the sentence went forth, that they also should be led away captive, as you have seen now. And the impiety of Manasseh was multiplied to such an extent that the Glory of the Most High departed from the sanctuary. Therefore Manasseh was called impious at that time, and in the end his dwelling was in the fire. For although his supplication was heard by the Most High in the end, when he fell into the brazen horse and the brazen horse was melted and a sign was made to him for an hour. For he did not live perfectly, nor was he worthy, but that from that time he might know in the end by whom he was to be tormented. For He who can do good, can also torment.


[p. 1181] {#po.0.p1181}


[p. 1182] {#po.0.p1182}


[p. 1183] {#po.0.p1183}
ܕ݁ܰܢܚܳܫܳܐ ܘܰܗܘܳܐ ܠܶܗ ܐܳܬܳܐ ܠܥܳܠܡܳܐ. ܠܳܐ ܘܳܐ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܡܫܰܠܡܳܢܳܐܝܺܬ ܚܝܳܐ: ܐܳܦ ܠܳܐ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܫܳܘܶܐ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ: ܐܶܠܳܐ ܕܡܶܢ ܗܳܕܶܐ ܢܶܕ݂ܰܥ ܕܒܰܐ̱ܚܪܺܝܬܳܐ ܡܶܢ ܡܰܢܘܽ ܐܝܺܬ ܠܶܗ ܕܢܶܫܬܰܢܰܩ. ܗܰܘ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܕ݁ܰܡܨܶܐ ܕܢܰܛܺܝܒ: ܐܳܦ ܕ݁ܰܢܫܰܢܶܩ ܡܨܶܐ܀ ܗܳܟ݁ܰܢܳܐ ܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܡܰܪܫܰܥ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܗܘܽ ܡܢܰܫܶܐ ܘܡܶܬܚܰܫܰܒ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܒܙܰܒܢܶܗ ܐܝܟ ܗܰܘ ܕܠܳܐ
LXV. For thus Manasseh acted impiously and thought in his time, just
ܥܬܝّܕ݂ ܚܰܝܠܬ݁ܳܢܳܐ ܕܢܰܒܥܳܐ ܐܶܢܶܝ̈ܢ ܠܗܳܠܶܝܢ. ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܐܶܢܘܽܢ ܡܰܝ̈ܳܐ ܐܽܘܟ̈ܳܡܶܐ ܬܫܝ̈ܥܳܝܶܐ
as if it were not to be that the Most Mighty would inquire into these things. These are the ninth black waters which you saw.
ܕܰܚܙܰܝܬ. ܘܡܰܝ̈ܳܐ ܥܣܺܝܪ̈ܳܝܶܐ ܢܰܗܺܝܪ̈ܶܐ ܕܰܚܙܰܝܬ: ܗܳܕ݂ܶܐ ܗܺܝ ܫܰܦܝܘܽܬܳܐ ܕܐܰܘ̈ܠܕ݂ܰܘܗ̱ܝ ܕܝܘܽܫܺܝܐ: ܡܰܠܟܳܐ ܕܝܺܗܘܽܕܳܐ܆ ܕܰܒܗܰܘ ܙܰܒܢܳܐ ܗܘ ܒܰܠܚܘܕ݁ܽܘܗ̱ܝ ܡܫܰܥܒܰܕ݂ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܠܚܰܝܠܬ݁ܳܢܳܐ ܡܶܢ ܟ݁ܽܠܶܗ ܠܶܒ݁ܶܗ ܘܡܶܢ ܟ݁ܽܠܳܗ ܢܰܦܫܶܗ. ܘܗܘّ ܕܰܟܝܺ ܠܰܐܪܥܳܐ ܡܶܢ ܦܬܰܟܪ̈ܶܐ ܘܩܰܕܶܫ ܟܽܠܗܘܽܢ ܡܳܐܢ̈ܶܐ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܐܶܣܬܰܝܰܒܘ: ܘܩܘܽܪ̈ܒ݁ܳܢܶܐ ܦܰܢܝܺ ܠܡܰܕ݂ܒܚܳܐ ܘܐܰܪܚܺܝܡ ܩܰܪܢܳܐ ܕܰܩܕܺܝ̈ܫܶܐ ܘܪܰܡܪܶܡ ܠܙܰܕ݁ܺܝܩ̈ܶܐ ܘܫܰܒܰܚ ܠܟܘܽܠܗܘܽܢ ܚܰܟܺܝ̈ܡܶܐ ܒܰܣܽܘܟܳܠܬ݁ܳܢܘܽܬܳܐ: ܘܐܰܗܦ݁ܺܝܟ ܟܳܗܢ̈ܶܐ ܠܬܶܫܡܶܫܬܗܘܽܢ: ܘܰܥܩܰܪ ܘܐܰܥܒܰܪ ܐܰܫܘܳܦ̈ܶܐ ܘܚܰܪ̈ܳܫܶܐ ܘܙܳܟܘܪ̈ܶܐ ܡܶܢ ܐܰܪܥܳܐ. ܘܠܳܐ ܙܕ݂ܶܩ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܒܰܠܚܘܽܕ݂ ܠܕ̈ܰܫܚܺܝ̈ܢܶܐ ܕܚܰܝ̈ܝܢ ܗ̱ܘܰܘ ܩܛܰܠ: ܘܐܳܦ ܓܰܪ̈ܡܝܗܘܽܢ ܕܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܡܺܝܬܘ: ܐܰܦ݁ܰܩܘ ܡܶܢ ܩܰܒܪ̈ܶܐ ܘܰܐܘܩܶܕ݂ܘ ܐܶܢܘܽܢ ܒܢܘܽܪܳܐ. ܘܥܺܐܕ݂̈ܶܐ
LXVI. And the tenth clear waters which you saw, this is the purity of the generations of Josiah the king of Judah, who at that time alone was subject to the Most Mighty with all his heart and with all his soul. And he himself purged the land of idols and sanctified all the vessels which had been defiled and restored the offerings to the altar and exalted the horn of the holy ones and lifted up the righteous and glorified all the wise in understanding and brought back the priests to their ministry and blotted out and removed the enchanters and sorcerers and soothsayers from the land. And not only did he kill the impious who were alive, but also the bones of those who were dead they drew out of the sepulchers and burned them with fire. And he established feasts and sabbaths with their sacred rites and burned the defiled with fire, and the false prophets who were deceiving the people, even these he burned with fire, and the people who obeyed them, while they lived, he cast into the valley of Kidron and heaped stones upon them. And he was zealous with the zeal of the Most Mighty with all his soul, and he showed himself alone to be firm in the law at that time, so that he did not leave anyone uncircumcised or


[p. 1184] {#po.0.p1184}


[p. 1185] {#po.0.p1185}
ܐܰܘܩܶܕ݂ ܒܢܘܽܪܳܐ: ܘܠܰܢܒܺܝ̈ܶܐ ܪܰܓ݁ܳܠܶܐ ܕܡܰܛܥܶܝܢ ܗ̱ܘܰܘ ܠܥܰܡܳܐ: ܐܳܦ ܠܗܳܢܘܽܢ ܐܰܘܩܶܕ݂ ܐܶܢܘܽܢ ܒܢܘܽܪܳܐ: ܘܰܠܥܰܡܳܐ ܕܡܶܫܬܡܰܥ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܠܗܘܽܢ: ܟ݁ܰܕ݂ ܚܰܝ̈ܝܢ ܐܰܪܡܺܝ ܐܶܢܘܽܢ ܒܢܰܚܠܳܐ ܕ݁ܩܶܕܪܘܽܢ ܘܰܟܢܰܫ ܥܠܰܝܗܘܢ ܟܺܐܦ̈ܶܐ. ܘܛܢ ܛܢܳܢܶܗ ܕ݁ܚܰܝܠܬ݁ܳܢܳܐ ܡܶܢ ܟ݀ܽܠܳܗ ܢܰܦܫܶܗ ܘܐܶܫܬܰܪܰܪ ܒܰܠܚܘܽܕܰܘܗ̱ܝ ܒܢܳܡܘܽܣܳܐ ܒܗܰܘ ܙܰܒ݂ܢܳܐ: ܐܰܝܟ ܠܳܐ ܢܶܫܒܘܽܩ ܠܐ݈ܢܳܫ ܥܘܽܪܠܳܐ ܐܰܘ ܕܡܰܡܪܰܫܰܥ ܒܟ݁ܽܠܳܗ ܐܰܪܥܳܐ ܟܘܽܠܗܘܽܢ ܝܰܘ̈ܡܳܬܳܐ ܕܚܰܝ̈ܘܗ̱ܝ. ܗܳܢܰܘ ܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܕܢܶܣܰܒ ܐܰܓ݂ܪܳܐ ܕ݁ܰܠܥܳܠܰܡ ܘܗܘ ܢܶܫܬ݁ܰܒ݁ܰܚ ܠܘܳܬ ܚܰܝܠܬ݁ܳܢܳܐ ܝܰܬܝܰܪ ܡܶܢ ܣܰܓ̈ܺܝܐܶܐ ܒܙܰܒܢܳܐ ܐ̱ܚܪܳܝܳܐ. ܡܶܛܽܠܳܬܶܗ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܕܗܳܢܶܐ ܘܡܶܛܽܠ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܕ݂ܳܡܶܝܢ ܠܶܗ: ܐܶܬܒܪ̈ܝܺ ܘܐܶܬܥܰܬ݁ܕ݂ܰܝ ܬܶܫܒܺܚܳܬ݁ܳܐ ܡܝܰܩܪ̈ܳܬܳܐ
acting impiously in the whole land all the days of his life. But this is he who will receive the eternal reward, and he himself will be glorified before the Most Mighty more than many in the last time. For his sake and for the sake of those who are like him, the most precious glories, which were foretold to you before, were created and prepared. These are the clear waters which you saw.
ܘܡܰܝ̈ܳܐ ܕܰܚܕ݂ܰܥܣܰܪ ܐܘܽܟܳܡܶܐ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܐܶܢܘܽܢ ܡܰܝ̈ܳܐ ܢܰܗܝܺܪ̈ܶܐ ܕܰܚܙܰܝܬ܀ ` ܘܡܰܝ̈ܳܐ ܕܰܚܕ݂ܰܥܣܰܪ ܐܘܽܟܳܡܶܐ ܕܰܚܙܰܝܬ: ܗܳܕܳܐ ܗ̱ܝ ܒܚܺܫܬܳܐ ܕܓ݂ܳܕ݂ܫܳܐ ܗܳܟܳܝܐ ܠܨܲܗܝܘܽܢ. ܐܰܘ ܣܳܒܪܰܐ ܕܠܰܝܬ ܠܗܘܢ ܥܳܩܬܳܐ ܠܡܰܠܰܐܟ݁ܶܐ ܩܕ݂ܳܡ ܚܰܝܠܬ݁ܳܢܳܐ ܕܗܳܟ݁ܰܢܳܐ ܐܶܫܬܰܠܡܰܬ ܨܶܗܝܘܽܢ: ܘܰܕܗܳܐ ܥܰܡ̱ܡܶܐ ܡܶܫܬܰܒܗܪܝܺܢ ܒܠܶܒܰܘܳܬܗܘܽܢ ܘܟܶܢ̈ܫܶܐ ܩܕ݂ܳܡ ܦܬܰܟܪ̈ܰܝܗܘܽܢ ܘܐܳܡܪܝܺܢ: ܐܶܬܕܝܺܫܰܬ ܗܳܝ ܕܙܰܒ݂ܢܳܐ ܣܰܓ݁ܝܳܐܳܐ ܕܳܫܰܬ: ܘܐܶܫܬܰܥܒܰܕ ܗܳܝ ܕܰܡܫܰܥܒܕ݂ܳܐ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ܆ ܣܳܒܪܰܐ ܕܰܒܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܚܳܕ݂ܶܐ ܡܪܺܝܡܳܐ܆ ܐܰܘ ܫܶܡܗ ܡܶܫܬܰܒܰܚ ܥܰܠ ܐܶܠܳܐ ܡܳܢܳܐ ܢܶܗܘܶܐ ܠܕ݂ܝܺܢܶܗ ܟܺܐܢܳܐ܆ ܐܶܠܳܐ ܡܶܢ ܒܳܬܰܪ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ: ܢܶܬܬܰܚܕܘܽܢ ܒܐܘܽܠܨܳܢܳܐ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܐܶܬܒܰܕ݂ܰܪܘ ܒܶܝܬ ܥܰܡ̱ܡܶܐ: ܘܰܒܚܺܝܣܕ݂ܳܐ ܢܶܥܡܪܘܽܢ ܒܟ݁ܽܠ ܐܰܬܰܪ܆ ܡܶܛܽܠ ܕܰܟܡܳܝܐ ܕܨܶܗܝܘܽܢ ܡܰܫܠܡܳܐ ܗ̱ܝ
LXVII. And the eleventh black waters which you saw, this is the calamity which has happened now to Zion. Or do you think there is no sadness to the Angels themselves before the Most Mighty, that Zion has been delivered up in this way and that behold the nations boast in their hearts and the crowds before their idols and say: She is trampled who long trampled, and she is subdued who was subduing? Do you think the Most High rejoices in these things, or that His name is glorified? But what will it be in His just judgment? Nevertheless, after these things, those who are dispersed among the nations will be caught in tribulation, and they will dwell in ignominy everywhere. For while


[p. 1186] {#po.0.p1186}


[p. 1187] {#po.0.p1187}
ܘܐܽܘܪܽܫܠܶܡ ܚܰܪܒܳܐ ܗ̱ܝ ܘܰܦܬܰܟܪ݁ܶܐ ܒܰܡܕ݂ܺܝܢܳܬ݁ܳܐ ܕܥܰܡ̱ܡܶܐ ܡܰܨܠܚܝܺܢ: ܘܥܶܛܪܳܐ ܕܐܶܢܳܢܳܐ ܕܒܶܣ̈ܡܶܐ ܕܙܰܕ݂ܝܺܩܘܽܬܳܐ ܕܡܶܢ ܢܳܡܘܽܣܳܐ ܕܥܶܟ ܡܶܢ ܨܲܗܝܘܽܢ ܘܰܒܐܰܬܪܳܗ ܕܨܲܗܝܘܽܢ ܒܟܽܠ ܕܘܽܟ: ܗܳܐ ܬܶܢܳܢܳܐ ܕܪܘܽܫܥܳܐ ܐܝܺܬ ܒܳܗ. ܐܶܠܳܐ ܢܩܘܽܡ ܡܰܠܟܳܐ ܕܒܳܒ݂ܺܝܠ ܗܰܘ ܕ݁ܣܰܝܶܦ ܗܳܢܳܐ ܠܨܲܗܝܘܽܢ ܘܢܶܫܬܰܒܗܰܪ ܥܰܠ ܥܰܡܳܐ ܘܰܢܡܰܠܠ ܪܰܘܪ̈ܒܳܬ݂ܳܐ ܒܠܶܒܶܗ ܩܕ݂ܳܡ ܡܪܰܝܡܳܐ: ܐܳܦ ܗܘ
Zion is delivered up and Jerusalem is laid waste and the idols in the cities of the nations prosper and the vapor of the smoke of the offerings of righteousness from the law is extinguished from Zion and in the region of Zion everywhere: behold the smoke of impiety is in it. But the king of Babylon, who has now destroyed Zion, will rise, and he will boast of the people and will speak great things in his heart before the Most High. But even he himself will fall in the end. These are the black waters.
ܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܒܰܐܚܪܳܝܬ݁ܳܐ ܢܶܦܶܠ. ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܐܶܢܶܝܢ ܡܰܝ̈ܳܐ ܐܘ̈ܽܟܳܡܶܐ܀ ܘܡܰܝ̈ܳܐ ܕܰܬܪܶܥܣܰܪ ܢܰܗܝܺܪ̈ܶܐ ܕܰܚܙܰܝܬ: ܗܳܕܳܐ ܗ̱ܝ ܡܶܠܬ݁ܳܐ: ܢܺܐܬܶܐ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܙܰܒܢܳܐ ܡܶܢ ܒܳܬܰܪ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܘܢܶܦ݁ܶܠ ܥܰܡܳܟ ܠܰܐܢܰܢܩܺܐ ܐܰܝܟ ܕܩܢܕ݂ܺܝܢܘܽܣ ܢܶܥܒܕܘܽܢ: ܕܢܺܐܒܕܘܽܢ ܟܽܠܗܘܽܢ ܐܰܟܚܕ݂ܳܐ. ܒܪܰܡ ܕܶܝܢ ܢܶܬܦܰܪܩܘܽܢ: ܘܰܒܥܶܠܕܒܳܒܰܝ̈ܗܘܽܢ ܩܕ݂ܳܡܰܝܗܘܽܢ ܢܶܦܠܘܽܢ: ܘܢܶܗܘܶܐ ܠܗܘܽܢ ܒ݁ܰܙܒܰܢ ܒܘܽܣܳܡܳܐ ܣܰܓܝܐܳܐ. ܘܰܒܗܰܘ ܙܰܒܢܳܐ ܒܳܬܰܪ ܩܰܠܺܝܠ ܬܘܒ ܬܶܬܒ݂ܢܶܐ ܨܲܗܝܘܽܢ ܘܢܶܬܩܢܘܽܢ ܬܘܽܒ ܩܘܽܪ̈ܒܳܢܶܝܗ: ܘܟ݂ܳܗ̈ܢܶܐ ܢܶܗܦܟܘܽܢ ܠܬܫܡܶܫܬܗܘܽܢ: ܘܬܘܒ ܢܺܐܬܘܽܢ ܥܰܡ̱ܡܶܐ ܐܰܝܟ ܕܢܶܫܒܘܽܢܳܗ. ܒܪܰܡ ܕܶܝܢ ܠܳܐ ܡܰܠܝܳܐܝܺܬ ܐܰܝܟ ܕܰܒܩܰܕ݂ܡܳܝܬܳܐ. ܐܶܠܳܐ ܢܶܗܘܶܐ ܡܶܢ ܒܳܬܰܪ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ: ܬܶܗܘܶܐ ܡܰܦܘܽܠܬܳܐ ܕܥܰܡ̱ܡܶܐ ܣܰܓܝ̈ܳܐܶܐ. ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܐܶܢܘܽܢ ܡܰܝ̈ܳܐ ܢܰܗܝܺܪ̈ܶܐ ܕܰܚܙܰܝܬ܀
LXVIII. And the twelfth clear waters which you saw, this is the word: For the time will come after these things and your people will fall into calamity, so that all will be in danger of perishing together. Nevertheless, they will be saved and their enemies will fall before them. And there will be for them at some time much joy. And at that time after a little while Zion will be built again and its offerings will be established again and the priests will return to their ministry, and again the nations will come to lead it captive, but not fully, as in the beginning. But it will be after these things, there will be the ruin of many nations. These are the clear waters which you saw.


[p. 1188] {#po.0.p1188}


[p. 1189] {#po.0.p1189}
ܡܰܝ̈ܳܐ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܐ̱ܚܪ̈ܳܝܶܐ ܕܰܚܙܰܝܬ: ܕܐܘܿܟܵܡܝܢ ܙܗ̱ܘܰܘ ܡܶܢ ܟܽܠܗܘܽܢ ܗܳܢܘܽܢ ܕܰܩܕܳܡܰܝܗܘܽܢ: ܗܳܢܘܽܢ ܕܰܗܘܰܘ ܡܶܢ ܒܳܬܰܪ ܡܶܢܝܳܢܳܐ ܕܰܬܪܶܥܣܺܝܪ ܕܰܟܢܝܫܳܢ ܗ̱ܘܰܘ ܐܰܟܚܕ݂ܳܐ: ܕܟܘܽܠܶܗ ܐܳܢܘܽܢ ܥܳܠܡܳܐ. ܦܰܠܶܓ݂ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܡܪܰܝܡܳܐ ܡܶܢ ܩܕ݂ܺܝܡ: ܡܶܛܽܠ ܕܗܘܰܝܘܽ ܒܰܠܚܘܕܰܘܗ̱ܝ ܝܳܕ݂ܰܥ ܡܶܕ݁ܶܡ ܕܰܥܬܝܰܕ݂ ܕܶܓܕܰܫ. ܕܒܢܺܝܫܳܬ݁ܳܐ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܕܪ̈ܘܥܶܐ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܰܥܬܝܰܕ݂ܝܺܢ ܗ̱ܘܰܘ ܕܢܶܗܘܘܽܢ ܩܕ݂ܳܡܰܘܗ̱ܝ: ܩܵܕ݁ܶܡ ܚܙܳܐ ܕܘܰܒܳܪ̈ܰܝܗܘܽܢ ܫܬܳܐ. ܘܕ݂ܰܥܒܳܕ݂ܶܐ ܛܳܒ̈ܶܐ ܕܙܰܕܝܳܩ̈ܶܐ ܥܬܝܰܕ݂ܝܺܢ ܕܢܶܫܬܰܠܡܘܽܢ ܩܕ݂ܳܡܰܘܗ̱ܝ: ܩܰܕ݁ܶܡ ܚܙܳܐ ܕܘܽܒܳܪ̈ܰܝܗܘܽܢ ܫܬܳܐ: ܣܛܰܪ ܡܶܢ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܰܥܬܝܰܕ݂ܝܺܢ ܕ݁ܢܶܥܒ݁ܶܕ݂ ܒܫܘܽܠܳܡ
LXIX. For the last waters which you saw, which were black above all those former ones, those which were after the twelfth number, which were collected together, are of the whole world. For the Most High divided from the beginning, because He alone knows what is to happen. For of the outrages of the impieties which were to be in His sight, He foresaw six modes, and of the good works of the righteous which were to be performed in His sight, He foresaw six modes, besides those which He is to do at the end of the age. For this reason, not black waters with black, nor clear with clear: for it is the end.
ܕܥܳܠܡܳܐ. ܡܶܛܽܠ ܗܳܢܳܐ ܠܳܐ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܰܡܫܰܚܠܦܳܢ ܐܰܝܟ݂ ܡܰܝ̈ܳܐ ܐܘܽܟܳܡܶܐ ܥܰܡ ܐܘܰܟܳܡܶܐ: ܘܠܳܐ ܢܰܗܝܺܪ̈ܶܐ ܥܰܡ ܢܰܗܝܳܪ̈ܶܐ: ܫܘܽܠܳܡܵܐ ܗ̱ܘ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ܀
LXX. Hear therefore the interpretation


[p. 1190] {#po.0.p1190}
ܫܡܰܥ ܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܦܽܘܫܳܩܳܐ ܕܡܰܝ̈ܳܐ ܐ̱ܚܪ̈ܳܝܶܐ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܕܰܥܬܝܰܕ݂ܝܺܢ ܕܢܺܐܬܘܽܢ ܡܶܢ ܒܳܬܰܪ ܐܘܰܟܳܡܶܐ: ܗܳܕܳܐ ܗ̱ܝ ܡܶܠܬ݁ܳܐ: ܗܳܐ ܝܰܘ̈ܡܳܬܳܐ ܐܳܬܿܶܝܢ: ܘܢܶܗܘܶܐ ܟ݁ܰܕ݂ ܢܶܒܫܰܠ ܙܰܒ݂ܢܶܗ ܕܥܳܠܡܳܐ: ܘܢܺܐܬ݁ܶܐ ܚܨܳܕ݂ܳܐ ܕܙܰܪܥܰܘܗ̱ܝ ܕܒܢܰ̈ܝ ܐ̱ܢܳܫܳܐ ܘܰܕܛܳܒ̈ܶܐ: ܢܰܝܬܶܐ ܚܰܝܠܬ݁ܳܢܳܐ ܥܰܠ ܐܰܪܥܳܐ ܘܥܳܡܘܽܪ̈ܶܝܗ ܘܥܰܠ ܡܕ݂ܰܒܪ̈ܳܢܶܝܗ ܫܓܝܳܫܘܽܬܳܐ ܕܪܘܺܚܳܐ ܘܰܬܘܰܗܬܳܐ ܕܠܶܒܳܐ ܘܢܶܣܢܘܽܢ ܚܰܕ݂ ܠܚܰܕ݂ ܘܢܶܬܓܰܪܓܺܝܘܽܢ ܚܰܕ݂ ܥܰܠ ܚܰܕ݂ ܠܰܩܪܳܒܳܐ.
of the last black waters, which are to come [after the black ones]. This is the word: Behold, days will come, and it will be when the time of the age has ripened and the harvest of its seeds of evil and good has come, the Most Mighty will bring upon the earth and its inhabitants and upon its governors confusion of spirit and amazement of heart, and they will have hatred for one another


[p. 1191] {#po.0.p1191}
ܘܢܶܫܬܰܠܛܘܽܢ ܡܨܰܥܪ̈ܶܐ ܥܰܠ ܡܝܰܩܪ̈ܶܐ ܘܢܶܬܬܪܝܡܘܽܢ ܙܥܘܽܪ̈ܶܐ ܥܰܠ ܡܫܰܒܺܚ̈ܶܐ: ܘܢܶܫܬܰܠܡܘܽܢ ܣܰܓܝ̈ܺܝܐܶܐ ܠܰܙܥܘܽܪ̈ܶܐ: ܘܢܶܫܬܰܠܛܘܽܢ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܠܳܐ ܗܘܰܘ ܡܶܕܶܡ ܥܰܠ
and will provoke one another to battle, and the ignoble will rule over the honored, and the lowly will be exalted over the glorious, and many will be delivered to the few, and those who were nothing will rule over the powerful, and the poor will abound over the rich, and the impious will prevail over the heroes, and the wise will be silent and the foolish


[p. 1192] {#po.0.p1192}
ܓܰܢ̱ܒܳܪ̈ܶܐ: ܘܢܶܬܝܰܬܪܘܽܢ ܡܣܰܟܢ̈ܶܐ ܥܰܠ ܥܰܬܝܪ̈ܶܐ: ܘܢܶܬܓܰܒܪܘܽܢ ܪ̈ܰܫܺܝܥܶܐ ܥܰܠ ܓܰܢ̱ܒܳܪ̈ܶܐ: ܘܢܶܫܬܩܘܽܢ ܚܰܟܺܝܡ̈ܶܐ: ܘܣܰܟ݁ܺܠܶܐ ܢܡܰܠ̱ܠܘܽܢ ܘܠܳܐ ܬ݁ܶܫܬ݁ܰܪܰܪ ܗܳܝܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܡܰܚܫܰܒܬܳܐ ܕܰܒܢܰ̈ܝ ܐ̱ܢܳܫܳܐ: ܐܳܦ݂ ܠܳܐ ܬܰܪܥܺܝܬܳܐ ܕܚܰܝܠܬ݁ܳܢܶܐ: ܘܠܳܐ ܢܶܫܬ݁ܰܪܰܪ ܣܰܒܪܗܘܽܢ ܕܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܰܡܣܰܒܪܝܺܢ. ܘܢܶܗܘܶܐ ܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܡܳܐ ܕܰܗܘܰܝ̈ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܕܰܡܩܰܕ݁̈ܡܳܢ ܐܶܬ݂ܡܰܠܰܠܝܺܢ:
will speak, nor will the thought of men be confirmed then, nor the counsel of the strong, nor will the hope of those who hope be verified. But it will be, when the things which have been foretold have come to pass, that confusion will fall upon all men, and some of them will fall in battle and some will perish in distress and some will be hindered by those who are their own. But the Most High will signify to those peoples whom He prepared before, and they will come and fight with the leaders who remain then.
ܒܰܩܪܳܒܳܐ ܢܦܠܘܽܢ: ܘܡܶܢܗܘܽܢ ܒ݁ܥܳܩ̈ܳܬܳܐ ܢܶܬܚܰܒܠܘܽܢ: ܘܡܶܢܗܘܽܢ ܡܶܢ ܐܰܝܠܺܝܢ ܕܕܝܳܠܗܘܽܢ ܐܶܢܘܽܢ ܢܶܬܥܰܘܟܘܽܢ. ܢܶܓܙܶܐ ܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܡܪܰܝܡܳܐ ܠܗܳܢܘܽܢ ܥܰܡ݈ܡ̈ܶܐ ܕܩܰܕܶܡ ܥܰܬܶܕ݂: ܘܢܺܐܬ݂ܘܳܢ ܘܢܰܩܪܒܘܽܢ ܥܰܡ ܡܕ݂ܰܒ݁ܪ̈ܳܢܶܐ ܕܡܶܫܬ݁ܰܚܪܝܺܢ ܗܳܝܕܶܝܢ. ܘܢܶܗܘܶܐ ܕܟܽܠ ܡܢ ܕܡܶܬܦ݁ܰܨܶܐ ܡܶܢ ܩܪܳܒܳܐ ܒܙܰܘܥܳܐ ܢܡܘܽܬ: ܘܰܕ݂ܡܶܬܦ݁ܰܨܳܐ ܡܶܢ ܙܰܘܥܳܐ ܒܢܘܽܪܳܐ ܢܺܐܩܰܕ݂: ܘܐܰܝܢܳܝܐ ܕܡܶܬܦܰܨܶܐ ܡܶܢ ܢܘܽܪܳܐ ܒܟܰܦܢܳܐ ܢܣܝܘܰܦ. ܘܢܶܗܘܶܐ ܕܟ݁ܽܠ ܡܰܢ ܕܡܶܬܦ݁ܰܨܶܐ ܘܥܳܪܶܩ ܡܶܢ ܟܽܠܗܶܝܢ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕ݁ܰܡܩܰܕ݁̈ܡܳܢ ܐܶܬ݂ܡܰܠܰܠܝܺܢ: ܡܶܢ ܐܰܝܠܺܝܢ ܕ݁ܰܙܟ݂ܰܘ ܘܐܶܙܕܟܧܝܘ: ܢܶܫܬܰܠܡܘܽܢ ܠܐܚܺܝܕ݂ܰܘܗ̱ܝ ܕܥܰܒܕܝ ܡܫܺܝܚܳܐ. ܬܶܒܠܰܥ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܟ݁ܽܠ ܐܰܪܥܳܐ ܥܳܡܘܽܪ̈ܶܝܗ.
And it will be, whoever escapes from the war will die in an earthquake, and he who escapes from the earthquake will be burned by fire, and he who has escaped from the fire will perish by famine. And it will be, whoever escapes and flees from all these things foretold, of those who conquered and were conquered: they will be delivered into the hands of my servant the Messiah. For the whole earth will devour its inhabitants.


[p. 1193] {#po.0.p1193}
ܠܥܳܡܘܽܪ̈ܶܝܗ܀ ܐܰܪܥܳܐ ܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܩܰܕ݂ܝܰܫܬ݁ܳܐ ܬ݁ܶܪܚܰܡ ܥܰܠ ܕ݂ܝܽܠܳܗ
LXXI. But the holy land will have mercy on its own and will protect its inhabitants at that time. This is the vision,
ܗܳܢܰܘ ܚܶܙܘܳܐ ܕܰܚܙܰܝܬ ܘܗܳܢܰܘ ܦܘܽܫܳܩܶܗ. ܐܶܬܺܝܬ ܕܺܝܢ ܐܶܢܳܐ ܕܐܶܡܰܪ ܠܺܝܟ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܡܶܛܘܽܠ ܕܐܶܫܬܰܡܥܰܬ ܒܳܥܘܽܬ݁ܳܟ݂
which you saw, and this is its interpretation. But I have come to tell you these things, because your petition has been heard before the Most High.


[p. 1194] {#po.0.p1194}
ܠܘܳܬ ܡܪܰܝܡܳܐ܀ ܫܡܰܥ ܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܐܳܦ ܥܰܠ ܡܰܝ̈ܳܐ ܢܰܗܝܺܪܶܐ ܕܰܥܬܝܺܕ݂ܝܺܢ ܕܢܶܗܘܘܽܢ ܒܫܘܽܠܳܡܳܐ ܡܶܢ ܒܳܬܰܪ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܐܘܽܟܳܡܶܐ: ܗܳܢܰܘ ܡܶܠܬܳܐ. ܡܶܢ ܒܳܬܰܪ ܕܐܳܬܝܳܢ ܐܳܬܘ̈ܳܬܳܐ ܕܐܬܐܰܡܰܪ ܠܳܟ݂ ܡܶܢ ܩܕ݂ܺܝܡ: ܟ݁ܰܕ݂ ܢܶܫܬ݁ܰܓܫܘܽܢ ܥܰܡ̈ܡܶܐ ܘܢܺܐܬ݂ܶܐ ܙܰܒ݂ܢܶܗ ܕܰܡܫܺܝܚܝ: ܘܢܶܩܪܶܐ ܠܟ݁ܽܠܗܘܽܢ ܥܰܡ̈ܡܶܐ: ܘܡܶܢܗܘܽܢ ܢܰܚܶܐ ܘܡܶܢܗܘܽܢ ܢܶܩܛܘܽܠ.
LXXII. Hear also about the bright waters, which are to be at the end after these black ones. This is the word: After the signs have come which were told to you before, the nations will be troubled and the time of my Messiah will come and he will summon all the nations and some of them he will make alive and some of them he will kill. These things, therefore, will come upon those nations which are to live by him. Every people which does not know Israel nor has trampled the seed of Jacob, it is they who will live. And this, because they will be subjected
ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܕܶܝܢ ܕܰܢܚܰܝܽܘܢ ܕܢܰܚܘܽܢ ܡܶܢܶܗ. ܟ݁ܽܠ ܥܰܡܳܐ ܕܠܳܐ ܝܺܕ݂ܰܥ ܠܐܺܝܣܪܳܝܺܝܠ: ܘܰܕ݂ܠܳܐ ܕ݁ܳܫ ܠܙܰܪܥܶܗ ܕܝܰܥܩܘܽܒ ܗܘܽܘܝܘܽ ܕܢܺܚܝܶܐ. ܘܗܳܕ݂ܶܐ ܡܶܛܘܽܠ ܕܢܶܫܬܰܥܒܕܘܽܢ ܡܶܢ ܟܘܽܠ
from all the nations to your people. But all those who have dominated you, or have known you, all these will be delivered to the sword.


[p. 1195] {#po.0.p1195}
ܥܰܡ̈ܡܺܝܢ ܠܥܰܡܳܟ. ܟܘܽܠ ܐܰܝܠܺܝܢ ܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܕܐܶܫܬܰܠܰܛܘ ܥܠܰܝܟܘܽܢ ܐܰܘ ܕܝܺܕܰܥܟܘܽܢ: ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܟܘܽܠܗܘܽܢ ܠܚܰܪܒܳܐ ܢܶܫܬܰܠܡܘܽܢ܀ ܘܢܶܗܘܶܐ ܡܶܢ ܒܳܬܰܪ ܕ݁ܰܡܡܰܟ݁ܶܟ ܠܟܘܽܠ ܕܐܝܺܬ ܒܥܳܠܡܳܐ ܘܝܳܬ݂ܶܒ݂ ܒ݁ܰܫܠܳܡܳܐ ܠܥܳܠܰܡ܂ ܥܰܠ ܬ݁ܪܽܘܢܳܘܣ ܕܡܰܠܟ݁ܘܽܬܶܗ: ܗܳܝܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܢܶܬ݂ܓ݁ܠܶܐ
LXXIII. And it will be, after he has humbled all that are in the world, and has sat in peace forever upon the throne of his kingdom, then joy will be revealed and
ܒܚܘܽܕ݂ܳܝܘܽܬܳܐ: ܘܰܢܫܰܝܢܳܐ ܢܶܬܚܙܶܐ. ܘܗܳܝܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܐܳܣܝܘܽܬܳܐ ܬ݁ܶܗܘܶܐ ܢܶܚܬܳܐ ܒܛܰܠ̱ܠܳܐ: ܘܟܘܽܪܗܳܢܶܐ ܢܶܬܪܰܚܰܩ ܘܨܶܦܬ݁ܳܐ ܘܥܺܝܩܬ݁ܳܐ ܘܐܶܢܩܳܬܳܐ ܢܶܥܒܪܳܢ ܡܶܢ ܒܢܰܝ̈ܢܳܫܳܐ ܘܰܢܗܰܠܶܟ ܚܕܘܳܐ ܒ݁ܟܽܠܳܗ ܐܰܪܥܳܐ. ܘܬܘܰܒ ܠܳܐ ܢܡܘܽܬ ܕܠܳܐ ܒܙܰܒܢܶܗ: ܘܠܳܐ ܡܶܢ ܫܶܠܝܳܐ ܢܶܓܕܰܫ ܣܰܩܘܽܒܠܘܽܬܳܐ ܡܶܕܲܡ: ܘܕܝܳܢ̈ܶܐ ܘܡܰܕ݁ܰܘܝܳܬ݂ܳܐ ܘܚܶܪ̈ܝܳܢܶܐ ܘܐܰܒܺܝܕ݂ܳܬܳܐ ܘܰܕ݂ܡܳܐ ܘܰܪ̈ܓܺܝܓ݁ܳܬ݁ܳܐ ܘܰܚܣܳܡܳܐ ܘܣܶܢܝܳܬܳܐ ܘܟ݁ܽܠ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕ݁ܰܠܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܕܳܡܝܳܢ ܠܚܘܽܝܳܒܳܐ ܢܺܐܙܰܠܢ ܟ݁ܰܕ ܢܶܫܬܰܩܠܳܢ. ܗܶܢܶܝܢ ܐܶܢܶܝܢ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܕܰܠܗܳܢܳܐ ܥܳܠܡܳܐ ܡܠܰܝܢܳܝܗ̱ܝ ܒ̈ܺܝܫܳܬܳܐ. ܘܡܶܛܽܠ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܝܰܬܝܺܪܳܐܝܺܬ ܫܓܫ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܥܘܽܡܪܗܘܽܢ ܕܰܒܢܰܝ̈ܢܳܫܳܐ. ܘܢܺܐܬܝܳܢ ܚܰܝ̈ܘܳܬ݁ܳܐ ܡܶܢ ܥܳܒܳܐ ܘܰܢܫܰܡܫܳܢ ܠܰܒܢܰܝ̈ ܐ̱ܢܳܫܳܐ. ܘܐܰܣܦܺܝܣ ܘܰܕ݂ܪ̈ܰܩܳܘܢܶܐ ܢܶܦܩܘܽܢ ܡܶܢ ܢܶܩܥܰܝ̈ܗܘܽܢ ܐܰܝܟ ܕܢܶܫܬܰܥܒܕܘܽܢ ܠܛܰܠܝܳܐ. ܘܢܶܫ̈ܶܐ ܗܳܝܕܶܝܢ ܬܘܒ ܠܳܐ ܢܶܗܘܶܐ ܠܗܶܝܢ ܟ݁ܺܐܒ݂ܳܐ ܡܳܐ ܕܝܳܠ݁ܕ݂ܳܢ ܘܠܳܐ ܢܶܫܬܰܢܳܩܳܢ ܡܳܐ ܕܝܳܗ̈ܒܳܢ ܦܺܐܪܶܐ ܕܡܰܪܒܥܳܐ܀
tranquility will appear and then health will descend in dew and infirmity will recede and anxiety and anguish and groaning will pass away from men and joy will walk through the whole earth. And no one will die prematurely anymore, nor will any adversity happen suddenly. And judgments and accusations and contentions and vengeances and blood and desires and envy and hatred and all things similar to these will go into condemnation, when they have been taken away. For they are the very things that have filled this world with evils and because of these primarily the life of men was troubled. And wild beasts will come from the forest and will serve men; and asps and dragons will come out of their holes to submit themselves to an infant. And women then will no longer have pain when they give birth, nor will they be tormented when they give the fruits of the womb.


[p. 1196] {#po.0.p1196}


[p. 1197] {#po.0.p1197}
ܘܢܶܗܘܶܐ ܒ݁ܗܳܢܘܽܢ ܝܰܘ̈ܡܳܬܳܐ: ܠܳܐ ܢܶܠܐܘܽܢ ܚܳܨܽܘܕ݂̈ܶܐ ܘܠܳܐ ܢܶܥܡܠܘܽܢ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܒܳܢܶܝܢ: ܡܶܢ ܢܦܫܗܶܝܢ ܓܝܪ ܢܶܗܘ̈ܝܳܢ ܪ̈ܳܗܛܳܢܶܐ ܥܒܳܕ݂̈ܶܐ ܥܰܡ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܥܳܒܕܝܺܢ ܠܗܶܝܢ ܒܰܢܝܳܚܳܐ ܣܰܓܺܝܐܳܐ. ܡܶܛܽܠ ܕܙܰܒ݂ܢܳܐ ܗܰܘ ܫܘܽܠܳܡܳܐ ܗ̱ܘ ܕܡܶܕ݁ܶܡ ܕܡܶܬܚܰܒܰܠ ܘܪܺܫܳܝܐ ܕܡܶܕܶܡ ܕܠܳܐ ܡܶܬܚܰܒܰܠ. ܡܶܛܽܠ ܗܳܢܳܐ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܕܰܡܩܰܕ݁̈ܡܳܢ ܐܰܡܚܳܕ݂ܳܢ ܒܶܗ ܗܳܘ̈ܝܳܢ: ܡܶܛܽܠ
LXXIV. And it will be, in those days the reapers will not grow weary nor will those building labor; for works will be running of their own accord with those who do them with much tranquility. Because that time is the end of that which is corrupted and the beginning of that which is not corrupted. Therefore those things which were foretold will be in it; therefore it is far from evils and near to those things which do not die. These are the final bright waters, which came after the final black waters.
ܕܪܰܚܺܝܩ ܗ̱ܘ ܡܶܢ ܒܺܝ̈ܫܳܬܳܐ ܘܩܰܪܺܝܒ ܗ̱ܘ ܠܰܐܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܠܳܐ ܡܳܝܬܝܺܢ. ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܐܶܢܘܽܢ ܡܰܝ̈ܳܐ ܢܰܗܝܺܪ̈ܶܐ ܐ̱ܚܪ̈ܳܝܶܐ ܕܐܶܬܰܘ ܡܶܢ ܒܳܬܰܪ ܡܰܝ̈ܳܐ ܐܘܰܟ݁ܳܡܶܐ ܐ̱ܚܪ̈ܳܝܶܐ܀ ܘܰܥܢܺܝܬ ܘܐܶܡܪܶܬ: ܡܰܢܘܽ ܢܶܬ݁ܕܰܡܶܐ ܡܳܪܝ ܒܛܳܒܘܽܬܳܟ܆ ܠܳܐ ܓ݁ܶܝܪ ܡܶܬ݁ܕ݁ܰܪܟ݁ܳܐ. ܐܰܘ ܡܰܢܘܽ ܢܶܒܨܶܐ ܪ̈ܰܚܡܰܝܟ ܗܳܢܘܽܢ ܕܠܳܐ ܡܶܣܬܰܝܟܝ̈ܢ܆ ܐܰܘ ܡܰܢܘܽ ܢܰܕ݂ܪܶܟ ܣܘܽܟܳܠܳܐ ܕܝܰܠܳܟ܆ ܐܰܘ ܡܰܢܘܽ ܡܨܿܐ ܕܢܶܬܢܳܐ ܡܰܚܫ̈ܒܳܬ݂ܶܗ ܕܡܰܕ݁ܥܳܟ܆ ܐܰܘ ܡܰܢܘܽ ܡܶܢ ܒܢܰܝ̈ܢܳܫܳܐ ܢܣܰܒܰܪ ܕܢܺܐܬܶܐ ܠܗܳܠܶܝܢ: ܐܶܠܳܐ ܐܶܢ ܗܰܘ ܕܰܐܢ̱ܬ ܡܶܬܪܰܚܰܡ ܐܰܢ̱ܬ ܘܰܡܪܰܚܺܦ ܐܰܢ̱ܬ ܥܠܰܘܗ̱ܝ܆ ܡܶܛܽܠ ܕܐܶܢ ܠܳܐ ܡܶܬܪܰܚܰܡ ܐܰܢ̱ܬ ܥܰܠ ܒܢܰܝ̈ܢܳܫܳܐ: ܐܰܝܠܺܝܢ ܕ݁ܰܚܶܝܬ ܝܰܡܺܝܢܳܟ: ܠܳܐ ܡܶܫܟܚܺܝܢ ܕܢܶܡܛܘܽܢ ܠܗܳܠܶܝܢ: ܐܶܠܳܐ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܰܒܡܰܢܝܳܢܶܐ ܡܫܰܡ̈ܗܶܐ ܡܶܫܟܚܺܝܢ ܕܢܶܬܩܪܘܽܢ. ܚܢܰܢ ܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕ݂ܩܳܝܡܝܺܢ ܚ̱ܢܰܢ: ܐܶܢܗܘܽ ܕܢܶܕ݂ܰܥ ܥܰܠ ܡܶܕ݁ܳܡ ܕܐܶܬ݁ܰܝܢ ܘܢܶܫܬܰܥܒܰܕ݂ ܠܗܰܘ ܕܐܰܦܶܩܰܢ ܡܶܢ ܡܶܨܪܶܝܢ: ܘܢܶܐܬܶܐ ܬܽܘܒ ܘܢܶܬܕܰܟ݁ܪܰܢ ܐܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܰܥܒܰܪ: ܘܢܶܚܕܶܐ ܥܰܠ ܡܶܕܶܡ ܕܰܗܘܳܐ. ܐܶܠܳܐ ܐܶܢ ܠܳܐ ܢܶܕ݂ܰܥ ܗܳܫܳܐ ܕܥܰܠ ܡܳܢܳܐ ܐܶܬܰܝܢ ܘܠܳܐ ܢܶܫܬ݁ܰܘܕ݁ܰܥ ܪܺܝܫܳܢܘܳܬ݂ܳܐ ܕܗܰܘ ܕܐܰܣܩܰܢ ܡܶܢ ܡܶܨܪܶܝܢ: ܢܺܐܬܶܝܢܰܢ ܬܘܒ ܘܒܳܥܶܝܢܰܢ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܰܗܘܰܝ̈ ܗܳܫܳܐ ܘܢܶܬܟܰܡܪܰܢ ܥܰܡ ܚܰܫ̈ܶܐ ܥܰܠ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܰܗܘܰܝ̈.
LXXV. And I answered and said: Who will be likened to your goodness, Lord? For it is incomprehensible. Or who will search out your mercies, which are infinite? Or who will comprehend your intelligence? Or who will be able to recount the thoughts of your mind? Or who of those born will hope that he will come to these things, unless it be he whom you pity, and are kind to. Because unless you have pity on men, those who are under your right hand cannot arrive at these things, unless those who [are] in the illustrious numbers can be called. But we who subsist, if we know that for which we came, and have subjected ourselves to him who led us out of Egypt, we will come again and will remember those things which passed, and we will rejoice in that which was. But if we do not know now that for which we came nor recognize the lordship of him who made us ascend from Egypt, we will come again, and will seek those things which now are, and will be saddened with pains about those things which have happened.


[p. 1198] {#po.0.p1198}


[p. 1199] {#po.0.p1199}
ܐܝܠܝܢ ܕܰܓܕܰܫ܀ ܘܥܢܳܐ ܘܐܶܡܰܪ ܠܝܺ: ܡܶܛܘܽܠ ܕܐܶܬܦ݂ܰܫ݁ܰܩ
LXXVI. And he answered and said to me:
ܠܳܟ ܓ݂ܶܠܝܳܢܶܗ ܕ݁ܚܶܙܘܳܐ ܗܳܢܳܐ ܐܰܝܟ݁ ܕܨܰܠܝܺܬ: ܫܡܰܥ ܡܶܠܬ݁ܶܗ ܕܰܡܪܰܝܡܳܐ ܕܬ݂ܶܕ݂ܰܥ ܡܶܕ݁ܶܡ ܕ݁ܰܥܬܝܺܕ݂ ܕܢܶܓܕ݂ܰܫ ܠܳܟ݂ ܡܶܢ ܒܳܬܰܪ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ. ܡܛܠ ܕܡܺܐܙܰܠ ܐܳܙܰܠ ܐܰܢ̱ܬ ܡܶܢ ܗܳܕ݂ܶܐ ܐܰܪܥܳܐ: ܒܪܰܡ ܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܠܳܐ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܠܡܰܘܬܳܐ: ܐܶܠܳܐ ܠܰܢܛܺܝܪܳܐ ܕܙܰܒ݂̈ܢܶܐ. ܣܰܩ ܗܳܟ݂ܶܠ ܠܪܝܺܫܶܗ ܕܛܘܽܪܳܐ ܗܳܢܳܐ ܘܢܶܥܒܪܘܽܢ ܩܕ݂ܳܡܝܟ ܟܽܠܗܘܽܢ ܐܰܬܪ̈ܰܘܳܬܳܐ ܕܙܶܪܥܳܝܐ ܗܳܕܶܐ: ܘܰܕܡܘܽܬܳܗ ܕܬܰܒ݁ܺܝܠ: ܘܪܝܺܫܳܐ ܕܛܘܽܪ̈ܶܐ: ܘܥܘܽܡܩܳܐ ܕܢܰܚܠܶܐ: ܘܥܘܽܡܩܰܘܗ̱ܝ ܕܝܰܡܳܐ: ܘܡܶܢܝܳܢܳܐ ܕܢܰܗܪ̈ܰܘܳܬ݂ܳܐ: ܕܬ݂ܶܚܙܶܐ ܡܳܐ ܫܳܒ݂ܶܩ ܐܰܢ̱ܬ ܘܠܰܐܝܟ݁ܳܐ ܐܳܙܶܠ ܐܰܢ̱ܬ. ܗܳܕ݂ܶܐ ܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܓ݁ܳܕ݂ܫܳܐ ܡܶܢ ܒܳܬܰܪ ܐܰܪܒܥܝܢ
[Because the revelation of this vision was declared to you as you prayed,] hear the word of the Most High so that you may know what is to happen to you after these things. Because departing you will depart from this earth, truly not to death, but to the reservation of times. Ascend therefore to the summit of this mountain and all the regions of this earth will pass in your sight and the figure of the world and the summit of the mountains and the depth of the valleys and the bottom of the sea and the number of the rivers, so that you may see what you leave and where you go. But this will happen after forty days. Now therefore in these days go and teach the people as much as you are able, so that they may learn, lest they die in the final time, but may learn, so that they may live in the final times.
ܝܰܘܡܺܝܢ. ܗܳܫܳܐ ܗܳܟ݂ܶܠ ܒܝܰܘܡܳܬ݂ܳܐ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܙܶܠ ܘܐܰܠܶܦܳܝܗ̱ܝ ܠܥܰܡܳܐ ܟܡܳܐ ܕܡܶܫܟ݁ܰܚ ܐܰܢ̱ܬ: ܕܢܺܐܠܦܘܽܢ ܕܠܳܐ ܢܡܘܽܬܘܽܢ ܒܙܰܒ݂ܢܳܐ ܐ̱ܚܪܳܝܳܐ: ܐܶܠܳܐ ܢܺܐܠܦܘܽܢ ܕܢܺܐܚܘܽܢ ܒܙܰܒ݂ܢܶܐ ܐ̱ܚܪ̈ܳܝܶܐ܀ ܘܐܶܙܶܠ݈ܬ ܐܶܢܳܐ ܒ݁ܰܪܘܟ ܡܶܢ ܬܰܡܳܢ ܘܐܶܬܝܺܬ ܨܶܝܕ݂ ܥܰܡܳܐ ܘܟܰܢܫܶܬ ܐܶܢܘܽܢ ܡܶܢ ܪܰܒ݁ܳܐ ܘܰܥܕܰܡܳܐ ܠܰܙܥܘܽܪܳܐ ܘܐܶܡܰܪܬ ܠܗܘܽܢ: ܫܡܰܥܘ: ܒܢܰܝ̈ ܐܝܺܣܪܳܝܶܠ: ܚܙܰܘ ܕ݁ܰܟ݂ܡܳܐ ܐܶܫܬܚܰܪܬܘܽܢ ܡܶܢ ܬܪܶܥܣܰܪ̈ ܫܰܒ݁̈ܛܶܐ ܕܐܝܺܣܪܳܝܺܠ. ܠܟܘܽܢ ܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܘܠܰܐܒܳܗܰܝܟܘܽܢ ܡܳܪܝܳܐ ܝܰܗ̱ܒ ܢܳܡܘܽܣܳܐ ܝܰܬܝܺܪ ܡܶܢ ܟܘܽܠܗܘܽܢ ܥܰܡ̱ܡܶܐ. ܘܡܶܛܽܠ ܕ݂ܰܥܒ݁ܰܪܘ ܐܰܚܰܝ̈ܟܘܽܢ ܦܘܽܩܕܳܢܰܘ̈ܗ̱ܝ ܕܰܡܪܰܝܡܳܝܐ: ܐܰܝܬܝܺ ܥܠܰܝܟܘܽܢ ܘܰܥܠܰܝܗܘܽܢ ܬܒܰܥܬܳܐ: ܘܠܳܐ ܚܳܣ ܥܰܠ ܩܰܕ̈ܡܳܝܶܐ: ܐܶܠܳܐ ܐܳܦ ܠܰܐ݈ܚܪ̈ܳܢܶܐ ܝܰܗ̱ܒ ܠܫܶܒܝܳܐ ܘܠܳܐ ܫܒܰܩ ܡܶܢܗܘܽܢ ܫܰܪܟܳܢܳܐ. ܘܗܳܐ ܐܰܢ̱ܬܘܽܢ ܐܶܫܬܰܚܰܪܬܘܽܢ ܗܳܟ݂ܳܐ ܥܰܡܰܢ. ܐܶܢ ܗܳܟ݂ܶܠ ܬܬܰܪܨܘܽܢ ܐܘܽܪ̈ܚܳܬܟܘܽܢ: ܠܳܐ ܐܳܙܠܝܺܬܘܽܢ ܐܳܦ ܐܰܢ݈ܬܘܽܢ ܐܰܝܟ݁ ܕܐܶܙܰܠܘ ܐܰܚܰܝ̈ܟܘܽܢ: ܐܶܠܳܐ ܗܶܢܘܽܢ ܢܺܐܬܘܽܢ ܠܘܳܬܟܘܽܢ. ܡܶܛܘܽܠ ܕܰܡܪܰܚܡܳܢܳܐ ܗ̱ܘ ܗܰܘ ܕܦܳܠܚܝܺܢ ܐܰܢ̱ܬܘܽܢ ܠܶܗ ܘܰܡܪܰܚܡܳܢܳܐ ܗ̱ܘ ܗܰܘ ܕܰܡܣܰܒܪܝܺܢ ܐܰܢ̱ܬܘܽܢ ܒܶܗ ܘܫܰܪܝܺܪܳܐ ܗ̱ܘ ܕ݁ܢܰܛܶܐܒ݂ ܘܠܳܐ ܕܢܰܒܶܐܫ. ܠܳܐ ܗܳܐ ܐܰܢ̱ܬܘܽܢ ܚܙܰܝܬܘܽܢ ܡܶܕܶܡ ܕ݁ܓ݂ܰܕ݂ܫܳܗ ܠܨܶܗܝܽܘܢ& ܐܰܘ ܕ݁ܰܠܡܳܐ ܣܳܒܪܝܺܬܘܽܢ ܕܐܰܬܪܳܐ ܚܛܳܐ ܘܡܶܛܽܠ ܗܳܢܳܐ ܐܶܣܬܚܝܦ܆& ܐܰܘ ܐܰܪܥܳܐ ܡܶܕ݂ܶܡ ܐܰܣܟܠܰܬ ܘܡܶܛܽܠ ܗܳܢܳܐ ܐܶܫܬܰܠܡܰܬ܆ ܘܠܳܐ ܝܳܕ݂ܥܝܺܢ ܐܢܬܘܢ ܕܡܶܛܘܽܠܳܬܟܘܽܢ ܕܰܚܛܰܝܬܘܽܢ ܐܶܣܬ݁ܚܺܝܦ ܗܰܘ ܐܰܝܢܳܐ ܕܠܳܐ ܚܛܳܐ ܘܡܶܛܽܠ ܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܐܰܥܘܶܠܘ ܐܶܫܬܠܶܡ ܠܰܒܥܶܠܕ݁̈ܒܳܒܶܐ ܗܰܘ. ܐܰܝܢܳܐ ܕܠܳܐ ܐܰܣܟܶܠ܆ ܘܰܥܢܳܐ ܟܽܠܶܗ ܥܰܡܳܐ ܘܐܶܡܰܪܘ ܠܝܺ: ܟܘܽܠ ܕܰܡܨܶܝܢܰܢ ܕܢܶܗܘܶܐ ܥܳܗܕܝܺܢܰܢ ܛܳܒ݂̈ܳܬ݁ܳܐ ܕ݁ܰܥܒܰܕ݂ ܥܰܡܰܢ ܚܰܝܠܬ݁ܳܢܳܐ: ܥܳܗܕܝܺܢ ܚܢܰܢ: ܘܐܰܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܠܳܐ ܥܳܗܕܝܺܢ ܚܢܰܢ: ܗܘ ܒܪ̈ܰܚܡܰܘܗ̱ܝ ܝܳܕ݂ܰܥ. ܒܪܰܡ ܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܗܳܕ݂ܶܐ ܥܒܶܕ݂ ܠܰܢ ܠܥܰܡܳܟ: ܟܬܘܽܒ ܐܳܦ ܠܰܐܚܰܝ̈ܢ ܠܒܳܒܶܠ ܐܶܓ݁ܰܪܬ݁ܳܐ ܕܚܘܽܠܦܳܢܳܝܬܳܐ ܘܟܰܪܟܳܝܬܳܐ ܕܰܣܒܰܪܬܳܐ: ܕܐܳܦ݂ ܠܗܘܽܢ ܬܚܰܝܶܠ ܡܶܢ ܩܕ݂ܳܡ ܕܠܳܐ ܬ݁ܺܐܙܰܠ ܡܶܢܰܢ. ܪܳܥܰܝܘܳܬܶܗ ܕܐܝܺܣܪܳܝܺܠ: ܘܰܕ݂ܥܶܟ݂ܘ ܫܪܳܓ݂̈ܶܐ
LXXVII. And I, Baruch, went from there and came to the people and gathered them from the greatest to the least, and I said to them: Hear, children of Israel, see how many you have remained from the twelve tribes of Israel. But to you and your fathers the Lord gave the law above all peoples; and because your brothers transgressed the precepts of the Most High, he brought vengeance upon you and upon them, nor did he spare the former, but also gave the latter into captivity, nor did he leave a remnant of them. And behold you are here, my people. If therefore you direct your ways, you will not go even you, as your brothers went, but they will come to you. Because he whom you worship is merciful, and he in whom you hope is kind and is truthful, so that he may do good and not so that he may bring evils. Do you not behold that which happened to Zion? Do you perhaps think that the place sinned and therefore was overturned, or that the earth committed some fault and therefore was delivered? Nor do you know that because of you, who sinned, that which did not sin was overturned, and because of those who acted wickedly that which did not commit a fault was delivered to enemies? And the entire people answered and said to me: As much as we can remember the good things which the Almighty did with us, we remember, and those which we do not remember, he knows by his mercy. Nevertheless, do this for us, your people: write also to our brothers in Babylon a letter of doctrine and a scroll of announcement, so that you may confirm them also, before you depart from us. For the shepherds of Israel have failed and the lamps which were illuminating have been extinguished and the fountains have held back their flow, from which we were drinking. But we have been left in


[p. 1200] {#po.0.p1200}


[p. 1201] {#po.0.p1201}


[p. 1202] {#po.0.p1202}


[p. 1203] {#po.0.p1203}


[p. 1204] {#po.0.p1204}
ܪܗܛܗܘܽܢ ܡܶܢ ܐܰܝܟ݁ܳܐ ܕܫܳܬ݁ܶܝܢ ܗ̱ܘܰܝܢ. ܚܢܰܢ ܕܶܝܢ ܐܶܫܬܒܶܝܢ ܒܚܺܝܫܘܽܟܳܐ ܘܰܒܗܢܺܝܠܳܐ ܕܥܳܒܳܝܐ ܘܰܒܨܰܗܝܳܐ ܕܡܰܕܒܪܳܐ. ܘܰܥܢܺܝܬ ܘܐܶܡܪܶܬ ܠܗܘܽܢ: ܪܳܥܘܳܬܳܐ ܘܰܒܪܳܓ݂ܶܐ ܘܡܰܒ̈ܘܽܥܶܐ ܡܶܢ ܢܳܡܘܽܣܳܐ ܐܺܝܬܰܝܗܘܽܢ ܗ̱ܘܰܘ. ܘܐܶܢ ܚܢܰܢ ܐܳܙܰܠ̱ܝܢ ܚ̱ܢܰܢ: ܐܶܠܳܐ ܢܶܡܘܽܣܳܐ ܩܰܝܳܡ ܗ̱ܘ. ܐܶܢ ܗܳܟܝܶܠ ܬܚܘܽܪܘܽܢ ܒܢܳܡܘܽܣܳܐ ܘܬܶܗܘܘܽܢ ܙܗܝܺܪܝܺܢ ܒܚܶܟܡܬܳܐ: ܠܳܐ ܚܳܣܰܪ ܫܪܳܓ݁ܳܐ ܘܪܳܥܝܳܐ ܠܳܐ ܥܳܢܶܕ݂ ܘܡܰܒܘܽܥܳܐ ܠܳܐ ܚܳܒܶܫ. ܒܪܰܡ ܕܶܝܢ ܐܰܝܟ ܕܐܶܡܰܪܬܘܽܢ ܠܝ: ܐܶܢܳܐ ܟܳܬܶܒ ܐ̱ܢܳܐ ܐܳܦ ܠܰܐܚܰ̈ܝܟܘܽܢ ܕܰܒܒܳܒ݂ܶܠ ܘܐܶܫܰܕ݁ܰܪ ܒܝܰܕ݂ ܒܢܰܝ̈ ܐ̱ܢܳܫܳܐ: ܘܰܠܬܶܫܥܳܐ ܫܰܒ݁ܛܺܝܢ ܘܦ݁ܶܠܓ݁ܶܗ ܗܳܟܰܢܳܝܐ ܐܶܟܬܘܽܒ ܘܐܶܫܰܕ݁ܰܪ ܒܚܰܕ݂ ܟܳܪܰܚܬܳܐ. ܘܰܗܘܳܐ ܒܚܰܕ݂ ܘܥܶܣܪܝܺܢ ܒܚܰܪܚܳܝܐ ܕ݁ܰܐܡܳܢܝܳܐ ܐܶܬܝܺܬ ܐܶܢܳܐ ܒܰܪܘܽܟ ܘܢܶܬܒܶܬ ܬܚܶܝܬ ܒܰܠܘܽܛܳܐ ܒܛܶܠܳܠܳܐ ܕܣܰܘ̈ܟܶܐ ܘܐ݈ܢܳܫ ܠܳܐ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܥܰܡܝ: ܐܶܠܳܐ ܐܶܢܳܐ ܒܰܠܚܘܽܕܰܝ. ܘܟܶܬܒܶܬ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ ܬܰܪܬܶܝܢ ܐܶܓܪ̈ܳܢ: ܚܕ݂ܳܐ ܫܰܕ݂ܪܶܬ ܒܚܰܕ݂ ܢܶܫܪܳܐ ܠܬܶܫܥܳܐ ܫܰܒ݁ܛܺܝܢ ܘܦܶܠܓܶܗ: ܘܰܐ݈ܚܪܳܬܳܐ ܫܰܕ݂ܪܶܬ ܠܰܐܝܠܶܝܢ ܕܰܒܒܳܒܶܠ: ܒܝܰܕ݂ ܬܠܳܬ݁ܳܐ ܐ̱ܢܳܫ̈ܫܳܐ. ܘܰܩܪܝܺܬ ܠܢܶܫܪܳܐ ܘܐܶܡܪܶܬ ܠܶܗ ܡ̈ܺܝܠܶܐ ܗܳܠܶܝܢ: ܠܳܟ ܥܒܰܕ݂ ܡܪܰܚܡܳܢܳܐ ܕܬܶܗܘܶܐ ܡܥܰܠܰܝ ܡܶܢ ܟ݁ܽܠܳܗ ܦܳܪܰܚܬܳܐ. ܘܗܳܫܳܐ ܙܶܠ ܘܠܳܐ ܬ݁ܶܪܦܶܐ ܒܐܰܬ݂ܪܳܐ: ܘܠܳܐ ܬܶܥܘܽܠ ܠܩܶܢ̈ܳܝܐ: ܘܠܳܐ ܬܩܘܳܡ ܥܰܠ ܟܘܽܠ ܐܝܺܠܳܢ: ܥܕ݂ܰܡܳܐ ܕܬܶܥܒܰܪ ܦܬܰܝܗܘܽܢ ܕܡܰܝ̈ܳܐ ܣܰܓܝ̈ܺܐܶܐ ܕܢܰܗܪܳܐ ܦܪܳܬ ܘܬܺܐܙܰܠ ܠܘܳܬ ܥܰܡܳܐ ܗܰܘ ܕܥܳܡܰܪ ܬܰܡܳܢ. ܘܰܫܕܝܺ ܠܗܘܢ ܐܰܓ݁ܰܪܬ݁ܳܐ ܗܳܕ݂ܶܐ. ܐܶܬ݁ܕ݁ܰܟܪ ܕ݁ܶܝܢ ܕܰܒ݂ܙܰܒܢܳܐ ܕܛܰܘܦܳܢܳܐ ܡܶܢ ܝܰܘܢܳܐ ܩܰܒܶܠ ܢܘܽܚ ܦܺܐܪܳܐ ܕܙܰܝܬ݁ܳܐ: ܟ݁ܰܕ ܫܰܕܪܳܗ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܡܶܢ ܩܺܒܘܽܬܳܐ. ܐܶܠܳܐ ܐܳܦ ܥܘܽܪ̈ܒ݁ܳܐ ܫܰܡܰܫܘ ܠܶܐܠܺܝܳܐ ܟ݁ܰܕ݂ ܡܰܝܬ݁ܶܝܢ ܗ̱ܘܰܘ ܠܶܗ ܬܘܽܪܣܳܝܐ ܐܰܝܟ ܕܡܶܬܦܰܩܰܕ݂ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܠܗܘܽܢ. ܐܳܦ ܫܠܶܝܡܘܽܢ ܒܙܰܒ݂ܢܳܐ ܕܡܰܠܟ݁ܘܽܬܶܗ ܠܟܘّܠ ܐܰܬ݂ܪܳܐ ܕܨܳܒ݂ܶܐ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܕܰܢܫܰܕ݁ܰܪ ܐܰܘ ܕܢܶܒܥܶܐ ܡܶܕ݁ܶܡ: ܠܦܳܪܰܚܬ݁ܳܐ ܦܳܩܶܕ݂ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܘܡܶܫܬܰܡܥܳܐ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܠܶܗ: ܐܰܝܟ ܕܦܳܩܶܕ݂ ܗ̱ܘܳܐ ܠܳܗ. ܘܗܳܫܳܐ ܠܳܐ ܬܶܡܪܰܢ ܠܳܟ݂ ܘܠܳܐ ܬ݁ܰܪܟ݁ܶܢ ܠܝܰܡܝܺܢܳܐ ܐܰܘ ܠܣܶܡܳܠܳܐ: ܐܶܠܳܐ ܦ݁ܪܰܚ ܘܙܶܠ ܒܐܘܽܪܚܳܐ ܬ݁ܪܺܝܨܬ݁ܳܐ: ܕܬܶܛܰܪ ܦܘܽܩܕ݂ܳܢܳܗ ܕܚܰܝܠܬ݁ܳܢܳܐ: ܐܰܝܟ݂ ܕܐܶܡܪܶܬ ܠܳܟ܀
darkness and in the density of the forest and in the thirst of the desert. And I answered and said to them: The shepherds and the lamps and the fountains were from the law; and if we depart, nevertheless the law stands. If therefore you look into the law and are prudent in wisdom, the lamp will not fail and the shepherd will not recede and the fountain will not dry up. Nevertheless, as you said to me, I will write also to your brothers who are in Babylon, and I will send by men; and to the nine tribes and a half I will similarly write and send by a bird. And it happened on the twenty-first [day] of the eighth month, I, Baruch, came and sat under the oak in the shade of the branches and no man was with me, but I alone. And I wrote these two letters; one I sent by an eagle to the nine tribes and a half, and the other I sent to those who were in Babylon, by the help of three men. And I called the eagle and said to it these words: The Most High made you, so that you might be higher than all birds. And now go and do not linger anywhere, nor enter a nest, nor stand upon any tree, until you have crossed the breadth of the many waters of the river Euphrates and you will go to that people which dwells there. And throw this letter to them. But remember at the time of the flood Noah received the olive fruit from the dove, when he had sent it out from the ark. But also ravens served Elijah, bringing him food, as it was commanded to them. Also Solomon at the time of his kingdom, wherever he wished to send, or to seek something, he would command a bird; and it would obey him, as he commanded it. And now do not be weary, nor decline to the right or to the left, but fly and go by the straight way, so that you may keep the command of the Almighty, as I said to you.
